FILE: scripts\traceSelectorMapContainers.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGETS = new Set([5220, 5232, 5235]);
const DATA_START = 0x324ef9;
const DATA_END   = 0x4db7bd;

function u32(p) {
  return (
    b[p] |
    (b[p + 1] << 8) |
    (b[p + 2] << 16) |
    (b[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

console.log("=".repeat(64));
console.log("SELECTOR MAP CONTAINERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("=".repeat(64));

for (let p = DATA_START; p + 4 <= DATA_END; p += 4) {
  const v = u32(p);

  if (!TARGETS.has(v)) continue;

  console.log(
    `\nSELECTOR ${v} @ FILE 0x${p.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
  );

  const start = Math.max(DATA_START, p - 32);
  const end = Math.min(DATA_END, p + 36);

  for (let q = start; q < end; q += 4) {
    const x = u32(q);

    console.log(
      `  FILE 0x${q.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")} ` +
      `VALUE ${x} 0x${x.toString(16).padStart(8,"0")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("\n" + "=".repeat(64));
console.log("DONE");
console.log("=".repeat(64));

FILE: scripts\traceSelectorMappingRefs.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [
    0x19534c,
    0x195350,
    0x195354,
    0x195358,
    0x195490,
    0x195494,
    0x1954a0,
    0x1954a4,
    0x1954b0,
    0x1954b4
];

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readI32(pos: number) {
    return (
        wasm[pos] |
        (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR / MAPPING REFERENCES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("TARGET ADDRESSES:");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    console.log(
        hex(t),
        "=",
        readI32(t)
    );
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let p = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0)
    throw new Error("CODE section not found");

let q = codeStart;

const fc = uleb(q);
q = fc.next;

console.log("DEFINED FUNCTIONS =", fc.value);
console.log("");

let totalHits = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < fc.value; ordinal++) {

    const bs = uleb(q);
    q = bs.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bs.value;

    /*
     * Scan raw bytes for little-endian target addresses.
     *
     * This catches:
     *   i32.const target
     *
     * and raw address literals embedded in instructions.
     */
    for (let at = bodyStart; at + 4 <= bodyEnd; at++) {

        const value = readI32(at);

        if (!TARGETS.includes(value))
            continue;

        console.log(
            `FUNC ORDINAL ${ordinal} @ ${hex(at)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  RAW I32 = ${hex(value)}`
        );

        /*
         * Print surrounding bytes.
         */
        const from = Math.max(
            bodyStart,
            at - 8
        );

        const to = Math.min(
            bodyEnd,
            at + 12
        );

        const bytes: string[] = [];

        for (let x = from; x < to; x++) {
            bytes.push(
                wasm[x].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            );
        }

        console.log(
            "  BYTES =",
            bytes.join(" ")
        );

        totalHits++;
        console.log("");
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("RAW ADDRESS / VALUE HITS =", totalHits);
console.log("");

console.log("TARGET VALUES:");
console.log("  0x19534c = 4788");
console.log("  0x195350 = 4789");
console.log("  0x195354 = 4790");
console.log("  0x195358 = 4791");
console.log("");
console.log("  0x195494 = 4788");
console.log("  0x1954a4 = 4790");
console.log("  0x1954b4 = 4789");
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorMemoryInit.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_START = 0x0019534c;
const TARGET_END = 0x0019535b;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    if (pos >= bytes.length) {
      throw new Error("ULEB out of bounds");
    }

    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too large");
    }
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    if (pos >= bytes.length) {
      throw new Error("SLEB out of bounds");
    }

    b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too large");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= ~0 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function readBytes(pos: number, count: number) {
  return bytes.subarray(pos, pos + count);
}

function overlaps(
  start: number,
  size: number,
  targetStart = TARGET_START,
  targetEnd = TARGET_END
) {
  if (size <= 0) return false;

  const end = start + size - 1;

  return start <= targetEnd && end >= targetStart;
}

function hexBytes(data: Uint8Array, max = 32) {
  const n = Math.min(data.length, max);

  const out: string[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    out.push(data[i].toString(16).padStart(2, "0"));
  }

  return out.join(" ");
}

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR MEMORY INIT");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET MEMORY RANGE");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`START : ${hex(TARGET_START)}`);
console.log(`END   : ${hex(TARGET_END)}`);
console.log(`SIZE  : ${TARGET_END - TARGET_START + 1} bytes`);
console.log("============================================================");

if (
  bytes[0] !== 0x00 ||
  bytes[1] !== 0x61 ||
  bytes[2] !== 0x73 ||
  bytes[3] !== 0x6d
) {
  throw new Error("Not a WASM binary");
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * PASS 1
 * Parse Data Section.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

interface DataSegment {
  index: number;
  mode: "active" | "passive";
  memoryIndex: number | null;
  offset: number | null;
  dataStart: number;
  dataSize: number;
  dataEnd: number;
}

const dataSegments: DataSegment[] = [];

let sectionPos = 8;

while (sectionPos < bytes.length) {
  const sectionId = bytes[sectionPos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(sectionPos);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;

  sectionPos = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = sectionPos;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionEnd > bytes.length) {
    throw new Error(
      `Section extends beyond file: ${hex(sectionStart)}`
    );
  }

  /*
   * Data section = 11
   */
  if (sectionId === 11) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(sectionPos);
    const count = countInfo.value;

    let p = countInfo.next;

    console.log("");
    console.log("DATA SECTION");
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`DATA SEGMENT COUNT = ${count}`);

    for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {
      const flagsInfo = readULEB(p);
      const flags = flagsInfo.value;

      p = flagsInfo.next;

      let mode: "active" | "passive";
      let memoryIndex: number | null = null;
      let offset: number | null = null;

      if (flags === 0) {
        /*
         * Active segment, implicit memory 0.
         * offset expression follows.
         */
        mode = "active";
        memoryIndex = 0;

        /*
         * Expected form:
         *
         *   i32.const <offset>
         *   end
         */
        const op = bytes[p++];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected data offset opcode at ${hex(p - 1)}: ${hex(
              op,
              2
            )}`
          );
        }

        const off = readSLEB(p);
        offset = off.value >>> 0;
        p = off.next;

        if (bytes[p++] !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error(
            `Missing end opcode for data segment ${index}`
          );
        }
      } else if (flags === 1) {
        /*
         * Passive data segment.
         */
        mode = "passive";
      } else if (flags === 2) {
        /*
         * Active segment with explicit memory index.
         */
        mode = "active";

        const mem = readULEB(p);
        memoryIndex = mem.value;
        p = mem.next;

        const op = bytes[p++];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
          throw new Error(
            `Unexpected explicit data offset opcode at ${hex(
              p - 1
            )}: ${hex(op, 2)}`
          );
        }

        const off = readSLEB(p);
        offset = off.value >>> 0;
        p = off.next;

        if (bytes[p++] !== 0x0b) {
          throw new Error(
            `Missing end opcode for data segment ${index}`
          );
        }
      } else {
        throw new Error(
          `Unknown data segment flags ${flags} at ${hex(p)}`
        );
      }

      const sizeInfo2 = readULEB(p);
      const dataSize = sizeInfo2.value;
      p = sizeInfo2.next;

      const dataStart = p;
      const dataEnd = dataStart + dataSize;

      const seg: DataSegment = {
        index,
        mode,
        memoryIndex,
        offset,
        dataStart,
        dataSize,
        dataEnd,
      };

      dataSegments.push(seg);

      if (
        mode === "active" &&
        offset !== null &&
        overlaps(offset, dataSize)
      ) {
        console.log("");
        console.log(
          `*** TARGET-OVERLAPPING ACTIVE DATA SEGMENT ***`
        );
        console.log(`SEGMENT       = ${index}`);
        console.log(`MODE          = ACTIVE`);
        console.log(`MEMORY INDEX  = ${memoryIndex}`);
        console.log(`OFFSET        = ${hex(offset)}`);
        console.log(`SIZE          = ${dataSize}`);
        console.log(`END           = ${hex(offset + dataSize - 1)}`);
        console.log(
          `DATA FILE POS  = ${hex(dataStart)} .. ${hex(dataEnd - 1)}`
        );

        const shown = readBytes(dataStart, dataSize);

        console.log(`DATA          = ${hexBytes(shown, 64)}`);

        const targetOffset = Math.max(
          TARGET_START - offset,
          0
        );

        const targetEndOffset = Math.min(
          TARGET_END - offset,
          dataSize - 1
        );

        if (
          targetOffset <= targetEndOffset &&
          targetOffset >= 0
        ) {
          const targetBytes = shown.subarray(
            targetOffset,
            targetEndOffset + 1
          );

          console.log(
            `TARGET BYTES  = ${hexBytes(targetBytes, 64)}`
          );
        }
      }

      p = dataEnd;
    }

    console.log("");
    console.log("DATA SEGMENT SCAN COMPLETE");
  }

  sectionPos = sectionEnd;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * PASS 2
 * Scan code for:
 *
 *   memory.init
 *   memory.copy
 *   memory.fill
 *   data.drop
 *
 * Bulk memory instructions are encoded under 0xfc.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("BULK MEMORY INSTRUCTION SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

let memoryInitCount = 0;
let memoryCopyCount = 0;
let memoryFillCount = 0;
let dataDropCount = 0;

let bulkHits = 0;

interface BodyInfo {
  ordinal: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  instrStart: number;
}

const bodies: BodyInfo[] = [];

sectionPos = 8;

while (sectionPos < bytes.length) {
  const sectionId = bytes[sectionPos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(sectionPos);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;

  sectionPos = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = sectionPos;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(sectionPos);
    const count = countInfo.value;

    let p = countInfo.next;

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count; ordinal++) {
      const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
      const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;

      p = bodySizeInfo.next;

      const bodyStart = p;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

      const localDeclInfo = readULEB(p);
      const localDeclCount = localDeclInfo.value;

      p = localDeclInfo.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(p);
        p = n.next;

        /*
         * local type
         */
        p += 1;
      }

      bodies.push({
        ordinal,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        instrStart: p,
      });

      p = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  sectionPos = sectionEnd;
}

/*
 * We need a lightweight constant stack only for bulk-memory
 * destination/length arguments.
 *
 * memory.fill:
 *     dst, value, len
 *
 * memory.copy:
 *     dst, src, len
 *
 * memory.init:
 *     dst, src, len
 *
 * For target detection, the destination is the important value.
 */

function scanBody(body: BodyInfo) {
  let p = body.instrStart;

  /*
   * Stack entries are:
   *   number  -> exact constant
   *   null    -> unknown
   */
  const stack: (number | null)[] = [];

  function pop() {
    if (stack.length === 0) return null;
    return stack.pop()!;
  }

  function push(v: number | null) {
    stack.push(v);
  }

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const start = p;
    const op = bytes[p++];

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const x = readSLEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      push(x.value >>> 0);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      push(null);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      pop();
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      const v = stack.length
        ? stack[stack.length - 1]
        : null;

      stack.push(v);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;
      push(null);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      pop();
      push(null);
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x36 || op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b) {
      const a = readULEB(p);
      p = a.next;

      const o = readULEB(p);
      p = o.next;

      /*
       * address + value
       */
      pop();
      pop();
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * i32.add / i32.sub
     */
    if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {
      const b = pop();
      const a = pop();

      if (a !== null && b !== null) {
        push(
          op === 0x6a
            ? (a + b) >>> 0
            : (a - b) >>> 0
        );
      } else {
        push(null);
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call
     *
     * We cannot safely infer its result or parameter count here,
     * so invalidate the stack.
     */
    if (op === 0x10) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * call_indirect
     */
    if (op === 0x11) {
      const x = readULEB(p);
      p = x.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Bulk memory prefix.
     */
    if (op === 0xfc) {
      const sub = readULEB(p);
      p = sub.next;

      /*
       * memory.init = 0x08
       */
      if (sub.value === 0x08) {
        const seg = readULEB(p);
        p = seg.next;

        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        memoryInitCount++;

        const len = pop();
        const src = pop();
        const dst = pop();

        if (dst !== null && len !== null) {
          if (overlaps(dst, len)) {
            bulkHits++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
              `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} @ ${hex(start)}`
            );
            console.log("  OP           = memory.init");
            console.log(`  DATA SEGMENT = ${seg.value}`);
            console.log(`  MEMORY       = ${mem.value}`);
            console.log(`  DST          = ${hex(dst)}`);
            console.log(`  SRC           = ${src === null ? "UNKNOWN" : hex(src)}`);
            console.log(`  LENGTH       = ${len}`);
            console.log(
              `  RANGE        = ${hex(dst)} .. ${hex(
                dst + Math.max(len - 1, 0)
              )}`
            );
            console.log(
              "  *** TARGET RANGE OVERLAP ***"
            );
          }
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * data.drop = 0x09
       */
      if (sub.value === 0x09) {
        const seg = readULEB(p);
        p = seg.next;

        dataDropCount++;

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * memory.copy = 0x0a
       */
      if (sub.value === 0x0a) {
        const dst = pop();
        const src = pop();
        const len = pop();

        const memDst = readULEB(p);
        p = memDst.next;

        const memSrc = readULEB(p);
        p = memSrc.next;

        memoryCopyCount++;

        if (dst !== null && len !== null) {
          if (overlaps(dst, len)) {
            bulkHits++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
              `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} @ ${hex(start)}`
            );
            console.log("  OP           = memory.copy");
            console.log(`  DST          = ${hex(dst)}`);
            console.log(
              `  SRC          = ${
                src === null ? "UNKNOWN" : hex(src)
              }`
            );
            console.log(`  LENGTH       = ${len}`);
            console.log(
              `  RANGE        = ${hex(dst)} .. ${hex(
                dst + Math.max(len - 1, 0)
              )}`
            );
            console.log(
              "  *** TARGET RANGE OVERLAP ***"
            );
          }
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * memory.fill = 0x0b
       */
      if (sub.value === 0x0b) {
        const dst = pop();
        const value = pop();
        const len = pop();

        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        memoryFillCount++;

        if (dst !== null && len !== null) {
          if (overlaps(dst, len)) {
            bulkHits++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
              `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} @ ${hex(start)}`
            );
            console.log("  OP           = memory.fill");
            console.log(`  MEMORY       = ${mem.value}`);
            console.log(`  DST          = ${hex(dst)}`);
            console.log(
              `  VALUE        = ${
                value === null ? "UNKNOWN" : hex(value)
              }`
            );
            console.log(`  LENGTH       = ${len}`);
            console.log(
              `  RANGE        = ${hex(dst)} .. ${hex(
                dst + Math.max(len - 1, 0)
              )}`
            );
            console.log(
              "  *** TARGET RANGE OVERLAP ***"
            );
          }
        }

        continue;
      }

      /*
       * Unknown 0xfc instruction.
       * We cannot safely parse its immediates.
       */
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * End / branch / control flow:
     * invalidate our tiny local stack model.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04 ||
      op === 0x05 ||
      op === 0x0b ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x0e ||
      op === 0x0f
    ) {
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Most remaining instructions are not relevant to our
     * constant destination analysis. To avoid false positives,
     * invalidate the stack when an unknown producer appears.
     */
    stack.length = 0;
  }
}

for (const body of bodies) {
  try {
    scanBody(body);
  } catch {
    /*
     * Read-only scanner: skip malformed/unsupported body
     * rather than inventing a result.
     */
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`DATA SEGMENTS       = ${dataSegments.length}`);
console.log(`memory.init COUNT   = ${memoryInitCount}`);
console.log(`memory.copy COUNT   = ${memoryCopyCount}`);
console.log(`memory.fill COUNT   = ${memoryFillCount}`);
console.log(`data.drop COUNT     = ${dataDropCount}`);
console.log(`TARGET BULK HITS    = ${bulkHits}`);
console.log("");

const activeTargets = dataSegments.filter(
  (s) =>
    s.mode === "active" &&
    s.offset !== null &&
    overlaps(s.offset, s.dataSize)
);

console.log(
  `ACTIVE DATA TARGET OVERLAPS = ${activeTargets.length}`
);

for (const s of activeTargets) {
  console.log(
    `  SEGMENT ${s.index}: ${hex(s.offset!)} + ${s.dataSize}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorProducer.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const START = 0x001226d;
const END   = 0x001295b;
const TARGET = 0x0019534c;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FUNC 122 - SELECTOR PRODUCER TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`BODY   : 0x${START.toString(16)} .. 0x${END.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`TARGET : 0x${TARGET.toString(16)}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (let p = START; p < END; p++) {
  const b = wasm[p];

  // i32.const opcode
  if (b === 0x41) {
    let v = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let q = p + 1;

    while (q < END) {
      const x = wasm[q++];
      v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;

      if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;
      shift += 7;
      if (shift > 35) break;
    }

    const u = v >>> 0;

    if (u === TARGET) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("############################################################");
      console.log("TARGET PRODUCER FOUND");
      console.log(`OFFSET : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
      console.log(`VALUE  : 0x${u.toString(16)}`);
      console.log("############################################################");
    }
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorProducerExact.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [
  { f: 6611, call: 0x22c948, local: 6 },
  { f: 6611, call: 0x22c9ef, local: 2 },
  { f: 6613, call: 0x22cb34, local: 2 },
  { f: 6615, call: 0x22cc55, local: 1 },
  { f: 6615, call: 0x22cc5e, local: 1 },
  { f: 6618, call: 0x22d083, local: 1 },
  { f: 6464, call: 0x22838e, local: 11 },
];

function uleb(p: number) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, n = 0;
  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    v |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
    n++;
    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }
  return { v: v >>> 0, n };
}

function instrLen(p: number): number {
  const op = wasm[p];

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22 ||
      op === 0x23 || op === 0x24 ||
      op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d ||
      op === 0x10 || op === 0x41)
    return 1 + uleb(p + 1).n;

  if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
    const a = uleb(p + 1);
    const b = uleb(p + 1 + a.n);
    return 1 + a.n + b.n;
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const a = uleb(p + 1);
    return 1 + a.n + 1;
  }

  if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04)
    return 2;

  return 1;
}

function findCode() {
  for (let i = 0; i < wasm.length - 10; i++) {
    if (wasm[i] !== 0x0a) continue;

    const s = uleb(i + 1);
    const start = i + 1 + s.n;

    const c = uleb(start);

    if (c.v !== 9398) continue;

    let p = start + c.n;
    const bodies = [];

    for (let f = 83; f < 83 + 9398; f++) {
      const sz = uleb(p);
      const bs = p + sz.n;

      bodies.push({
        f,
        start: bs,
        end: bs + sz.v
      });

      p = bs + sz.v;
    }

    return bodies;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

const bodies = findCode();

function opname(op: number) {
  const n: Record<number,string> = {
    0x20:"local.get",
    0x21:"local.set",
    0x22:"local.tee",
    0x41:"i32.const",
    0x6a:"i32.add",
    0x6b:"i32.sub",
    0x6c:"i32.mul",
    0x71:"i32.and",
    0x72:"i32.or",
    0x73:"i32.xor",
    0x74:"i32.shl",
    0x75:"i32.shr_s",
    0x76:"i32.shr_u",
    0x45:"i32.eqz"
  };
  return n[op] ?? `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT SELECTOR PRODUCER DEFINITIONS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {

  const body = bodies.find(x => x.f === t.f);
  if (!body) continue;

  const defs: {pos:number, kind:string, local:number}[] = [];

  let p = body.start;

  while (p < t.call) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      const x = uleb(p + 1);

      defs.push({
        pos: p,
        kind: op === 0x21 ? "set" : "tee",
        local: x.v
      });

      p += 1 + x.n;
      continue;
    }

    p += instrLen(p);
  }

  const relevant = defs.filter(x => x.local === t.local);

  console.log(`\nFUNC ${t.f}  CALL113 @ 0x${t.call.toString(16)}`);
  console.log(`SELECTOR LOCAL = ${t.local}`);
  console.log("--------------------------------------------");

  if (!relevant.length) {
    console.log("NO LOCAL DEFINITION FOUND BEFORE CALL");
    continue;
  }

  for (const d of relevant) {

    console.log(
      `\nDEF ${d.kind.toUpperCase()} @ 0x${d.pos.toString(16)}`
    );

    let q = Math.max(body.start, d.pos - 60);

    while (q < Math.min(body.end, d.pos + 4)) {

      const op = wasm[q];

      if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 ||
          op === 0x22 || op === 0x23 ||
          op === 0x24 || op === 0x41) {

        const x = uleb(q + 1);

        console.log(
          `  0x${q.toString(16)} ${opname(op)} ${x.v}` +
          (op === 0x41 ? ` (0x${x.v.toString(16)})` : "")
        );

        q += 1 + x.n;
        continue;
      }

      if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
        const a = uleb(q + 1);
        const b = uleb(q + 1 + a.n);

        console.log(
          `  0x${q.toString(16)} memop 0x${op.toString(16)}` +
          ` align=${a.v} offset=${b.v}`
        );

        q += 1 + a.n + b.n;
        continue;
      }

      console.log(
        `  0x${q.toString(16)} ${opname(op)}`
      );

      q++;
    }
  }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorReadersDataflow.ts
