import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x0019534c, "SELECTOR_4788"],
  [0x00195350, "SELECTOR_4789"],
  [0x00195354, "SELECTOR_4790"],
  [0x00195358, "SELECTOR_4791"],
]);

const TARGET_START = 0x0019534c;
const TARGET_END = 0x0019535b;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos,
      };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too large");
    }
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      break;
    }

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too large");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= ~0 << shift;
  }

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: pos,
  };
}

function targetName(addr: number) {
  return TARGETS.get(addr) ?? null;
}

function isTarget(addr: number) {
  return addr >= TARGET_START && addr <= TARGET_END;
}

function loadWidth(op: number) {
  switch (op) {
    case 0x28:
      return 4; // i32.load

    case 0x29:
      return 8; // i64.load

    case 0x2a:
      return 4; // f32.load

    case 0x2b:
      return 8; // f64.load

    case 0x2c:
    case 0x2d:
      return 1;

    case 0x2e:
    case 0x2f:
      return 2;

    default:
      return 0;
  }
}

function loadName(op: number) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function storeName(op: number) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",
    0x3c: "i64.store8",
    0x3d: "i64.store16",
    0x3e: "i64.store32",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function isLoad(op: number) {
  return op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x2f;
}

function isStore(op: number) {
  return op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e;
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",

    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",
    0x3c: "i64.store8",
    0x3d: "i64.store16",
    0x3e: "i64.store32",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor",
    0x74: "i32.shl",
    0x75: "i32.shr_s",
    0x76: "i32.shr_u",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

interface Instruction {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  op: number;
  name: string;

  imm?: number;
  imm2?: number;

  align?: number;
  offset?: number;

  typeIndex?: number;
  tableIndex?: number;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number): Instruction {
  const start = pos;
  const op = bytes[pos++];

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = readSLEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (isLoad(op) || isStore(op)) {
    const a = readULEB(pos);
    const o = readULEB(a.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: o.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      align: a.value,
      offset: o.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: "call",
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    const t = readULEB(x.next);

    return {
      start,
      end: t.next,
      op,
      name: "call_indirect",
      typeIndex: x.value,
      tableIndex: t.value,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const count = readULEB(pos);
    let q = count.next;

    const labels: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      labels.push(x.value);
    }

    const d = readULEB(q);
    q = d.next;

    return {
      start,
      end: q,
      op,
      name: "br_table",
      imm: count.value,
    };
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    name: opcodeName(op),
  };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Parse CODE section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

interface Body {
  ordinal: number;
  globalFunc: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  instrStart: number;
}

let importedFunctions = 0;

const bodies: Body[] = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < bytes.length) {
  const sectionId = bytes[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;

  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 2) {
    /*
     * Import section.
     */
    const count = readULEB(p);
    let q = count.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
      const moduleName = readULEB(q);
      q = moduleName.next;

      const fieldName = readULEB(q);
      q = fieldName.next;

      const kind = bytes[q++];

      if (kind === 0x00) {
        const typeIndex = readULEB(q);
        q = typeIndex.next;

        importedFunctions++;
      } else if (kind === 0x01) {
        q += 1;

        const flags = readULEB(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = readULEB(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = readULEB(q);
          q = max.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 0x02) {
        const flags = readULEB(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = readULEB(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = readULEB(q);
          q = max.next;
        }
      } else if (kind === 0x03) {
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    const count = readULEB(p);
    let q = count.next;

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
      const bodySize = readULEB(q);
      q = bodySize.next;

      const bodyStart = q;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

      const localDecl = readULEB(q);
      q = localDecl.next;

      for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        /*
         * local type byte
         */
        q++;
      }

      bodies.push({
        ordinal,
        globalFunc: importedFunctions + ordinal,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        instrStart: q,
      });

      q = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Abstract value model
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

type Value =
  | {
      kind: "const";
      value: number;
      origin: string;
    }
  | {
      kind: "selector";
      address: number;
      name: string;
      origin: string;
    }
  | {
      kind: "local";
      index: number;
      origin: string;
    }
  | {
      kind: "unknown";
      origin: string;
    };

function valueText(v: Value) {
  if (v.kind === "const") {
    return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;
  }

  if (v.kind === "selector") {
    return `${v.name}(${hex(v.address)})`;
  }

  if (v.kind === "local") {
    return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;
  }

  return "UNKNOWN";
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Analyze each function.
 *
 * We intentionally keep this conservative.
 * Unknown calls / branches invalidate the abstract stack,
 * preventing false positives.
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let readerCount = 0;
let selectorValueUses = 0;
let comparisonCount = 0;
let indirectCallCount = 0;
let directCallCount = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  const instructions: Instruction[] = [];

  let q = body.instrStart;

  while (q < body.bodyEnd) {
    let ins: Instruction;

    try {
      ins = decodeInstruction(q);
    } catch {
      break;
    }

    instructions.push(ins);

    if (ins.end <= q) {
      break;
    }

    q = ins.end;
  }

  /*
   * stack contains abstract values.
   */
  const stack: Value[] = [];

  /*
   * locals maps local index to abstract value.
   */
  const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

  /*
   * selectorLoadedLocals:
   *
   * local -> selector information
   *
   * This lets us follow:
   *
   * load selector
   * local.tee 3
   * ...
   * local.get 3
   */
  const selectorLoadedLocals = new Map<
    number,
    {
      address: number;
      name: string;
      loadOffset: number;
      loadPC: number;
    }
  >();

  function pop(): Value {
    if (stack.length === 0) {
      return {
        kind: "unknown",
        origin: "stack-underflow",
      };
    }

    return stack.pop()!;
  }

  function push(v: Value) {
    stack.push(v);
  }

  function resolveLocal(v: Value): Value {
    let current = v;

    for (let depth = 0; depth < 12; depth++) {
      if (current.kind !== "local") {
        return current;
      }

      const next = locals.get(current.index);

      if (!next) {
        return current;
      }

      current = next;
    }

    return {
      kind: "unknown",
      origin: "local-depth-limit",
    };
  }

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * CONSTANT
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x41) {
      push({
        kind: "const",
        value: ins.imm! >>> 0,
        origin: `i32.const @${hex(ins.start)}`,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * LOCAL.GET
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x20) {
      const local = ins.imm!;

      const known = locals.get(local);

      if (known) {
        push({
          ...known,
        });
      } else {
        push({
          kind: "local",
          index: local,
          origin: `local.get @${hex(ins.start)}`,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * LOCAL.SET
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x21) {
      const local = ins.imm!;
      const value = resolveLocal(pop());

      locals.set(local, value);

      if (value.kind === "selector") {
        selectorLoadedLocals.set(local, {
          address: value.address,
          name: value.name,
          loadOffset: ins.offset ?? 0,
          loadPC: ins.start,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * LOCAL.TEE
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x22) {
      const local = ins.imm!;

      const value =
        stack.length > 0
          ? resolveLocal(stack[stack.length - 1])
          : {
              kind: "unknown" as const,
              origin: "tee-stack-underflow",
            };

      locals.set(local, value);

      if (value.kind === "selector") {
        selectorLoadedLocals.set(local, {
          address: value.address,
          name: value.name,
          loadOffset: ins.offset ?? 0,
          loadPC: ins.start,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * I32.LOAD
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (isLoad(ins.op)) {
      const addressValue = resolveLocal(pop());

      if (addressValue.kind === "const") {
        const effective =
          (addressValue.value + (ins.offset ?? 0)) >>> 0;

        const name = targetName(effective);

        if (
          name !== null &&
          effective >= TARGET_START &&
          effective <= TARGET_END
        ) {
          readerCount++;

          console.log("");
          console.log("============================================================");
          console.log(
            `READER #${readerCount} | FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} | GLOBAL FUNC ${body.globalFunc}`
          );
          console.log("============================================================");
          console.log(`LOAD PC       = ${hex(ins.start)}`);
          console.log(`LOAD TYPE     = ${loadName(ins.op)}`);
          console.log(`BASE ADDRESS  = ${hex(addressValue.value)}`);
          console.log(`OFFSET        = ${hex(ins.offset ?? 0)}`);
          console.log(`EFFECTIVE     = ${hex(effective)}`);
          console.log(`TARGET        = ${name}`);

          /*
           * The result of this load is now tagged as selector data.
           */
          push({
            kind: "selector",
            address: effective,
            name,
            origin: `LOAD @${hex(ins.start)}`,
          });

          /*
           * Look forward a limited number of instructions and
           * show immediate uses of the loaded value.
           *
           * This is supplementary to the main abstract tracking.
           */
          const windowEnd = Math.min(
            instructions.length,
            i + 40
          );

          console.log("FORWARD WINDOW");
          console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

          for (let j = i + 1; j < windowEnd; j++) {
            const f = instructions[j];

            if (
              f.op === 0x46 ||
              f.op === 0x47 ||
              f.op === 0x45 ||
              (f.op >= 0x48 && f.op <= 0x4f)
            ) {
              comparisonCount++;

              console.log(
                `  ${hex(f.start)} ${f.name}`
              );
            }

            if (f.op === 0x11) {
              indirectCallCount++;

              console.log(
                `  ${hex(f.start)} call_indirect type=${f.typeIndex} table=${f.tableIndex}`
              );
            }

            if (f.op === 0x10) {
              directCallCount++;

              console.log(
                `  ${hex(f.start)} call ${f.imm}`
              );
            }

            if (
              f.op === 0x0f ||
              f.op === 0x0c ||
              f.op === 0x0d ||
              f.op === 0x0e
            ) {
              break;
            }
          }

          continue;
        }
      }

      /*
       * Normal load:
       * address consumed, result unknown.
       */
      push({
        kind: "unknown",
        origin: `LOAD @${hex(ins.start)}`,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * COMPARISONS
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      ins.op === 0x45 ||
      ins.op === 0x46 ||
      ins.op === 0x47 ||
      (ins.op >= 0x48 && ins.op <= 0x4f)
    ) {
      const rhs =
        ins.op === 0x45
          ? null
          : resolveLocal(pop());

      const lhs =
        ins.op === 0x45
          ? resolveLocal(pop())
          : resolveLocal(pop());

      const involved =
        (lhs?.kind === "selector") ||
        (rhs?.kind === "selector");

      if (involved) {
        selectorValueUses++;

        console.log(
          `  >>> SELECTOR USE @ ${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.name}`
        );
        console.log(
          `      LHS = ${valueText(lhs)}`
        );

        if (rhs !== null) {
          console.log(
            `      RHS = ${valueText(rhs)}`
          );
        }

        /*
         * If one side is selector and other side is constant,
         * this is particularly valuable.
         */
        if (
          lhs?.kind === "selector" &&
          rhs?.kind === "const"
        ) {
          console.log(
            `      *** ${lhs.name} COMPARED WITH ${hex(rhs.value)} ***`
          );
        }

        if (
          rhs?.kind === "selector" &&
          lhs?.kind === "const"
        ) {
          console.log(
            `      *** ${rhs.name} COMPARED WITH ${hex(lhs.value)} ***`
          );
        }
      }

      push({
        kind: "unknown",
        origin: `comparison @${hex(ins.start)}`,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * CALL INDIRECT
     *
     * Stack immediately before call_indirect contains:
     *
     *   arguments ... table-index
     *
     * We cannot reconstruct the full signature here without
     * parsing type information, but we can inspect whether
     * selector-tagged values are still present near the top.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x11) {
      let selectorFound = false;

      for (
        let k = Math.max(0, stack.length - 8);
        k < stack.length;
        k++
      ) {
        if (stack[k].kind === "selector") {
          selectorFound = true;

          console.log("");
          console.log(
            `  >>> SELECTOR REACHES CALL_INDIRECT @ ${hex(ins.start)}`
          );
          console.log(
            `      ${valueText(stack[k])}`
          );
          console.log(
            `      TYPE INDEX = ${ins.typeIndex}`
          );
          console.log(
            `      TABLE      = ${ins.tableIndex}`
          );
        }
      }

      /*
       * Unknown number of arguments/results:
       * invalidate stack conservatively.
       */
      stack.length = 0;

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * DIRECT CALL
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x10) {
      let selectorFound = false;

      for (
        let k = Math.max(0, stack.length - 8);
        k < stack.length;
        k++
      ) {
        if (stack[k].kind === "selector") {
          selectorFound = true;

          console.log("");
          console.log(
            `  >>> SELECTOR REACHES DIRECT CALL @ ${hex(ins.start)}`
          );
          console.log(
            `      ${valueText(stack[k])}`
          );
          console.log(
            `      TARGET FUNC = ${ins.imm}`
          );
        }
      }

      /*
       * Unknown signature:
       * invalidate stack.
       */
      stack.length = 0;

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * INTEGER ADD/SUB
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x6a || ins.op === 0x6b) {
      const rhs = resolveLocal(pop());
      const lhs = resolveLocal(pop());

      /*
       * Preserve selector identity when arithmetic is with zero.
       */
      if (
        lhs.kind === "selector" &&
        rhs.kind === "const" &&
        rhs.value === 0
      ) {
        push(lhs);
      } else if (
        rhs.kind === "selector" &&
        lhs.kind === "const" &&
        lhs.value === 0
      ) {
        push(rhs);
      } else {
        push({
          kind: "unknown",
          origin: `${ins.name} @${hex(ins.start)}`,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * DROP
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x1a) {
      pop();
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * CONTROL FLOW
     *
     * Conservative invalidation.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (
      ins.op === 0x02 ||
      ins.op === 0x03 ||
      ins.op === 0x04 ||
      ins.op === 0x05 ||
      ins.op === 0x0b ||
      ins.op === 0x0c ||
      ins.op === 0x0d ||
      ins.op === 0x0e ||
      ins.op === 0x0f
    ) {
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * GLOBAL GET
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (ins.op === 0x23) {
      push({
        kind: "unknown",
        origin: `global.get ${ins.imm}`,
      });

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * STORE
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    if (isStore(ins.op)) {
      /*
       * address + value
       */
      pop();
      pop();

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     * UNKNOWN INSTRUCTION
     *
     * Do not invent dataflow.
     * --------------------------------------------------------
     */

    stack.length = 0;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`READER LOADS FOUND        = ${readerCount}`);
console.log(`SELECTOR VALUE USES       = ${selectorValueUses}`);
console.log(`COMPARISON OBSERVATIONS   = ${comparisonCount}`);
console.log(`CALL_INDIRECT OBSERVATIONS= ${indirectCallCount}`);
console.log(`DIRECT CALL OBSERVATIONS  = ${directCallCount}`);
console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorStoreDataflow.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
  [0x0019534c, "SELECTOR_4788"],
  [0x00195350, "SELECTOR_4789"],
  [0x00195354, "SELECTOR_4790"],
  [0x00195358, "SELECTOR_4791"],
]);

const TARGET_MIN = 0x0019534c;
const TARGET_MAX = 0x0019535b;

const bytes = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function u32le(p: number) {
  return (
    bytes[p] |
    (bytes[p + 1] << 8) |
    (bytes[p + 2] << 16) |
    (bytes[p + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB too large");
    }
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = bytes[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB too large");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function isMemoryStore(op: number) {
  return (
    op === 0x36 || // i32.store
    op === 0x37 || // i64.store
    op === 0x38 || // f32.store
    op === 0x39 || // f64.store
    op === 0x3a || // i32.store8
    op === 0x3b || // i32.store16
    op === 0x3c || // i64.store8
    op === 0x3d || // i64.store16
    op === 0x3e    // i64.store32
  );
}

function storeSize(op: number) {
  switch (op) {
    case 0x3a:
      return 1;
    case 0x3b:
      return 2;
    case 0x3c:
      return 1;
    case 0x3d:
      return 2;
    case 0x3e:
      return 4;
    case 0x36:
      return 4;
    case 0x37:
      return 8;
    case 0x38:
      return 4;
    case 0x39:
      return 8;
    default:
      return 0;
  }
}

function storeName(op: number) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",
    0x3c: "i64.store8",
    0x3d: "i64.store16",
    0x3e: "i64.store32",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function decodeMemarg(p: number) {
  const a = readULEB(p);
  const o = readULEB(a.next);

  return {
    align: a.value,
    offset: o.value,
    next: o.next,
  };
}

function opcodeName(op: number) {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x2a: "f32.load",
    0x2b: "f64.load",
    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
    0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x38: "f32.store",
    0x39: "f64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",
    0x3b: "i32.store16",
    0x3c: "i64.store8",
    0x3d: "i64.store16",
    0x3e: "i64.store32",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
    0x0e: "br_table",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

/*
 * Parse type section just enough to identify function signatures.
 */
let typeCount = 0;
let funcTypes: number[] = [];
let importedFunctions = 0;
let codeBodies: {
  ordinal: number;
  globalFunc: number;
  bodyStart: number;
  bodyEnd: number;
  localsCount: number;
  instrStart: number;
}[] = [];

let p = 8;

if (
  bytes[0] !== 0x00 ||
  bytes[1] !== 0x61 ||
  bytes[2] !== 0x73 ||
  bytes[3] !== 0x6d
) {
  throw new Error("Not a WASM file");
}

while (p < bytes.length) {
  const sectionId = bytes[p++];
  const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 1) {
    const c = readULEB(p);
    typeCount = c.value;
  }

  if (sectionId === 2) {
    const c = readULEB(p);
    let q = c.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      const mod = readULEB(q);
      q = mod.next;

      const nm = readULEB(q);
      q = nm.next;

      const kind = bytes[q++];

      if (kind === 0x00) {
        const ti = readULEB(q);
        q = ti.next;
        importedFunctions++;
      } else if (kind === 0x01) {
        const elemType = bytes[q++];
        const flags = readULEB(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = readULEB(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = readULEB(q);
          q = max.next;
        }

        void elemType;
        void min;
      } else if (kind === 0x02) {
        const flags = readULEB(q);
        q = flags.next;

        const min = readULEB(q);
        q = min.next;

        if (flags.value & 1) {
          const max = readULEB(q);
          q = max.next;
        }

        void min;
      } else if (kind === 0x03) {
        q += 2;
      }
    }
  }

  if (sectionId === 3) {
    const c = readULEB(p);
    let q = c.next;

    funcTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      const ti = readULEB(q);
      q = ti.next;
      funcTypes.push(ti.value);
    }
  }

  if (sectionId === 10) {
    const c = readULEB(p);
    let q = c.next;

    for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < c.value; ordinal++) {
      const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
      const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
      q = bodySizeInfo.next;

      const bodyStart = q;
      const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

      const localDecls = readULEB(q);
      q = localDecls.next;

      let localsCount = 0;

      for (let j = 0; j < localDecls.value; j++) {
        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        q += 1;

        localsCount += n.value;
      }

      codeBodies.push({
        ordinal,
        globalFunc: importedFunctions + ordinal,
        bodyStart,
        bodyEnd,
        localsCount,
        instrStart: q,
      });

      q = bodyEnd;
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos: number) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = bytes[pos++];

  if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x42) {
    const x = readSLEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f ||
    isMemoryStore(op)
  ) {
    const m = decodeMemarg(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: m.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      align: m.align,
      offset: m.offset,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);
    let q = x.next;

    const table = readULEB(q);
    q = table.next;

    return {
      start,
      end: q,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      typeIndex: x.value,
      tableIndex: table.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readULEB(pos);

    return {
      start,
      end: x.next,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      imm: x.value,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x0e) {
    const c = readULEB(pos);
    let q = c.next;

    const labels: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < c.value; i++) {
      const x = readULEB(q);
      q = x.next;
      labels.push(x.value);
    }

    const d = readULEB(q);
    q = d.next;

    return {
      start,
      end: q,
      op,
      name: opcodeName(op),
      labels,
      defaultLabel: d.value,
    };
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    name: opcodeName(op),
  };
}

/*
 * Constant/address provenance lattice.
 *
 * We track only exact i32 constants and simple affine expressions:
 *
 *   CONST
 *   ADD(CONST, CONST)
 *   SUB(CONST, CONST)
 *   LOCAL
 *   UNKNOWN
 *
 * This is intentionally conservative.
 */

type Value =
  | { kind: "const"; value: number; from: string }
  | { kind: "local"; index: number; from: string }
  | { kind: "unknown"; from: string };

function describe(v: Value) {
  if (v.kind === "const") return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;
  if (v.kind === "local") return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;
  return "UNKNOWN";
}

function localResolve(
  locals: Map<number, Value>,
  index: number,
  depth = 0
): Value {
  if (depth > 12) {
    return { kind: "unknown", from: "depth-limit" };
  }

  const v = locals.get(index);

  if (!v) {
    return { kind: "local", index, from: "uninitialized" };
  }

  if (v.kind === "local") {
    if (v.index === index) return v;
    return localResolve(locals, v.index, depth + 1);
  }

  return v;
}

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR STORE DATAFLOW");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET CELLS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const [addr, name] of TARGETS) {
  console.log(`${name.padEnd(15)} : ${hex(addr)}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("EXACT ADDRESS PROVENANCE");
console.log("============================================================");

let exactHits = 0;
let partialHits = 0;

for (const body of codeBodies) {
  const instructions: any[] = [];

  let q = body.instrStart;

  while (q < body.bodyEnd - 1) {
    let ins;

    try {
      ins = decodeInstruction(q);
    } catch {
      break;
    }

    instructions.push(ins);

    if (ins.end <= q) break;

    q = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b && q >= body.bodyEnd) break;
  }

  /*
   * Lightweight forward stack simulation.
   *
   * We do not execute branches.
   * Unknown control-flow joins are conservatively represented as UNKNOWN.
   */
  const stack: Value[] = [];
  const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {
    const ins = instructions[i];

    if (ins.op === 0x41) {
      stack.push({
        kind: "const",
        value: ins.imm >>> 0,
        from: `${hex(ins.start)}`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.op === 0x20) {
      stack.push({
        kind: "local",
        index: ins.imm,
        from: `${hex(ins.start)}`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.op === 0x21) {
      const v =
        stack.length > 0
          ? stack.pop()!
          : { kind: "unknown", from: `${hex(ins.start)}` };

      locals.set(ins.imm, v);
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.op === 0x22) {
      const v =
        stack.length > 0
          ? stack[stack.length - 1]
          : { kind: "unknown", from: `${hex(ins.start)}` };

      locals.set(ins.imm, v);
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.op === 0x23) {
      stack.push({
        kind: "unknown",
        from: `global.get ${ins.imm}`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.op === 0x28) {
      if (stack.length >= 1) {
        stack.pop();
      }

      stack.push({
        kind: "unknown",
        from: `i32.load @${hex(ins.start)}`,
      });
      continue;
    }

    if (ins.op === 0x6a || ins.op === 0x6b) {
      const b =
        stack.length > 0
          ? stack.pop()!
          : { kind: "unknown", from: "missing-rhs" };

      const a =
        stack.length > 0
          ? stack.pop()!
          : { kind: "unknown", from: "missing-lhs" };

      if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const") {
        const value =
          ins.op === 0x6a
            ? (a.value + b.value) >>> 0
            : (a.value - b.value) >>> 0;

        stack.push({
          kind: "const",
          value,
          from: `${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.name}`,
        });
      } else {
        stack.push({
          kind: "unknown",
          from: `${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.name}`,
        });
      }

      continue;
    }

    if (isMemoryStore(ins.op)) {
      /*
       * Before store:
       *
       *   ... address value
       *
       * Therefore value is popped first and address second.
       */
      const value =
        stack.length > 0
          ? stack.pop()!
          : { kind: "unknown", from: "missing-value" };

      const address =
        stack.length > 0
          ? stack.pop()!
          : { kind: "unknown", from: "missing-address" };

      if (address.kind === "const") {
        const effective = (address.value + ins.offset) >>> 0;
        const size = storeSize(ins.op);

        const target =
          effective <= TARGET_MAX &&
          effective + size - 1 >= TARGET_MIN;

        if (target) {
          exactHits++;

          console.log("");
          console.log(
            `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} / GLOBAL FUNC ${body.globalFunc} @ ${hex(
              ins.start
            )}`
          );
          console.log(
            `  ${storeName(ins.op)} offset=${hex(ins.offset)} size=${size}`
          );
          console.log(`  ADDRESS PROVEN = ${hex(address.value)}`);
          console.log(`  EFFECTIVE      = ${hex(effective)}`);

          for (const [addr, name] of TARGETS) {
            if (
              effective <= addr + 3 &&
              effective + size - 1 >= addr
            ) {
              console.log(`  *** TARGET OVERLAP: ${name} ***`);
            }
          }

          console.log(`  STORED VALUE   = ${describe(value)}`);
        }
      } else if (address.kind === "local") {
        const resolved = localResolve(locals, address.index);

        if (resolved.kind === "const") {
          const effective = (resolved.value + ins.offset) >>> 0;
          const size = storeSize(ins.op);

          if (
            effective <= TARGET_MAX &&
            effective + size - 1 >= TARGET_MIN
          ) {
            partialHits++;

            console.log("");
            console.log(
              `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} / GLOBAL FUNC ${body.globalFunc} @ ${hex(
                ins.start
              )}`
            );
            console.log(
              `  ${storeName(ins.op)} offset=${hex(ins.offset)} size=${size}`
            );
            console.log(`  ADDRESS LOCAL  = ${address.index}`);
            console.log(`  RESOLVED       = ${hex(resolved.value)}`);
            console.log(`  EFFECTIVE      = ${hex(effective)}`);
            console.log(`  STORED VALUE   = ${describe(value)}`);
          }
        }
      }

      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Conservative handling for calls.
     * We cannot know their return values without interprocedural analysis.
     */
    if (ins.op === 0x10 || ins.op === 0x11) {
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Comparisons and most arithmetic not needed for exact address
     * provenance destroy our simple stack model.
     */
    if (
      ins.op === 0x45 ||
      ins.op === 0x46 ||
      ins.op === 0x47 ||
      (ins.op >= 0x48 && ins.op <= 0x4f)
    ) {
      if (stack.length >= 2) {
        stack.pop();
        stack.pop();
        stack.push({
          kind: "unknown",
          from: `${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.name}`,
        });
      } else {
        stack.length = 0;
        stack.push({
          kind: "unknown",
          from: `${hex(ins.start)} ${ins.name}`,
        });
      }
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Control flow can merge different stack states.
     * We deliberately invalidate the stack rather than inventing
     * an address.
     */
    if (
      ins.op === 0x02 ||
      ins.op === 0x03 ||
      ins.op === 0x04 ||
      ins.op === 0x05 ||
      ins.op === 0x0b ||
      ins.op === 0x0c ||
      ins.op === 0x0d ||
      ins.op === 0x0e ||
      ins.op === 0x0f
    ) {
      stack.length = 0;
      continue;
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`EXACT CONSTANT ADDRESS HITS = ${exactHits}`);
console.log(`LOCAL-RESOLVED ADDRESS HITS  = ${partialHits}`);
console.log("");
console.log(
  "NOTE: Only the effective Store address is considered a target."
);
console.log(
  "A nearby i32.const is NOT treated as evidence of a selector write."
);
console.log(
  "This scan is conservative and READ ONLY; WASM is never executed."
);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorStores.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGETS = [
  0x19534c,
  0x195350,
  0x195354,
  0x195358,
];

const TARGET_NAMES: Record<number, string> = {
  0x19534c: "SELECTOR_4788",
  0x195350: "SELECTOR_4789",
  0x195354: "SELECTOR_4790",
  0x195358: "SELECTOR_4791",
};

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (true) {
    b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value, next: p };
}

function findCodeSection() {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + s.value;

    if (id === 10) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function parseBodies() {
  const code = findCodeSection();

  let p = code.start;

  const count = readULEB(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies: any[] = [];

  for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
    const size = readULEB(p);
    p = size.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = p + size.value;

    const localCount = readULEB(p);
    p = localCount.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
      const n = readULEB(p);
      p = n.next;
      p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
      ordinal,
      bodyStart,
      bodyEnd,
      instrStart: p,
    });

    p = bodyEnd;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function decode(p: number) {
  const start = p;
  const op = wasm[p++];

  if (op === 0x41) {
    const x = readSLEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      imm: x.value,
      text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2a ||
    op === 0x2b ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x2e ||
    op === 0x2f ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x38 ||
    op === 0x39
  ) {
    const align = readULEB(p);
    p = align.next;

    const offset = readULEB(p);
    p = offset.next;

    const store =
      op === 0x36 ||
      op === 0x37 ||
      op === 0x38 ||
      op === 0x39;

    return {
      next: p,
      op,
      offset: offset.value,
      store,
      text:
        (store ? "STORE" : "LOAD") +
        ` align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x10) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      imm: x.value,
      text: `call ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (op === 0x11) {
    const type = readULEB(p);
    p = type.next;

    const table = readULEB(p);
    p = table.next;

    return {
      next: p,
      op,
      text: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
    };
  }

  if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22 ||
    op === 0x23 ||
    op === 0x24
  ) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    const names: Record<number, string> = {
      0x20: "local.get",
      0x21: "local.set",
      0x22: "local.tee",
      0x23: "global.get",
      0x24: "global.set",
    };

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      imm: x.value,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x71: "i32.xor",
    0x0b: "end",
    0x0f: "return",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",
  };

  if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
    const x = readULEB(p);

    return {
      next: x.next,
      op,
      imm: x.value,
      text: `${names[op]} ${x.value}`,
    };
  }

  return {
    next: p,
    op,
    text: names[op] ?? `op_0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`,
  };
}

const bodies = parseBodies();

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR STORES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET MEMORY CELLS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  console.log(
    `${TARGET_NAMES[target]} : ${hex(target)}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT STORE CANDIDATES");
console.log("============================================================");

let total = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {
  let p = body.instrStart;

  const recentConsts: {
    addr: number;
    value: number;
  }[] = [];

  const window: {
    addr: number;
    next: number;
    text: string;
    op: number;
    offset?: number;
    imm?: number;
    store?: boolean;
  }[] = [];

  while (p < body.bodyEnd) {
    const ins = decode(p);

    window.push({
      addr: p,
      next: ins.next,
      text: ins.text,
      op: ins.op,
      offset: ins.offset,
      imm: ins.imm,
      store: ins.store,
    });

    if (ins.op === 0x41) {
      recentConsts.push({
        addr: p,
        value: ins.imm!,
      });

      while (recentConsts.length > 8) {
        recentConsts.shift();
      }
    }

    if (ins.store) {
      const nearby = recentConsts.filter(
        x =>
          x.value >= 0x195300 &&
          x.value <= 0x195380
      );

      if (nearby.length > 0) {
        console.log("");
       console.log(
  `FUNC ORDINAL ${body.ordinal} @ ${hex(p)}`
);

console.log(
  `  ${ins.text}`
);

        for (const c of nearby) {
          console.log(
            `  RECENT CONST ${hex(c.value)} @ ${hex(c.addr)}`
          );

          if (TARGETS.includes(c.value)) {
            console.log(
              `  *** DIRECT TARGET ADDRESS CONSTANT ***`
            );
          }
        }

        const start =
          Math.max(0, window.length - 12);

        for (let i = start; i < window.length; i++) {
          const x = window[i];

          console.log(
            `  ${hex(x.addr)}  ${x.text}`
          );
        }

        total++;
      }
    }

    p = ins.next;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
  `DIRECT STORE CANDIDATES = ${total}`
);
console.log("");
console.log(
  "IMPORTANT: absence of a candidate does NOT prove that the selector is immutable."
);
console.log(
  "A computed pointer may still reach the target through dataflow."
);

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorValueProducers.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGETS = new Set([4788, 4789, 4790, 4791]);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function hex(n: number) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function opname(op: number): string {
    switch (op) {
        case 0x20: return "local.get";
        case 0x21: return "local.set";
        case 0x22: return "local.tee";
        case 0x28: return "i32.load";
        case 0x36: return "i32.store";
        case 0x37: return "i64.store";
        case 0x3a: return "i32.store8";
        case 0x3b: return "i32.store16";
        case 0x41: return "i32.const";
        case 0x10: return "call";
        case 0x11: return "call_indirect";
        case 0x6a: return "i32.add";
        case 0x6b: return "i32.sub";
        default: return `opcode 0x${op.toString(16)}`;
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTOR VALUE PRODUCERS");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET VALUES = 4788, 4789, 4790, 4791");
console.log("============================================================");

//
// Locate code section
//
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p++];

    const s = readULEB(p);
    const size = s.value;
    p = s.next;

    const payload = p;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = payload;
        codeEnd = payload + size;
        break;
    }

    p += size;
}

if (!codeStart) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let q = codeStart;

const functionCount = readULEB(q);
q = functionCount.next;

let totalHits = 0;

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount.value; ordinal++) {

    const bodyInfo = readULEB(q);
    const bodySize = bodyInfo.value;
    q = bodyInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;

    let x = bodyStart;

    //
    // locals
    //
    const groups = readULEB(x);
    x = groups.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < groups.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(x);
        x = n.next;
        x++;
    }

    const history: {
        pos: number;
        text: string;
    }[] = [];

    function push(pos: number, text: string) {
        history.push({ pos, text });

        if (history.length > 10)
            history.shift();
    }

    while (x < bodyEnd) {

        const opPos = x;
        const op = buf[x++];

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            push(opPos, "end");
            break;
        }

        //
        // i32.const
        //
        if (op === 0x41) {

            const r = readSLEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            const value = r.value;

            if (TARGETS.has(value >>> 0)) {

                totalHits++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
                console.log(`HIT #${totalHits}`);
                console.log(`FUNC ORDINAL = ${ordinal}`);
                console.log(`ADDRESS      = ${hex(opPos)}`);
                console.log(`VALUE        = ${value}`);
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

                for (const h of history) {
                    console.log(
                        `  ${hex(h.pos)}  ${h.text}`
                    );
                }

                console.log(
                    `  ${hex(opPos)}  i32.const ${value}`
                );
            }

            push(opPos, `i32.const ${value}`);
            continue;
        }

        //
        // local.get
        //
        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `local.get ${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // local.set
        //
        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `local.set ${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // local.tee
        //
        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `local.tee ${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // call
        //
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `call ${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // call_indirect
        //
        if (op === 0x11) {
            const r = readULEB(x);
            x = r.next;

            //
            // table index follows type index
            //
            const table = readULEB(x);
            x = table.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `call_indirect type=${r.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // i32.load
        //
        if (op === 0x28) {

            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(x);
            x = off.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `i32.load offset=${off.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // i32.store / i64.store / store8 / store16
        //
        if (
            op === 0x36 ||
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x3a ||
            op === 0x3b
        ) {

            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(x);
            x = off.next;

            push(
                opPos,
                `${opname(op)} offset=${off.value}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // i32.add/sub
        //
        if (op === 0x6a || op === 0x6b) {

            push(
                opPos,
                opname(op)
            );

            continue;
        }

        //
        // Other instructions
        //
        push(
            opPos,
            opname(op)
        );
    }

    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL SELECTOR VALUE LITERALS = ${totalHits}`);
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceSelectorWriteProvenance.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [0x0019534c, "SELECTOR_4788"],
    [0x00195350, "SELECTOR_4789"],
    [0x00195354, "SELECTOR_4790"],
    [0x00195358, "SELECTOR_4791"],
]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, w = 8) {
    return "0x" + (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let byte = 0;

    while (true) {
        byte = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((byte & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function readBlockType(pos: number) {
    const b = wasm[pos];

    if (b === 0x40)
        return { next: pos + 1 };

    if (b === 0x7f || b === 0x7e || b === 0x7d || b === 0x7c)
        return { next: pos + 1 };

    return readSLEB(pos);
}

type Val =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number }
    | { kind: "selector"; addr: number; name: string }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number; source?: Val }
    | { kind: "unknown" };

function clone(v: Val): Val {
    if (v.kind === "const")
        return { kind: "const", value: v.value };

    if (v.kind === "selector")
        return {
            kind: "selector",
            addr: v.addr,
            name: v.name,
        };

    if (v.kind === "local")
        return {
            kind: "local",
            index: v.index,
            source: v.source ? clone(v.source) : undefined,
        };

    return { kind: "unknown" };
}

function describe(v: Val): string {
    switch (v.kind) {
        case "const":
            return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;

        case "selector":
            return `${v.name}@${hex(v.addr)}`;

        case "local":
            if (v.source)
                return `LOCAL(${v.index}) <- ${describe(v.source)}`;
            return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;

        default:
            return "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

function parseCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = readULEB(p);
        p = s.next;

        const sectionStart = p;
        const sectionEnd = p + s.value;

        if (id === 10) {
            const count = readULEB(p);
            p = count.next;

            const bodies: {
                ordinal: number;
                start: number;
                end: number;
                codeStart: number;
            }[] = [];

            for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < count.value; ordinal++) {
                const bodySize = readULEB(p);
                p = bodySize.next;

                const bodyStart = p;
                const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize.value;

                const localCount = readULEB(p);
                p = localCount.next;

                for (let i = 0; i < localCount.value; i++) {
                    const n = readULEB(p);
                    p = n.next;

                    p += 1;
                }

                bodies.push({
                    ordinal,
                    start: bodyStart,
                    end: bodyEnd,
                    codeStart: p,
                });

                p = bodyEnd;
            }

            return {
                start: sectionStart,
                end: sectionEnd,
                bodies,
            };
        }

        p = sectionEnd;
    }

    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

function selectorFromAddress(addr: number): Val | null {
    const name = TARGETS.get(addr >>> 0);

    if (!name)
        return null;

    return {
        kind: "selector",
        addr: addr >>> 0,
        name,
    };
}

function parseFunction(
    body: {
        ordinal: number;
        start: number;
        end: number;
        codeStart: number;
    }
) {
    const stack: Val[] = [];
    const locals = new Map<number, Val>();

    let p = body.codeStart;

    let exactStores = 0;
    let exactIndirect = 0;

    function pop(): Val {
        if (stack.length === 0)
            return { kind: "unknown" };

        return stack.pop()!;
    }

    function push(v: Val) {
        stack.push(v);
    }

    function getLocal(index: number): Val {
        const v = locals.get(index);

        if (!v)
            return {
                kind: "local",
                index,
            };

        return clone(v);
    }

    function setLocal(index: number, value: Val) {
        locals.set(index, clone(value));
    }

    while (p < body.end) {
        const pc = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // END
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            break;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // NOP
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x01) {
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // BLOCK / LOOP / IF
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
            const r = readBlockType(p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // ELSE
        if (op === 0x05) {
            continue;
        }

        // BR / BR_IF
        if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;
            continue;
        }

        // BR_TABLE
        if (op === 0x0e) {
            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                const r = readULEB(p);
                p = r.next;
            }

            const d = readULEB(p);
            p = d.next;

            pop();
            continue;
        }

        // RETURN
        if (op === 0x0f) {
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // CALL
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            /*
             * We deliberately do not model call arguments here.
             * The important target is exact selector stores.
             *
             * Preserve locals and do not manufacture provenance.
             */
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // CALL_INDIRECT
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const type = readULEB(p);
            p = type.next;

            const table = readULEB(p);
            p = table.next;

            const index = pop();

            if (index.kind === "selector") {
                exactIndirect++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
                console.log(`SELECTOR USED AS INDIRECT INDEX`);
                console.log(`FUNCTION ORDINAL = ${body.ordinal}`);
                console.log(`PC               = ${hex(pc)}`);
                console.log(`TYPE INDEX       = ${type.value}`);
                console.log(`TABLE            = ${table.value}`);
                console.log(`INDEX            = ${describe(index)}`);
                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
            }

            /*
             * We cannot safely reconstruct the return stack without
             * function signatures, so discard remaining symbolic stack.
             */
            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LOCAL.GET
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push(getLocal(r.value));
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LOCAL.SET
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop();

            setLocal(r.value, value);
            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // LOCAL.TEE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            const value = pop();

            setLocal(r.value, value);
            push(value);

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I32.CONST
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push({
                kind: "const",
                value: r.value >>> 0,
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I64.CONST
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const r = readSLEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I32.LOAD
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x28) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            const address = pop();

            let result: Val = {
                kind: "unknown",
            };

            if (address.kind === "const") {
                const effective = (address.value + off.value) >>> 0;

                const selector = selectorFromAddress(effective);

                if (selector) {
                    result = selector;

                    console.log(
                        `READ TARGET @ ${hex(pc)} ${describe(selector)}`
                    );
                }
            }

            push(result);

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I32.STORE
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x36) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            /*
             * WASM store stack:
             *
             *     ... address value
             *
             * Therefore value is popped first, address second.
             */
            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            let resolvedAddress: Val = address;

            if (address.kind === "const") {
                const effective = (address.value + off.value) >>> 0;

                const selector = selectorFromAddress(effective);

                if (selector)
                    resolvedAddress = selector;
            }

            if (resolvedAddress.kind === "selector") {
                exactStores++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log("EXACT SELECTOR STORE");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log(`FUNCTION ORDINAL = ${body.ordinal}`);
                console.log(`PC               = ${hex(pc)}`);
                console.log(`ADDRESS          = ${describe(resolvedAddress)}`);
                console.log(`VALUE            = ${describe(value)}`);
                console.log(`ALIGN            = ${align.value}`);
                console.log(`OFFSET           = ${off.value}`);
                console.log("============================================================");
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I32.STORE8 / STORE16
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x3a || op === 0x3b) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            if (address.kind === "const") {
                const effective = (address.value + off.value) >>> 0;

                const selector = selectorFromAddress(effective);

                if (selector) {
                    console.log("");
                    console.log("============================================================");
                    console.log("BYTE/SHORT STORE TO SELECTOR");
                    console.log("============================================================");
                    console.log(`FUNCTION ORDINAL = ${body.ordinal}`);
                    console.log(`PC               = ${hex(pc)}`);
                    console.log(`ADDRESS          = ${describe(selector)}`);
                    console.log(`VALUE            = ${describe(value)}`);
                    console.log(`ALIGN            = ${align.value}`);
                    console.log(`OFFSET           = ${off.value}`);
                    console.log("============================================================");
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // GENERIC LOADS
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x29 ||
            op === 0x2a ||
            op === 0x2b ||
            op === 0x2c ||
            op === 0x2d ||
            op === 0x2e ||
            op === 0x2f ||
            op === 0x30 ||
            op === 0x31 ||
            op === 0x32 ||
            op === 0x33 ||
            op === 0x34 ||
            op === 0x35
        ) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            pop();

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // OTHER STORES
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x37 ||
            op === 0x38 ||
            op === 0x39
        ) {
            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            pop();
            pop();

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // SELECT
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x1b) {
            const condition = pop();
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            if (a.kind === "selector")
                push(a);
            else if (b.kind === "selector")
                push(b);
            else
                push({ kind: "unknown" });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I32.EQZ
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x45) {
            pop();

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------
        // I32 binary operators
        // ------------------------------------------------------------

        const binaryOps = new Set([
            0x46, 0x47, 0x48, 0x49, 0x4a, 0x4b,
            0x4c, 0x4d, 0x4e, 0x4f, 0x50, 0x51,
            0x6a, 0x6b, 0x6c, 0x6d, 0x6e, 0x6f,
            0x70, 0x71, 0x72, 0x73, 0x74, 0x75,
            0x76, 0x77, 0x78, 0x79, 0x7a,
            0x7b, 0x7c, 0x7d, 0x7e,
        ]);

        if (binaryOps.has(op)) {
            pop();
            pop();

            push({
                kind: "unknown",
            });

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Unknown instructions are intentionally ignored.
         *
         * This is a conservative provenance pass:
         * unknown != selector.
         */
    }

    return {
        exactStores,
        exactIndirect,
    };
}

const code = parseCodeSection();

console.log("TRACE SELECTOR WRITE PROVENANCE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CODE SECTION = ${hex(code.start)} .. ${hex(code.end)}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGET SELECTOR ADDRESSES");
console.log(`  ${hex(0x0019534c)} = SELECTOR_4788`);
console.log(`  ${hex(0x00195350)} = SELECTOR_4789`);
console.log(`  ${hex(0x00195354)} = SELECTOR_4790`);
console.log(`  ${hex(0x00195358)} = SELECTOR_4791`);
console.log("");
console.log(`CODE BODIES = ${code.bodies.length}`);
console.log("============================================================");

let totalStores = 0;
let totalIndirect = 0;

for (const body of code.bodies) {
    const result = parseFunction(body);

    totalStores += result.exactStores;
    totalIndirect += result.exactIndirect;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`EXACT SELECTOR STORES   = ${totalStores}`);
console.log(`SELECTOR INDIRECT CALLS = ${totalIndirect}`);
console.log("============================================================");

if (totalStores === 0) {
    console.log("");
    console.log("NO EXACT SELECTOR STORE FOUND");
    console.log("");
    console.log("Interpretation:");
    console.log("No constant-address i32.store to the four selector cells");
    console.log("was proven by this conservative pass.");
    console.log("");
    console.log("This does NOT yet prove runtime immutability.");
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("NO WASM EXECUTION");

FILE: scripts\traceStateDeps.js
