const fs = require("fs");

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTIONS = [104, 270];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE DEPENDENCY TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGETS   : FUNC 104 / FUNC 270");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32Leb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readI32Leb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function opcodeName(op) {
  const names = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",

    0x0c: "br",
    0x0d: "br_if",

    0x10: "call",

    0x1a: "drop",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",

    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",

    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x6d: "i32.div_s",
    0x6e: "i32.div_u",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor"
  };

  return names[op] ||
    `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos) {

  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let text = opcodeName(op);

  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {

    const align = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = align.next;

    const offset = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = offset.next;

    text +=
      ` align=${align.value}` +
      ` offset=0x${offset.value.toString(16)}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x41) {

    const imm = readI32Leb(pos);
    pos = imm.next;

    text +=
      ` ${imm.value}` +
      ` (0x${(imm.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {

    const imm = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = imm.next;

    text += ` local=${imm.value}`;
  }

  else if (op === 0x10) {

    const imm = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = imm.next;

    text += ` func=${imm.value}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x02 ||
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {

    const blockType = wasm[pos++];

    text +=
      ` blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
  }

  else if (
    op === 0x0c ||
    op === 0x0d
  ) {

    const depth = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = depth.next;

    text += ` depth=${depth.value}`;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text
  };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Sections
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;

let importCount = 0;
let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = p + size;

  if (sectionId === 2) {

    const countInfo = readU32Leb(sectionStart);

    importCount = countInfo.value;
  }

  if (sectionId === 10) {

    codeSectionStart = sectionStart;
    codeSectionEnd = sectionEnd;

    break;
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("IMPORT FUNCTIONS :", importCount);

let q = codeSectionStart;

const functionCountInfo = readU32Leb(q);
const functionCount = functionCountInfo.value;
q = functionCountInfo.next;

console.log("CODE FUNCTIONS :", functionCount);
console.log("TOTAL FUNCTIONS:", importCount + functionCount);
console.log("");

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {

  const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(q);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  q = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  bodies.push({
    codeIndex: i,
    functionIndex: importCount + i,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
    size: bodySize
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Trace functions
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

for (const target of TARGET_FUNCTIONS) {

  const codeIndex =
    target - importCount;

  const body = bodies[codeIndex];

  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`FUNC ${target}`);
  console.log("############################################################");

  console.log("CODE INDEX :", codeIndex);

  if (!body) {

    console.log("FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    "BODY :",
    `0x${body.start.toString(16)}`,
    "..",
    `0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
  );

  console.log("SIZE :", body.size);
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  let ip = body.start;

  const localInfo = readU32Leb(ip);
  const localGroups = localInfo.value;
  ip = localInfo.next;

  console.log("LOCAL GROUPS :", localGroups);

  for (let i = 0; i < localGroups; i++) {

    const countInfo = readU32Leb(ip);
    ip = countInfo.next;

    const type = wasm[ip++];

    console.log(
      `  GROUP ${i}: count=${countInfo.value}` +
      ` type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const instructions = [];

  while (ip < body.end) {

    const ins = decodeInstruction(ip);

    instructions.push(ins);

    ip = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b)
      break;
  }

  console.log(
    "INSTRUCTION COUNT :",
    instructions.length
  );

  /*
   * Print ALL instructions.
   *
   * These functions are dependencies of FUNC 6989,
   * so we need the complete bodies rather than only
   * memory hits.
   */

  console.log("");
  console.log("INSTRUCTIONS");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  for (const ins of instructions) {

    console.log(
      `0x${ins.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ${ins.text}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`END FUNC ${target}`);
  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

const fs = require("fs");

const file =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const buf = fs.readFileSync(file);

const IMPORTED = 83;

const TARGET_FUNCS = [
    6989, 6990, 6991, 6992, 6993, 6994, 6995, 6996,
    6997, 6998, 6999, 7000, 7001, 7002, 7003, 7004,
    7005, 7006
];

const STATE_MIN = 0x250f70;
const STATE_MAX = 0x250fe0;

function readULEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * (2 ** shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function hex(n, w = 8) {
    return "0x" +
        (n >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(w, "0");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Find code section
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let pos = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {

    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const s = readULEB(pos);
    const size = s.value;
    pos = s.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Read bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------
let p = codeStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const bodies = new Map();

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    p = sizeInfo.next;

    const func = IMPORTED + i;

    bodies.set(func, {
        func,
        start: p,
        end: p + size,
        size
    });

    p += size;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode relevant instructions
// ------------------------------------------------------------
function decode(p) {

    const op = buf[p];

    if (op === 0x41) {
        const r = readSLEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "i32.const",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x20) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "local.get",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "local.set",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "local.tee",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x23) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "global.get",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x24) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "global.set",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x2c ||
        op === 0x2d) {

        const a = readULEB(p + 1);
        const o = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            name:
                op === 0x28 ? "i32.load" :
                op === 0x2c ? "i32.load8_s" :
                              "i32.load8_u",
            align: a.value,
            offset: o.value,
            next: o.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x36 ||
        op === 0x3a ||
        op === 0x3b) {

        const a = readULEB(p + 1);
        const o = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            name:
                op === 0x36 ? "i32.store" :
                op === 0x3a ? "i32.store8" :
                              "i32.store16",
            align: a.value,
            offset: o.value,
            next: o.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x37) {
        const a = readULEB(p + 1);
        const o = readULEB(a.next);

        return {
            name: "i64.store",
            align: a.value,
            offset: o.value,
            next: o.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "call",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x11) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);
        const t = readULEB(r.next);

        return {
            name: "call_indirect",
            value: r.value,
            type: t.value,
            next: t.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x14) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "call_ref",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x1a) {
        return {
            name: "drop",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6a) {
        return {
            name: "i32.add",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x6b) {
        return {
            name: "i32.sub",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x45) {
        return {
            name: "i32.eqz",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x46) {
        return {
            name: "i32.eq",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x47) {
        return {
            name: "i32.ne",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x48) {
        return {
            name: "i32.lt_s",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x49) {
        return {
            name: "i32.lt_u",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x4a) {
        return {
            name: "i32.gt_s",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x4b) {
        return {
            name: "i32.gt_u",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0b) {
        return {
            name: "end",
            next: p + 1
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x02) {
        return {
            name: "block",
            next: p + 2
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x03) {
        return {
            name: "loop",
            next: p + 2
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x04) {
        return {
            name: "if",
            next: p + 2
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0c) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "br",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    if (op === 0x0d) {
        const r = readULEB(p + 1);

        return {
            name: "br_if",
            value: r.value,
            next: r.next
        };
    }

    return {
        name: "opcode " + hex(op, 2),
        next: p + 1
    };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Main
// ------------------------------------------------------------
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE FAMILY 6989..7006");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(
    `STATE RANGE : ${hex(STATE_MIN)} .. ${hex(STATE_MAX)}`
);
console.log(
    "FOCUS       : 0x250F9C / 0x250FA0 / 0x250FA8"
);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const func of TARGET_FUNCS) {

    const body = bodies.get(func);

    if (!body) {
        console.log(`FUNC ${func} : NOT FOUND`);
        continue;
    }

    console.log("\n############################################################");
    console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
    console.log("############################################################");

    console.log(
        `BODY : ${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end)}`
    );

    let p = body.start;

    while (p < body.end) {

        const d = decode(p);

        let important = false;

        if (
            d.name === "i32.const" &&
            d.value >= STATE_MIN &&
            d.value <= STATE_MAX
        ) {
            important = true;
        }

        if (
            d.name === "i32.load" ||
            d.name === "i32.store" ||
            d.name === "i32.store8" ||
            d.name === "i32.store16"
        ) {
            important = true;
        }

        if (
            d.name === "call" &&
            [3315, 3317].includes(d.value)
        ) {
            important = true;
        }

        if (important) {

            let extra = "";

            if (d.value !== undefined)
                extra += ` value=${d.value}`;

            if (d.offset !== undefined)
                extra += ` offset=${d.offset}`;

            if (d.type !== undefined)
                extra += ` type=${d.type}`;

            console.log(
                `${hex(p)}  ${d.name}${extra}`
            );
        }

        if (d.next <= p)
            p++;
        else
            p = d.next;
    }
}

console.log("\n============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceStateFamilyWriters.mjs
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const FILE = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TARGET STATE FAMILY
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const TARGETS = [
  0x195348, // S0
  0x19534c, // S1
  0x195350, // S2
  0x195354, // S3
  0x195358, // S4
  0x19535c, // S5
  0x195360  // S6
];

const TARGET_END = 0x195360;

function hx(v, n = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(n, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function isTarget(addr) {
  return TARGETS.includes(addr >>> 0);
}

function targetName(addr) {
  const i = TARGETS.indexOf(addr >>> 0);
  return i >= 0 ? `S${i}` : "?";
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Locate WASM sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function readSectionHeader(pos) {
  const id = b[pos++];

  const size = readULEB(pos);
  pos = size.next;

  return {
    id,
    payloadStart: pos,
    payloadEnd: pos + size.value,
    next: pos + size.value
  };
}

let p = 8;

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

let dataStart = -1;
let dataEnd = -1;

while (p < b.length) {
  const s = readSectionHeader(p);

  if (s.id === 10) {
    codeStart = s.payloadStart;
    codeEnd = s.payloadEnd;
  }

  if (s.id === 11) {
    dataStart = s.payloadStart;
    dataEnd = s.payloadEnd;
  }

  p = s.next;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// CODE SECTION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

if (codeStart < 0) {
  throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

let q = codeStart;

const functionCount = readULEB(q);
q = functionCount.next;

const functions = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < functionCount.value; ordinal++) {
  const bodySize = readULEB(q);
  q = bodySize.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize.value;

  functions.push({
    ordinal,
    globalIndex: ordinal + 83,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
    size: bodySize.value
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Exact direct address byte pattern
//
// i32.const TARGET
// TARGET is encoded as ULEB.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function constEncoding(value) {
  const out = [];

  let x = value >>> 0;

  while (true) {
    let byte = x & 0x7f;
    x >>>= 7;

    if (x !== 0)
      byte |= 0x80;

    out.push(byte);

    if (x === 0)
      break;
  }

  return out;
}

function matchAt(pos, pattern) {
  if (pos + pattern.length > b.length)
    return false;

  for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
    if (b[pos + i] !== pattern[i])
      return false;
  }

  return true;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// SEARCH
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE FAMILY WRITER TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET FAMILY:");
console.log("S0 = 0x195348");
console.log("S1 = 0x19534C");
console.log("S2 = 0x195350");
console.log("S3 = 0x195354");
console.log("S4 = 0x195358");
console.log("S5 = 0x19535C");
console.log("S6 = 0x195360");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

const directConstHits = [];

for (const fn of functions) {
  for (let pos = fn.start; pos < fn.end; pos++) {

    // i32.const
    if (b[pos] !== 0x41)
      continue;

    const r = readULEB(pos + 1);

    if (!isTarget(r.value))
      continue;

    directConstHits.push({
      fn,
      at: pos,
      value: r.value,
      next: r.next
    });
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("DIRECT i32.CONST TARGET HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

if (directConstHits.length === 0) {
  console.log("NONE");
} else {
  for (const h of directConstHits) {
    console.log(
      `${targetName(h.value)} ${hx(h.value)} ` +
      `FUNC ${h.fn.globalIndex} ` +
      `@ ${hx(h.at)}`
    );
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Search for direct stores whose address operand is an immediate
// target address:
//
// i32.const TARGET
// ... 
// i32.store
//
// We do not assume arbitrary stack depth. We only report when
// the target constant is immediately before the store sequence
// or appears within a short local window.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("DIRECT STORE CANDIDATES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let storeCandidates = 0;

for (const fn of functions) {

  for (let pos = fn.start; pos < fn.end; pos++) {

    if (b[pos] !== 0x36)
      continue;

    const align = readULEB(pos + 1);
    const off = readULEB(align.next);

    const storeEnd = off.next;

    // Look backward up to 32 bytes for i32.const TARGET.
    const from = Math.max(fn.start, pos - 32);

    for (let x = from; x < pos; x++) {

      if (b[x] !== 0x41)
        continue;

      const r = readULEB(x + 1);

      if (!isTarget(r.value))
        continue;

      const distance = pos - r.next;

      if (distance <= 32) {

        const effective =
          (r.value + off.value) >>> 0;

        console.log(
          `FUNC ${fn.globalIndex} ` +
          `STORE @ ${hx(pos)} ` +
          `CONST @ ${hx(x)} ` +
          `base=${targetName(r.value)}(${hx(r.value)}) ` +
          `offset=${off.value} ` +
          `EA=${hx(effective)}`
        );

        if (isTarget(effective)) {
          console.log(
            `       *** TARGET STORE: ${targetName(effective)} ***`
          );
        }

        storeCandidates++;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}

if (storeCandidates === 0)
  console.log("NONE");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Scan for exact raw target address bytes.
//
// This catches address materialization even when our opcode
// interpretation is not enough.
//
// Each target is searched as:
// little-endian 32-bit bytes.
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("RAW 32-BIT TARGET ADDRESS HITS");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let rawHits = 0;

for (const target of TARGETS) {

  const pattern = [
    target & 0xff,
    (target >>> 8) & 0xff,
    (target >>> 16) & 0xff,
    (target >>> 24) & 0xff
  ];

  for (let pos = 0; pos <= b.length - 4; pos++) {

    if (!matchAt(pos, pattern))
      continue;

    console.log(
      `${targetName(target)} ${hx(target)} ` +
      `RAW HIT @ file ${hx(pos)}`
    );

    rawHits++;
  }
}

if (rawHits === 0)
  console.log("NONE");

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// DATA SEGMENT confirmation
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("");
console.log("DATA SEGMENT TARGET INITIALIZATION");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let dataHits = 0;

if (dataStart >= 0) {

  for (let pos = dataStart; pos < dataEnd; pos++) {

    for (const target of TARGETS) {

      const pattern = [
        target & 0xff,
        (target >>> 8) & 0xff,
        (target >>> 16) & 0xff,
        (target >>> 24) & 0xff
      ];

      if (matchAt(pos, pattern)) {

        console.log(
          `${targetName(target)} ${hx(target)} ` +
          `DATA RAW @ ${hx(pos)}`
        );

        dataHits++;
      }
    }
  }
}

if (dataHits === 0)
  console.log("NONE");

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`FUNCTIONS SCANNED : ${functions.length}`);
console.log(`DIRECT CONST HITS : ${directConstHits.length}`);
console.log(`STORE CANDIDATES  : ${storeCandidates}`);
console.log(`RAW ADDRESS HITS  : ${rawHits}`);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceStateFunctions.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTIONS = [6989, 6991];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("STATE FUNCTION CONTEXT TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGETS   : FUNC 6989 / FUNC 6991");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

function readU32Leb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return {
    value: value >>> 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function readI32Leb(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
      break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return {
    value: value | 0,
    next: p
  };
}

function opcodeName(op) {
  const names = {
    0x00: "unreachable",
    0x01: "nop",
    0x02: "block",
    0x03: "loop",
    0x04: "if",
    0x05: "else",
    0x0b: "end",

    0x10: "call",

    0x1a: "drop",

    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",

    0x23: "global.get",
    0x24: "global.set",

    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x29: "i64.load",

    0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",

    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x37: "i64.store",
    0x3a: "i32.store8",

    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",

    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x6d: "i32.div_s",
    0x6e: "i32.div_u",

    0x71: "i32.and",
    0x72: "i32.or",
    0x73: "i32.xor"
  };

  return names[op] ||
    `0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function decodeInstruction(pos) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  let text = opcodeName(op);

  /*
   * Memory instructions
   */
  if (
    op === 0x28 ||
    op === 0x29 ||
    op === 0x2c ||
    op === 0x2d ||
    op === 0x36 ||
    op === 0x37 ||
    op === 0x3a
  ) {
    const align = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = align.next;

    const offset = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = offset.next;

    text +=
      ` align=${align.value}` +
      ` offset=0x${offset.value.toString(16)}`;
  }

  /*
   * Constants
   */
  else if (op === 0x41) {
    const imm = readI32Leb(pos);
    pos = imm.next;

    text +=
      ` ${imm.value}` +
      ` (0x${(imm.value >>> 0).toString(16)})`;
  }

  /*
   * locals
   */
  else if (
    op === 0x20 ||
    op === 0x21 ||
    op === 0x22
  ) {
    const imm = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = imm.next;

    text += ` local=${imm.value}`;
  }

  /*
   * call
   */
  else if (op === 0x10) {
    const imm = readU32Leb(pos);
    pos = imm.next;

    text += ` func=${imm.value}`;
  }

  /*
   * block / loop / if
   * blocktype is one byte for the functions we're tracing.
   */
  else if (
    op === 0x02 ||
    op === 0x03 ||
    op === 0x04
  ) {
    const blockType = wasm[pos++];

    text +=
      ` blocktype=0x${blockType.toString(16)}`;
  }

  return {
    start,
    end: pos,
    op,
    text
  };
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Locate sections
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let p = 8;

let importCount = 0;
let codeSectionStart = -1;
let codeSectionEnd = -1;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const sectionId = wasm[p++];

  const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(p);
  const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
  p = sizeInfo.next;

  const sectionStart = p;
  const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

  /*
   * IMPORT SECTION
   */
  if (sectionId === 2) {

    let q = sectionStart;

    const countInfo = readU32Leb(q);
    importCount = countInfo.value;
  }

  /*
   * CODE SECTION
   */
  if (sectionId === 10) {
    codeSectionStart = sectionStart;
    codeSectionEnd = sectionEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

if (codeSectionStart < 0)
  throw new Error("CODE section not found");

console.log("IMPORT FUNCTIONS :", importCount);

console.log(
  "CODE SECTION :",
  `0x${codeSectionStart.toString(16)}`,
  "..",
  `0x${codeSectionEnd.toString(16)}`
);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Parse CODE section
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let q = codeSectionStart;

const functionCountInfo = readU32Leb(q);
const functionCount = functionCountInfo.value;
q = functionCountInfo.next;

console.log("CODE FUNCTIONS :", functionCount);
console.log(
  "TOTAL FUNCTIONS:",
  importCount + functionCount
);
console.log("");

const bodies = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount; i++) {

  const sizeInfo = readU32Leb(q);
  const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
  q = sizeInfo.next;

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

  /*
   * IMPORTANT:
   * Actual function index includes imported functions.
   */
  const actualFunctionIndex =
    importCount + i;

  bodies.push({
    codeIndex: i,
    functionIndex: actualFunctionIndex,
    start: bodyStart,
    end: bodyEnd,
    size: bodySize
  });

  q = bodyEnd;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * Trace requested functions
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

for (const target of TARGET_FUNCTIONS) {

  const codeIndex =
    target - importCount;

  const body = bodies[codeIndex];

  console.log("############################################################");
  console.log(`FUNC ${target}`);
  console.log("############################################################");

  console.log(
    "CODE INDEX :",
    codeIndex
  );

  if (!body) {
    console.log("FUNCTION BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  console.log(
    "BODY :",
    `0x${body.start.toString(16)}`,
    "..",
    `0x${body.end.toString(16)}`
  );

  console.log(
    "SIZE :",
    body.size
  );

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  /*
   * Decode locals.
   */

  let ip = body.start;

  const localCountInfo = readU32Leb(ip);
  const localCount = localCountInfo.value;
  ip = localCountInfo.next;

  console.log("LOCAL GROUPS :", localCount);

  for (let i = 0; i < localCount; i++) {

    const countInfo = readU32Leb(ip);
    ip = countInfo.next;

    const type = wasm[ip++];

    console.log(
      `  LOCAL GROUP ${i}:` +
      ` count=${countInfo.value}` +
      ` type=0x${type.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  const instructions = [];

  while (ip < body.end) {

    const ins = decodeInstruction(ip);

    instructions.push(ins);

    ip = ins.end;

    if (ins.op === 0x0b)
      break;
  }

  console.log(
    "INSTRUCTION COUNT :",
    instructions.length
  );

  /*
   * ----------------------------------------------------------
   * Find state-related instructions
   * ----------------------------------------------------------
   */

  for (let i = 0; i < instructions.length; i++) {

    const ins = instructions[i];

    let interesting = false;

    /*
     * Memory access.
     */
    if (
      ins.op === 0x28 ||
      ins.op === 0x29 ||
      ins.op === 0x2c ||
      ins.op === 0x2d ||
      ins.op === 0x36 ||
      ins.op === 0x37 ||
      ins.op === 0x3a
    ) {
      interesting = true;
    }

    /*
     * Constants near state-address construction.
     */
    if (ins.op === 0x41) {

      const m = ins.text.match(
        /i32\.const\s+(-?\d+)\s+\(0x([0-9a-f]+)\)/
      );

      if (m) {

        const value = Number(m[1]);

        if (
          value === 0x250fb4 ||
          value === 0x250fc0 ||
          value === 0x250fa0 ||
          value === 0x250f9c ||
          value === 0x250fd8
        ) {
          interesting = true;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!interesting)
      continue;

    console.log("");
    console.log(
      `******** CONTEXT @ 0x${ins.start.toString(16)} ********`
    );

    const from = Math.max(0, i - 10);
    const to = Math.min(
      instructions.length,
      i + 11
    );

    for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {

      const x = instructions[j];

      const marker =
        j === i ? " >>> " : "     ";

      console.log(
        marker +
        `0x${x.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}  ` +
        x.text
      );
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(`END FUNC ${target}`);
  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceStaticRootsToTargets.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const SEG412_START = 0x0018744c;
const SEG412_END   = 0x0018ab8d;

const SEG413_START = 0x0018ab98;
const SEG413_END   = 0x0018f512;

const ROOTS = [
  0x0018ac44,
  0x0018ae54,
];

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
  [2053, "Function 8952"],
  [2101, "Function 8927"],
  [2612, "Function 8637"],
]);

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function u32(pos: number): number {
  if (pos < 0 || pos + 4 > buf.length) return 0;
  return buf.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function hex(n: number): string {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function isSeg412(v: number): boolean {
  return v >= SEG412_START && v <= SEG412_END;
}

function isSeg413(v: number): boolean {
  return v >= SEG413_START && v <= SEG413_END;
}

function fileOffsetForMemory(mem: number): number | null {
  // Locate the memory address by scanning known data payloads.
  // This is deliberately independent of segment-number assumptions.
  let p = 8;

  while (p < buf.length) {
    const id = buf[p];
    p++;

    let shift = 0;
    let size = 0;

    while (true) {
      const b = buf[p++];
      size |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
      if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
      shift += 7;
    }

    const payload = p;
    const end = payload + size;

    if (id === 11 && end <= buf.length) {
      let q = payload;

      const countStart = q;
      let b = buf[q++];
      let count = b & 0x7f;
      let s = 7;

      while (b & 0x80) {
        b = buf[q++];
        count |= (b & 0x7f) << s;
        s += 7;
      }

      for (let seg = 0; seg < count; seg++) {
        let flags = buf[q++];

        let memAddr = 0;

        if (flags === 0) {
          // active, memory 0, offset expression
          const opcode = buf[q++];
          if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            // Skip unsupported offset expression conservatively.
            break;
          }

          let val = 0;
          let sh = 0;
          let x = buf[q++];

          while (true) {
            val |= (x & 0x7f) << sh;
            if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;
            sh += 7;
            x = buf[q++];
          }

          memAddr = val >>> 0;

          if (buf[q++] !== 0x0b) break;
        } else if (flags === 2) {
          // active, explicit memory index
          let sh = 0;
          let memidx = 0;
          let x = buf[q++];

          while (true) {
            memidx |= (x & 0x7f) << sh;
            if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;
            sh += 7;
            x = buf[q++];
          }

          const opcode = buf[q++];
          if (opcode !== 0x41) break;

          let val = 0;
          sh = 0;
          x = buf[q++];

          while (true) {
            val |= (x & 0x7f) << sh;
            if ((x & 0x80) === 0) break;
            sh += 7;
            x = buf[q++];
          }

          memAddr = val >>> 0;

          if (buf[q++] !== 0x0b) break;
        } else {
          // Passive / unsupported segment form.
          break;
        }

        let len = 0;
        let sh2 = 0;
        let x2 = buf[q++];

        while (true) {
          len |= (x2 & 0x7f) << sh2;
          if ((x2 & 0x80) === 0) break;
          sh2 += 7;
          x2 = buf[q++];
        }

        if (mem >= memAddr && mem < memAddr + len) {
          return q + (mem - memAddr);
        }

        q += len;
      }
    }

    p = end;
  }

  return null;
}

function inspectRoot(root: number) {
  const file = fileOffsetForMemory(root);

  console.log("");
  console.log(`ROOT ${hex(root)}`);

  if (file === null) {
    console.log("  FILE OFFSET = NOT FOUND");
    return;
  }

  console.log(`  FILE OFFSET = ${hex(file)}`);
  console.log(`  REGION      = ${isSeg412(root) ? "SEGMENT 412" : isSeg413(root) ? "SEGMENT 413" : "OTHER"}`);

  const seen = new Set<number>();
  const queue: Array<{ addr: number; depth: number; path: string }> = [
    { addr: root, depth: 0, path: hex(root) }
  ];

  const MAX_DEPTH = 4;
  const MAX_NODES = 120;

  let nodes = 0;
  let targetHits = 0;

  console.log("");
  console.log("GRAPH");

  while (queue.length && nodes < MAX_NODES) {
    const item = queue.shift()!;
    const { addr, depth, path } = item;

    if (seen.has(addr)) continue;
    seen.add(addr);
    nodes++;

    const f = fileOffsetForMemory(addr);
    if (f === null) continue;

    console.log(
      `depth=${depth} ${hex(addr)} file=${hex(f)}`
    );

    // Inspect 32-bit words in the record.
    for (let off = 0; off < 0x80; off += 4) {
      const value = u32(f + off);

      if (value === 2053 || value === 2101 || value === 2612) {
        const name = TARGETS.get(value)!;
        console.log(
          `  >>> TARGET ${value} (${hex(value)}) @ ${hex(addr + off)} -> ${name}`
        );
        targetHits++;
      }

      if (isSeg412(value) || isSeg413(value)) {
        console.log(
          `  ptr +${off.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")} : ${hex(value)} -> ${
            isSeg412(value) ? "SEG412" : "SEG413"
          }`
        );

        if (depth < MAX_DEPTH && !seen.has(value)) {
          queue.push({
            addr: value,
            depth: depth + 1,
            path: `${path} -> ${hex(value)}`
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("");
  console.log(`NODES VISITED = ${nodes}`);
  console.log(`TARGET HITS   = ${targetHits}`);
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" STATIC ROOT → DISPATCH TARGET TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size = ${buf.length}`);
console.log(`Segment 412 = ${hex(SEG412_START)} .. ${hex(SEG412_END)}`);
console.log(`Segment 413 = ${hex(SEG413_START)} .. ${hex(SEG413_END)}`);

for (const root of ROOTS) {
  inspectRoot(root);
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT TARGET ADDRESS SCAN");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const [index, fn] of TARGETS) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(`TARGET INDEX ${index} -> ${fn}`);

  const hits: number[] = [];

  for (let pos = 0; pos + 4 <= buf.length; pos += 4) {
    if (u32(pos) === index) hits.push(pos);
  }

  console.log(`LE32 ALIGNED HITS = ${hits.length}`);

  for (const pos of hits) {
    const mem =
      SEG412_START <= SEG412_END
        ? SEG412_START + (pos - 0)
        : 0;

    console.log(`  file=${hex(pos)}`);
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceStoreAddress1953.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_START = 0x195350;
const TARGET_END   = 0x195358;

const STORE_OPS = new Set([
    0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x39,
    0x3a, 0x3b, 0x3c, 0x3d, 0x3e
]);

function uleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= ~0 << shift;
    }

    return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(v: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function section(id: number) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sid = wasm[p++];

        const size = uleb(p);
        p = size.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size.value
            };
        }

        p += size.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

// ============================================================
// FUNCTION / CODE
// ============================================================

const funcSec = section(3);

let fp = funcSec.start;

const fc = uleb(fp);
fp = fc.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
    const x = uleb(fp);
    fp = x.next;
    typeIndices.push(x.value);
}

const codeSec = section(10);

let cp = codeSec.start;

const bc = uleb(cp);
cp = bc.next;

const IMPORTED = 83;

interface Body {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    instrStart: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bc.value; ordinal++) {

    const size = uleb(cp);
    cp = size.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size.value;

    let p = start;

    const locals = uleb(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {
        const count = uleb(p);
        p = count.next;
        p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED + ordinal,
        start,
        end,
        instrStart: p
    });

    cp = end;
}

// ============================================================
// ABSTRACT VALUE TRACKING
//
// Track simple stack values:
//   CONST(number)
//   LOCAL(n)
//   GLOBAL(n)
//   UNKNOWN
//
// Track local.set / local.tee.
// For memory addresses, recognize:
//   i32.const ADDRESS
//   local.get + i32.const offset + i32.add
//   local.get + i32.const offset + i32.sub
//   known CONST + arithmetic
//
// This is deliberately conservative.
// ============================================================

type Value =
    | { kind: "const"; value: number }
    | { kind: "local"; index: number }
    | { kind: "global"; index: number }
    | { kind: "unknown" };

function valString(v: Value): string {

    if (v.kind === "const") {
        return `CONST(${hex(v.value)})`;
    }

    if (v.kind === "local") {
        return `LOCAL(${v.index})`;
    }

    if (v.kind === "global") {
        return `GLOBAL(${v.index})`;
    }

    return "UNKNOWN";
}

function sameTarget(v: Value) {

    if (v.kind !== "const") {
        return false;
    }

    return (
        v.value >= TARGET_START &&
        v.value < TARGET_END
    );
}

function addValues(a: Value, b: Value): Value {

    if (a.kind === "const" && b.kind === "const") {
        return {
            kind: "const",
            value: (a.value + b.value) >>> 0
        };
    }

    return { kind: "unknown" };
}

// ============================================================
// SCAN
// ============================================================

console.log("TRACE COMPUTED STORE ADDRESSES");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

console.log(
    `TARGET RANGE = ${hex(TARGET_START)} .. ${hex(TARGET_END - 1)}`
);

console.log("");

let exact = 0;
let potential = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

    let p = body.instrStart;

    const stack: Value[] = [];
    const locals = new Map<number, Value>();

    function pop(): Value {
        return stack.length
            ? stack.pop()!
            : { kind: "unknown" };
    }

    function push(v: Value) {
        stack.push(v);
    }

    while (p < body.end) {

        const opOffset = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CONSTANTS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {

            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            push({
                kind: "const",
                value: x.value >>> 0
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {

            const x = sleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            push({ kind: "unknown" });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x43) {
            p += 4;
            push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x44) {
            p += 8;
            push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // LOCALS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x20) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            push(
                locals.get(x.value) ?? {
                    kind: "local",
                    index: x.value
                }
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            locals.set(x.value, pop());

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            const v = stack.length
                ? stack[stack.length - 1]
                : { kind: "unknown" };

            locals.set(x.value, v);

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // GLOBALS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x23) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            push({
                kind: "global",
                index: x.value
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x24) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            pop();

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // INTEGER ADD
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x6a) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            push(addValues(a, b));

            continue;
        }

        // i32.sub
        if (op === 0x6b) {

            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            if (
                a.kind === "const" &&
                b.kind === "const"
            ) {
                push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value: (a.value - b.value) >>> 0
                });
            } else {
                push({ kind: "unknown" });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // LOAD
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {

            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = uleb(p);
            p = off.next;

            const address = pop();

            if (sameTarget(address)) {

                console.log("");
                console.log("!!! TARGET LOAD !!!");
                console.log(
                    `FUNC   = ${body.func}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `OFFSET = ${hex(opOffset)}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `ADDR   = ${valString(address)}`
                );
            }

            push({ kind: "unknown" });

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // STORE
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (STORE_OPS.has(op)) {

            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = uleb(p);
            p = off.next;

            const value = pop();
            const address = pop();

            let target = false;

            if (sameTarget(address)) {
                target = true;
                exact++;

                console.log("");
                console.log("============================================================");
                console.log("EXACT TARGET STORE");
                console.log("============================================================");

                console.log(
                    `FUNC       = ${body.func}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `STORE      = ${hex(opOffset)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `ADDRESS    = ${valString(address)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `IMMEDIATE  = ${hex(off.value)}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `VALUE      = ${valString(value)}`
                );

                console.log("");
            }

            // Potential pointer store:
            // local/global/unknown address with immediate offset
            // near the target region.
            if (
                !target &&
                (
                    address.kind === "local" ||
                    address.kind === "global" ||
                    address.kind === "unknown"
                ) &&
                off.value <= TARGET_END
            ) {
                potential++;
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CALL
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x10) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            // We cannot know exact signature here,
            // so conservatively clear stack.
            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // CALL INDIRECT
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x11) {

            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            p++;

            stack.length = 0;

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // DROP
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x1a) {
            pop();
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // SELECT
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x1b) {

            const condition = pop();
            const b = pop();
            const a = pop();

            if (
                a.kind === "const" &&
                b.kind === "const" &&
                a.value === b.value
            ) {
                push(a);
            } else {
                push({ kind: "unknown" });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // BRANCHES / BLOCKS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04 ||
            op === 0x06
        ) {
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const n = uleb(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {
                const x = uleb(p);
                p = x.next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // MEMORY SIZE/GROW
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
            p++;
            push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------------
        // COMMON INTEGER OPS
        // ----------------------------------------------------

        if (
            op === 0x45 || // eqz
            op === 0x46 || // eq
            op === 0x47 || // ne
            op === 0x48 || // lt_s
            op === 0x49 || // lt_u
            op === 0x4a || // gt_s
            op === 0x4b || // gt_u
            op === 0x4c || // le_s
            op === 0x4d || // le_u
            op === 0x4e || // ge_s
            op === 0x4f    // ge_u
        ) {
            if (op === 0x45) {
                pop();
            } else {
                pop();
                pop();
            }

            push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        // Most unary/binary numeric operators:
        // conservatively preserve stack shape.
        if (
            op >= 0x67 &&
            op <= 0x78
        ) {
            pop();
            push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op >= 0x6a &&
            op <= 0xc4
        ) {
            pop();
            pop();
            push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }
    }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL RESULT");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(
    `EXACT STORES TO 0x195350..0x195357 = ${exact}`
);

console.log(
    `POTENTIAL COMPUTED POINTER STORES   = ${potential}`
);

console.log("");

if (exact === 0) {

    console.log(
        "NO EXACT CONSTANT-RESOLVED STORE FOUND."
    );

    console.log(
        "This does NOT yet prove the cells are immutable."
    );

    console.log(
        "Computed-pointer dataflow may require caller-sensitive analysis."
    );
}

if (exact > 0) {

    console.log(
        "RUNTIME WRITE PATH EXISTS."
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

FILE: scripts\traceStores4789_4790.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const TARGET_VALUES = new Set([4789, 4790]);
const TARGET_ADDRS = new Set([0x195350, 0x195354]);

const STORE_OPS = new Set([
    0x36, // i32.store
    0x37, // i64.store
    0x38, // f32.store
    0x39, // f64.store
    0x3a, // i32.store8
    0x3b, // i32.store16
    0x3c, // i64.store8
    0x3d, // i64.store16
    0x3e  // i64.store32
]);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: p
    };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function bytes(start: number, end: number) {
    return Array.from(wasm.subarray(start, end))
        .map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
        .join(" ");
}

function section(id: number) {

    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {

        const sid = wasm[p++];

        const size = readULEB(p);
        p = size.next;

        if (sid === id) {
            return {
                start: p,
                end: p + size.value
            };
        }

        p += size.value;
    }

    throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

// ============================================================
// FUNCTION + CODE SECTIONS
// ============================================================

const funcSec = section(3);

let fp = funcSec.start;

const functionCount = readULEB(fp);
fp = functionCount.next;

const typeIndices: number[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < functionCount.value; i++) {

    const x = readULEB(fp);
    fp = x.next;

    typeIndices.push(x.value);
}

const codeSec = section(10);

let cp = codeSec.start;

const bodyCount = readULEB(cp);
cp = bodyCount.next;

const IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS = 83;

interface Body {
    func: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    instrStart: number;
}

const bodies: Body[] = [];

for (let ordinal = 0; ordinal < bodyCount.value; ordinal++) {

    const size = readULEB(cp);
    cp = size.next;

    const start = cp;
    const end = cp + size.value;

    let p = start;

    const locals = readULEB(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < locals.value; i++) {

        const count = readULEB(p);
        p = count.next;

        p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
        func: IMPORTED_FUNCTIONS + ordinal,
        start,
        end,
        instrStart: p
    });

    cp = end;
}

// ============================================================
// RESULT TYPES
// ============================================================

interface ConstRef {
    func: number;
    offset: number;
    value: number;
}

interface StoreRef {
    func: number;
    offset: number;
    opcode: number;
    offsetImmediate: number;
}

const constRefs: ConstRef[] = [];
const stores: StoreRef[] = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

    let p = body.instrStart;

    while (p < body.end) {

        const opOffset = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        // i32.const
        if (op === 0x41) {

            const x = readSLEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            const value = x.value >>> 0;

            if (TARGET_VALUES.has(value)) {

                constRefs.push({
                    func: body.func,
                    offset: opOffset,
                    value
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        // i64.const
        if (op === 0x42) {

            const x = readSLEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            continue;
        }

        // f32.const
        if (op === 0x43) {
            p += 4;
            continue;
        }

        // f64.const
        if (op === 0x44) {
            p += 8;
            continue;
        }

        // local.get / set / tee
        if (
            op === 0x20 ||
            op === 0x21 ||
            op === 0x22
        ) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // global.get / set
        if (
            op === 0x23 ||
            op === 0x24
        ) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // branch
        if (
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d
        ) {
            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;
            continue;
        }

        // br_table
        if (op === 0x0e) {

            const n = readULEB(p);
            p = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value + 1; i++) {

                const x = readULEB(p);
                p = x.next;
            }

            continue;
        }

        // block / loop / if / try-like structured opcodes
        if (
            op === 0x02 ||
            op === 0x03 ||
            op === 0x04 ||
            op === 0x06
        ) {
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        // memory size/grow
        if (
            op === 0x3f ||
            op === 0x40
        ) {
            p++;
            continue;
        }

        // load instructions
        if (
            op >= 0x28 &&
            op <= 0x35
        ) {

            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            continue;
        }

        // STORE instructions
        if (STORE_OPS.has(op)) {

            const align = readULEB(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            stores.push({
                func: body.func,
                offset: opOffset,
                opcode: op,
                offsetImmediate: off.value
            });

            continue;
        }

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {

            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {

            const x = readULEB(p);
            p = x.next;

            p++;

            continue;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// OPCODE NAMES
// ============================================================

function storeName(op: number) {

    switch (op) {

        case 0x36:
            return "i32.store";

        case 0x37:
            return "i64.store";

        case 0x38:
            return "f32.store";

        case 0x39:
            return "f64.store";

        case 0x3a:
            return "i32.store8";

        case 0x3b:
            return "i32.store16";

        case 0x3c:
            return "i64.store8";

        case 0x3d:
            return "i64.store16";

        case 0x3e:
            return "i64.store32";

        default:
            return "STORE";
    }
}

// ============================================================

console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${bodies.length}`);

let nearMatches = 0;

for (const c of constRefs) {

    const candidates = stores.filter(s =>
        s.func === c.func &&
        s.offset > c.offset &&
        s.offset <= c.offset + 40
    );

    if (candidates.length === 0) {
        continue;
    }

    for (const s of candidates) {

        nearMatches++;

        console.log(

        );

        console.log(

        );

        );

        console.log(

        );

        const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === c.func);

        if (body) {

            const start = Math.max(
                body.instrStart,
                c.offset - 16
            );

            const end = Math.min(
                body.end,
                s.offset + 12
            );

            console.log(
                `  BYTES = ${bytes(start, end)}`
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }
}

// CONST + ADDRESS COMBINATION
// ============================================================

console.log("VALUE + ADDRESS COMBINATION SEARCH");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let combo = 0;

for (const body of bodies) {

    const start = body.instrStart;
    const end = body.end;

    for (let p = start; p < end; p++) {

        const op = wasm[p];

        if (op !== 0x41) {
            continue;
        }

        const c = readSLEB(p + 1);

        if (!TARGET_VALUES.has(c.value >>> 0)) {
            continue;
        }

        for (
            let q = c.next;
            q < Math.min(end, c.next + 40);
            q++
        ) {

            const sop = wasm[q];

            if (!STORE_OPS.has(sop)) {
                continue;
            }

            let x = q + 1;

            const align = readULEB(x);
            x = align.next;

            const off = readULEB(x);

            console.log(
                `FUNC ${body.func}: CONST ${c.value} @ ${hex(p)} -> ` +
                `${storeName(sop)} @ ${hex(q)} ` +
                `OFFSET=${hex(off.value)}`
            );

            combo++;
            break;
        }
    }
}

console.log("");

console.log(
    `VALUE->STORE COMBINATION MATCHES = ${combo}`
);

console.log("");

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    const body = bodies.find(x => x.func === c.func);

    if (!body) continue;

    const start = Math.max(
        body.instrStart,
        c.offset - 12
    );

    const end = Math.min(
        body.end,
        c.offset + 60
    );

    console.log(
        `FUNC ${c.func} TARGET=${c.value} @ ${hex(c.offset)}`
    );

    console.log(
        `  ${bytes(start, end)}`
    );

    console.log("");
}

// ============================================================

if (nearMatches > 0) {

    console.log(

    );

} else {

    console.log(

    );

} else {

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNC = 8952;
const TARGET_SLOT = 2053;

const b = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readU32(pos) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, p = pos;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << s;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: p };
    s += 7;
  }
}

function readVec(pos) {
  const r = readU32(pos);
  return { count: r.value, next: r.next };
}

function readSection(pos) {
  const id = b[pos++];
  const sz = readU32(pos);
  return {
    id,
    start: pos,
    size: sz.value,
    end: pos + sz.value
  };
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE TABLE 2053 -> FUNC 8952");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("WASM SIZE :", b.length);
console.log("TARGET SLOT :", TARGET_SLOT);
console.log("TARGET FUNC :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let pos = 8;
let elementStart = null;
let elementEnd = null;

while (pos < b.length) {
  const s = readSection(pos);
  if (s.id === 9) {
    elementStart = s.start;
    elementEnd = s.end;
    break;
  }
  pos = s.end;
}

if (elementStart === null) {
  throw new Error("Element section not found");
}

console.log(
  "ELEMENT SECTION :",
  "0x" + elementStart.toString(16),
  "..",
  "0x" + elementEnd.toString(16)
);

let p = elementStart;
const segCount = readU32(p);
p = segCount.next;

console.log("SEGMENT COUNT :", segCount.value);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let found = false;

for (let seg = 0; seg < segCount.value; seg++) {
  const flags = readU32(p);
  p = flags.next;

  if (flags.value !== 0) {
    console.log(
      "SEGMENT",
      seg,
      "FLAGS",
      flags.value,
      "unsupported for this exact scanner"
    );
    break;
  }

  const opcode = b[p++];
  if (opcode !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error(
      `Unexpected offset opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  const off = readU32(p);
  p = off.next;

  if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Expected end opcode after table offset");
  }

  const n = readU32(p);
  p = n.next;

  console.log(
    `SEGMENT ${seg}: OFFSET=${off.value} COUNT=${n.value}`
  );

  const firstIndex = off.value;

  for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
    const func = readU32(p);
    p = func.next;

    const slot = firstIndex + i;

    if (func.value === TARGET_FUNC || slot === TARGET_SLOT) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
      console.log("TARGET RELATION FOUND");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
      console.log("TABLE SLOT :", slot);
      console.log("FUNC INDEX :", func.value);
      console.log("ELEMENT INDEX :", i);
      console.log("OFFSET :", firstIndex);

      if (slot === TARGET_SLOT && func.value === TARGET_FUNC) {
        console.log("STATUS : EXACT MATCH");
        found = true;
      } else if (slot === TARGET_SLOT) {
        console.log("STATUS : SLOT MATCH, DIFFERENT FUNCTION");
      } else {
        console.log("STATUS : FUNCTION MATCH, DIFFERENT SLOT");
      }
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");

if (found) {
  console.log("TABLE[2053] -> FUNC 8952 : CONFIRMED");
} else {
  console.log("EXACT TABLE[2053] -> FUNC 8952 : NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
