const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

const TARGET_TABLE = 4275;
const TARGET_FUNC = 7484;
const TARGET_TYPE = 3;

function readULEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];

    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if (!(b & 0x80))
      break;

    shift += 7;
  }

  return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b;

  do {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;
  } while (b & 0x80);

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
    value |= (~0 << shift);

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function hex(n) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readNameByte(b) {
  const names = {
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",
    0x6c: "i32.mul",
    0x45: "i32.eqz",
    0x46: "i32.eq",
    0x47: "i32.ne",
    0x48: "i32.lt_s",
    0x49: "i32.lt_u",
    0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
    0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
    0x4c: "i32.le_s",
    0x4d: "i32.le_u",
    0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
    0x4f: "i32.ge_u",
    0x28: "i32.load",
    0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
    0x36: "i32.store",
    0x10: "call",
    0x11: "call_indirect"
  };

  return names[b] || ("opcode_" + hex(b));
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * LOCATE CODE SECTION
 * ============================================================
 */

let p = 8;

let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {

  const id = wasm[p++];

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const payloadStart = size.next;
  const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size.value;

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = payloadStart;
    codeEnd = payloadEnd;
    break;
  }

  p = payloadEnd;
}

if (!codeStart)
  throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");

/*
 * ============================================================
 * FUNCTION BODY LOCATOR
 * ============================================================
 */

p = codeStart;

const bodyCount = readULEB(p);

p = bodyCount.next;

const bodies = [];

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount.value; bodyIndex++) {

  const size = readULEB(p);

  const bodySize = size.value;
  const bodySizeStart = size.next;
  const bodyEnd = bodySizeStart + bodySize;

  bodies.push({
    bodyIndex,
    start: bodySizeStart,
    end: bodyEnd
  });

  p = bodyEnd;
}

/*
 * Function index = imported function count + body index.
 * The known module has 83 imports.
 */

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;

/*
 * ============================================================
 * SCAN TYPE-3 CALL_INDIRECT
 * ============================================================
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TRACE TABLE[4275] TYPE-3 DISPATCH");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log("TARGET TABLE INDEX :", TARGET_TABLE);
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION :", TARGET_FUNC);
console.log("TARGET TYPE :", TARGET_TYPE);
console.log("CODE SECTION :", hex(codeStart), "..", hex(codeEnd));
console.log("BODY COUNT :", bodyCount.value);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let totalType3 = 0;
let candidates = [];

for (const body of bodies) {

  const funcIndex = IMPORT_COUNT + body.bodyIndex;

  /*
   * Decode only enough bytes to identify call_indirect.
   *
   * We keep a rolling list of decoded instructions so that
   * the instructions immediately before the call can be shown.
   */

  let q = body.start;

  const localDecl = readULEB(q);
  q = localDecl.next;

  /*
   * Skip local declarations.
   */

  for (let i = 0; i < localDecl.value; i++) {

    const n = readULEB(q);
    q = n.next + 1;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (q < body.end) {

    const off = q;
    const op = wasm[q++];

    let text = readNameByte(op);

    try {

      if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {

        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        text += " local=" + x.value;

      } else if (op === 0x41) {

        const x = readSLEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        text += " " + x.value;

      } else if (op === 0x10) {

        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        text += " func=" + x.value;

      } else if (op === 0x11) {

        const x = readULEB(q);
        q = x.next;

        const table = readULEB(q);
        q = table.next;

        text +=
          " type=" + x.value +
          " table=" + table.value;

        if (x.value === TARGET_TYPE) {

          totalType3++;

          /*
           * Keep this call as a candidate.
           */

          const before = instructions.slice(-14);

          candidates.push({
            funcIndex,
            offset: off,
            before,
            call: text
          });
        }

      } else if (
        op === 0x28 ||
        op === 0x2d
      ) {

        const align = readULEB(q);
        q = align.next;

        const offset = readULEB(q);
        q = offset.next;

        text +=
          " align=" + align.value +
          " offset=" + offset.value;

      } else if (
        op === 0x6a ||
        op === 0x6b ||
        op === 0x6c ||
        op === 0x45 ||
        op === 0x46 ||
        op === 0x47 ||
        op === 0x48 ||
        op === 0x49 ||
        op === 0x4a ||
        op === 0x4b ||
        op === 0x4c ||
        op === 0x4d ||
        op === 0x4e ||
        op === 0x4f
      ) {

        /*
         * no immediate
         */

      } else if (op === 0x0b) {

        text = "end";

        instructions.push({
          off,
          text
        });

        break;
      }

    } catch {

      /*
       * Decoder failure.
       * Stop this body rather than inventing instructions.
       */

      break;
    }

    instructions.push({
      off,
      text
    });
  }
}

/*
 * ============================================================
 * PRINT CANDIDATES
 * ============================================================
 */

console.log("");
console.log("TYPE-3 CALL COUNT :", totalType3);
console.log("CANDIDATES :", candidates.length);

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of candidates) {

  console.log("");
  console.log("FUNC", c.funcIndex);
  console.log("CALL", hex(c.offset));
  console.log("CALL", c.call);

  console.log("PREVIOUS INSTRUCTIONS:");

  for (const x of c.before) {

    console.log(
      "  " + hex(x.off),
      x.text
    );
  }

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceTableSlot114.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGET_FUNCTION = 4154;
const TARGET_SLOT = 0x72;
const TARGET_VALUE = 114;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }

    return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
}

function sleb(buf: Buffer, p: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = buf[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        if (shift > 35) throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (-1 << shift);
    }

    return { value, next: p };
}

function hex(n: number, width = 8) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readName(buf: Buffer, p: number) {
    const r = uleb(buf, p);
    const end = r.next + r.value;
    return {
        name: buf.subarray(r.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end,
    };
}

function parseSections() {
    let p = 8;
    const sections: any[] = [];

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];

        const s = uleb(wasm, p);
        const size = s.value;
        p = s.next;

        const payload = p;
        sections.push({
            id,
            payload,
            size,
            end: payload + size,
        });

        p += size;
    }

    return sections;
}

function parseTypes(sec: any) {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const count = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = count.next;

    const types: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const form = wasm[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(`Unexpected type form ${hex(form, 2)}`);
        }

        const pc = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [...wasm.subarray(p, p + pc.value)];
        p += pc.value;

        const rc = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [...wasm.subarray(p, p + rc.value)];
        p += rc.value;

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseImports(sec: any) {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const count = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = count.next;

    const imports: any[] = [];
    let functionIndex = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const mod = readName(wasm, p);
        p = mod.next;

        const nm = readName(wasm, p);
        p = nm.next;

        const kind = wasm[p++];

        let typeIndex = -1;

        if (kind === 0x00) {
            const t = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = t.next;
            typeIndex = t.value;
            imports.push({
                functionIndex,
                module: mod.name,
                name: nm.name,
                typeIndex,
            });
            functionIndex++;
        } else if (kind === 0x01) {
            p += 4;
        } else if (kind === 0x02) {
            const flags = wasm[p++];
            const min = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = min.next;

            if (flags & 1) {
                const max = uleb(wasm, p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 0x03) {
            p += 2;
        } else if (kind === 0x04) {
            p++;
            const t = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = t.next;
        } else {
            throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind}`);
        }
    }

    return imports;
}

function parseFunctions(sec: any) {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const count = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = count.next;

    const types: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const t = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = t.next;
        types.push(t.value);
    }

    return types;
}

function parseCode(sec: any) {
    let p = sec.payload;

    const count = uleb(wasm, p);
    p = count.next;

    const bodies: any[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const sizeInfo = uleb(wasm, p);
        const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const bodyStart = p;
        const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

        const localCountInfo = uleb(wasm, p);
        p = localCountInfo.next;

        for (let j = 0; j < localCountInfo.value; j++) {
            const n = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = n.next;
            p += 1;
        }

        const instrStart = p;

        bodies.push({
            functionIndex: 83 + i,
            bodyStart,
            bodyEnd,
            instrStart,
            instrEnd: bodyEnd,
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return bodies;
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
    const names: Record<number, string> = {
        0x00: "unreachable",
        0x01: "nop",
        0x02: "block",
        0x03: "loop",
        0x04: "if",
        0x05: "else",
        0x0b: "end",
        0x0c: "br",
        0x0d: "br_if",
        0x0e: "br_table",
        0x0f: "return",
        0x10: "call",
        0x11: "call_indirect",

        0x1a: "drop",
        0x20: "local.get",
        0x21: "local.set",
        0x22: "local.tee",

        0x23: "global.get",
        0x24: "global.set",

        0x28: "i32.load",
        0x29: "i64.load",
        0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
        0x2c: "i32.load8_s",

        0x36: "i32.store",
        0x37: "i64.store",
        0x3a: "i32.store8",

        0x41: "i32.const",
        0x42: "i64.const",

        0x45: "i32.eqz",
        0x46: "i32.eq",
        0x47: "i32.ne",
        0x48: "i32.lt_s",
        0x49: "i32.lt_u",
        0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
        0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
        0x4c: "i32.le_s",
        0x4d: "i32.le_u",
        0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
        0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

        0x6a: "i32.add",
        0x6b: "i32.sub",
        0x6c: "i32.mul",
        0x6d: "i32.div_s",
        0x6e: "i32.div_u",
        0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
        0x70: "i32.rem_u",
        0x71: "i32.and",
        0x72: "i32.or",
        0x73: "i32.xor",
        0x74: "i32.shl",
        0x75: "i32.shr_s",
        0x76: "i32.shr_u",
        0x77: "i32.rotl",
        0x78: "i32.rotr",
    };

    return names[op] ?? `op_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

interface Instr {
    offset: number;
    end: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    immediates: number[];
}

function decodeInstruction(p: number, end: number): Instr {
    const start = p;
    const op = wasm[p++];
    const immediates: number[] = [];
    let text = opcodeName(op);

    switch (op) {
        case 0x00:
        case 0x01:
        case 0x05:
        case 0x0b:
        case 0x0f:
        case 0x1a:
        case 0x45:
        case 0x46:
        case 0x47:
        case 0x48:
        case 0x49:
        case 0x4a:
        case 0x4b:
        case 0x4c:
        case 0x4d:
        case 0x4e:
        case 0x4f:
        case 0x6a:
        case 0x6b:
        case 0x6c:
        case 0x6d:
        case 0x6e:
        case 0x6f:
        case 0x70:
        case 0x71:
        case 0x72:
        case 0x73:
        case 0x74:
        case 0x75:
        case 0x76:
        case 0x77:
        case 0x78:
            break;

        case 0x20:
        case 0x21:
        case 0x22:
        case 0x23:
        case 0x24: {
            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;
            immediates.push(r.value);
            text += ` ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x10: {
            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;
            immediates.push(r.value);
            text += ` func=${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x11: {
            const t = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = t.next;

            const table = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = table.next;

            immediates.push(t.value, table.value);

            text += ` type=${t.value} table=${hex(table.value)}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x41: {
            const r = sleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;
            immediates.push(r.value);
            text += ` ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x42: {
            const r = sleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;
            immediates.push(r.value);
            text += ` ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x02:
        case 0x03:
        case 0x04: {
            const b = wasm[p++];
            immediates.push(b);
            text += ` blocktype=${hex(b, 2)}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x0c:
        case 0x0d: {
            const r = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = r.next;
            immediates.push(r.value);
            text += ` ${r.value}`;
            break;
        }

        case 0x28:
        case 0x29:
        case 0x2c:
        case 0x2d:
        case 0x36:
        case 0x37:
        case 0x3a: {
            const align = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = align.next;

            const offset = uleb(wasm, p);
            p = offset.next;

            immediates.push(align.value, offset.value);
            text += ` align=${align.value} offset=${hex(offset.value)}`;
            break;
        }

        default:
            throw new Error(
                `Unsupported opcode ${hex(op, 2)} at ${hex(start)}`
            );
    }

    if (p > end) {
        throw new Error(`Instruction exceeds body at ${hex(start)}`);
    }

    return {
        offset: start,
        end: p,
        op,
        text,
        immediates,
    };
}

function decodeFunction(body: any): Instr[] {
    const result: Instr[] = [];
    let p = body.instrStart;

    while (p < body.instrEnd) {
        const ins = decodeInstruction(p, body.instrEnd);
        result.push(ins);
        p = ins.end;

        if (ins.op === 0x0b) break;
    }

    return result;
}

function findConst114(instrs: Instr[]) {
    return instrs.filter(
        x => x.op === 0x41 && x.immediates[0] === TARGET_VALUE
    );
}

function findIndirectAfter(instrs: Instr[], index: number) {
    const windowEnd = Math.min(instrs.length, index + 80);

    for (let i = index + 1; i < windowEnd; i++) {
        if (instrs[i].op === 0x11) {
            return {
                index: i,
                instruction: instrs[i],
            };
        }

        /*
         * Stop at a return/end if the value cannot survive
         * beyond the current straight-line region.
         */
        if (
            instrs[i].op === 0x0f ||
            instrs[i].op === 0x0b
        ) {
            break;
        }
    }

    return null;
}

export async function run() {
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TABLE SLOT 114 FORWARD TRACE");
    console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log();

    console.log("WASM size :", wasm.length);
    console.log("Target function :", TARGET_FUNCTION);
    console.log(
        "Target table slot:",
        hex(TARGET_SLOT),
        "=",
        TARGET_VALUE
    );
    console.log("Encoding        : 41 f2 00");
    console.log();

    const sections = parseSections();

    const typeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 1);
    const importSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 2);
    const functionSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 3);
    const codeSec = sections.find(x => x.id === 10);

    if (!typeSec || !importSec || !functionSec || !codeSec) {
        throw new Error("Required WASM sections missing");
    }

    const types = parseTypes(typeSec);
    const imports = parseImports(importSec);
    const definedTypes = parseFunctions(functionSec);
    const bodies = parseCode(codeSec);

    console.log("Types          :", types.length);
    console.log("Imported funcs :", imports.length);
    console.log("Defined funcs  :", definedTypes.length);
    console.log("Code bodies    :", bodies.length);
    console.log();

    const targetBody = bodies.find(
        x => x.functionIndex === TARGET_FUNCTION
    );

    if (!targetBody) {
        throw new Error(`Function ${TARGET_FUNCTION} body not found`);
    }

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" TARGET FUNCTION 4154");
    console.log("============================================================");

    const targetInstr = decodeFunction(targetBody);

    for (const ins of targetInstr) {
        console.log(
            hex(ins.offset),
            ins.text
        );
    }

    console.log();

    const targetTypeIndex =
        definedTypes[TARGET_FUNCTION - imports.length];

    console.log(
        "Type index:",
        targetTypeIndex,
        types[targetTypeIndex]
    );

    console.log();

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SEARCHING PRODUCERS OF 114");
    console.log("============================================================");

    let producerCount = 0;
    let forwardCandidates = 0;

    for (const body of bodies) {
        let instrs: Instr[];

        try {
            instrs = decodeFunction(body);
        } catch {
            continue;
        }

        const producers = findConst114(instrs);

        if (producers.length === 0) continue;

        producerCount += producers.length;

        for (const producer of producers) {
            const idx = instrs.indexOf(producer);
            const indirect = findIndirectAfter(instrs, idx);

            console.log();
            console.log(
                `Function ${body.functionIndex} @ ${hex(producer.offset)}`
            );
            console.log("  i32.const 114");

            const from = Math.max(0, idx - 3);
            const to = Math.min(instrs.length, idx + 12);

            for (let i = from; i < to; i++) {
                const x = instrs[i];

                console.log(
                    " ",
                    hex(x.offset),
                    x.text
                );
            }

            if (indirect) {
                forwardCandidates++;

                console.log();
                console.log(
                    "  >>> REAL DECODED call_indirect CANDIDATE"
                );
                console.log(
                    "  >>>",
                    hex(indirect.instruction.offset),
                    indirect.instruction.text
                );
            } else {
                console.log(
                    "  No call_indirect within forward window."
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log();
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(" SUMMARY");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("Decoded producer hits :", producerCount);
    console.log(
        "Forward call_indirect candidates :",
        forwardCandidates
    );

    console.log();
    console.log("Target mapping:");
    console.log(
        `table[${hex(TARGET_SLOT)}] -> function ${TARGET_FUNCTION}`
    );

    console.log();
    console.log("IMPORTANT:");
    console.log(
        "A producer is NOT considered a caller unless a decoded"
    );
    console.log(
        "instruction path reaches a real call_indirect."
    );
    console.log();
    console.log("No transaction sent.");
    console.log("No WASM modification.");
    console.log("Read-only / offline.");
}

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Set([8637, 8927, 8952]);

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
        shift += 7;
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;
        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
    }

    return { value, next: pos };
}

function findCodeSection() {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const id = wasm[p++];
        const s = readULEB(p);
        const size = s.value;
        const payload = s.next;

        if (id === 10) {
            return {
                start: payload,
                end: payload + size
            };
        }

        p = payload + size;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

const code = findCodeSection();

let p = code.start;
const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const bodyCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TARGET CALLER TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");
console.log(`WASM size       : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Code start      : 0x${code.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`Code end        : 0x${code.end.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`);
console.log(`Body count      : ${bodyCount}`);
console.log("");
console.log("TARGETS:");
console.log("  8637");
console.log("  8927");
console.log("  8952");
console.log("");

let found = 0;

for (let body = 0; body < bodyCount; body++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    const bodyStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localDecls = readULEB(q);
    q = localDecls.next;

    for (let i = 0; i < localDecls.value; i++) {
        const n = readULEB(q);
        q = n.next;

        q++; // value type
    }

    const instructionStart = q;

    const bytes: number[] = [];
    for (let x = instructionStart; x < bodyEnd; x++) {
        bytes.push(wasm[x]);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
        const op = bytes[i];

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(instructionStart + i + 1);
            const target = r.value;

            if (TARGETS.has(target)) {
                found++;

                console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
                console.log(
                    `CALLER BODY ${body} GLOBAL FUNCTION ${body + 83}`
                );
                console.log(
                    `CALL @ 0x${(instructionStart + i)
                        .toString(16)
                        .padStart(8, "0")}`
                );
                console.log(`TARGET FUNCTION = ${target}`);

                const from = Math.max(0, i - 40);
                const to = Math.min(bytes.length, i + 20);

                console.log("LOCAL BYTE WINDOW:");

                let line = "";

                for (let j = from; j < to; j++) {
                    line +=
                        bytes[j].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";

                    if ((j - from + 1) % 16 === 0) {
                        console.log(
                            `  0x${(instructionStart + j - 15)
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(8, "0")}  ${line}`
                        );
                        line = "";
                    }
                }

                if (line.length > 0) {
                    console.log(
                        `  0x${(instructionStart + to - line.trim().split(/\s+/).length)
                            .toString(16)
                            .padStart(8, "0")}  ${line}`
                    );
                }

                console.log("");

                // Lightweight backward scan for arg-producing instructions.
                console.log("BACKWARD ARGUMENT CANDIDATES:");

                let depth = 0;

                for (let j = i - 1; j >= 0 && i - j < 100; j--) {
                    const b = bytes[j];

                    if (b === 0x20) {
                        const r2 = readULEB(instructionStart + j + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `  local.get ${r2.value} @ 0x${(
                                instructionStart + j
                            )
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(8, "0")}`
                        );
                    }

                    else if (b === 0x41) {
                        const r2 = readSLEB(instructionStart + j + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `  i32.const ${r2.value} @ 0x${(
                                instructionStart + j
                            )
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(8, "0")}`
                        );
                    }

                    else if (b === 0x28) {
                        console.log(
                            `  i32.load candidate @ 0x${(
                                instructionStart + j
                            )
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(8, "0")}`
                        );
                    }

                    else if (b === 0x10) {
                        const r2 = readULEB(instructionStart + j + 1);

                        console.log(
                            `  call ${r2.value} @ 0x${(
                                instructionStart + j
                            )
                                .toString(16)
                                .padStart(8, "0")}`
                        );
                    }

                    depth++;

                    if (depth > 35) break;
                }

                console.log("");
            }
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TOTAL TARGET CALLS = ${found}`);
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceTargetGroupOwners.ts

import fs from "fs";

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

const DATA_SECTION = 0x324ef5;
let p = DATA_SECTION + 4;

const count = readULEB(p);
p = count.next;

const segments: {
  index: number;
  offset: number;
  size: number;
  fileStart: number;
  fileEnd: number;
}[] = [];

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
  const flags = readULEB(p);
  p = flags.next;

  if (flags.value & 2) {
    const mi = readULEB(p);
    p = mi.next;
  }

  if (wasm[p++] !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error(`Bad data init expr at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);
  }

  const off = readULEB(p);
  p = off.next;

  if (wasm[p++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error(`Bad data init end at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);
  }

  const size = readULEB(p);
  p = size.next;

  segments.push({
    index: i,
    offset: off.value,
    size: size.value,
    fileStart: p,
    fileEnd: p + size.value,
  });

  p += size.value;
}

const MEMORY_SIZE = 32 * 1024 * 1024;
const memory = new Uint8Array(MEMORY_SIZE);

for (const s of segments) {
  if (s.offset + s.size <= MEMORY_SIZE) {
    memory.set(
      wasm.subarray(s.fileStart, s.fileEnd),
      s.offset
    );
  }
}

function u32(addr: number): number {
  return (
    memory[addr] |
    (memory[addr + 1] << 8) |
    (memory[addr + 2] << 16) |
    (memory[addr + 3] << 24)
  ) >>> 0;
}

function hex(v: number) {
  return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function isSeg412(addr: number) {
  return addr >= 0x187000 && addr < 0x18ab8d;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET GROUP OWNER TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`WASM size = ${wasm.length}`);
console.log(`DATA segments = ${segments.length}`);
console.log("");

const groups = [
  {
    name: "GROUP A",
    target: 2053,
    function: 8952,
    start: 0x00189dd8,
    end: 0x00189df4,
  },
  {
    name: "GROUP B",
    target: 2101,
    function: 8927,
    start: 0x00189f88,
    end: 0x00189fa4,
  },
  {
    name: "GROUP C",
    target: 2612,
    function: 8637,
    start: 0x0018a89c,
    end: 0x0018a8b8,
  },
];

for (const g of groups) {
  console.log("============================================================");
  console.log(
    `${g.name}: ${hex(g.start)} .. ${hex(g.end)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `TARGET ${g.target} -> FUNCTION ${g.function}`
  );
  console.log("============================================================");

  console.log("\nGROUP CONTENT:");

  for (let a = g.start; a <= g.end; a += 4) {
    console.log(
      `${hex(a)} : ${u32(a)} (${hex(u32(a))})`
    );
  }

  console.log("\nBACKWARD POINTER SEARCH:");

  const hits: {
    addr: number;
    value: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (
    let a = 0x187000;
    a + 4 <= 0x18ab8d;
    a += 4
  ) {
    const v = u32(a);

    if (
      v === g.start ||
      v === g.start + 4 ||
      v === g.start + 8 ||
      v === g.start + 12
    ) {
      hits.push({
        addr: a,
        value: v,
      });
    }
  }

  if (hits.length === 0) {
    console.log("  NO DIRECT SEG412 POINTERS");
  } else {
    for (const h of hits) {
      console.log(
        `  ${hex(h.addr)} -> ${hex(h.value)}`
      );

      const base = h.addr - 0x20;

      console.log("    CONTEXT:");

      for (let a = base; a <= h.addr + 0x20; a += 4) {
        const v = u32(a);

        let mark = "";

        if (a === h.addr) {
          mark = " <-- POINTER";
        }

        if (
          v === g.target ||
          (v >= g.start && v <= g.end)
        ) {
          mark += " [TARGET/GROUP]";
        }

        console.log(
          `      ${hex(a)} : ${hex(v)} = ${v}${mark}`
        );
      }
    }
  }

  console.log("\nGROUP PREDECESSOR:");

  const pred = g.start - 4;

  console.log(
    `  ${hex(pred)} = ${hex(u32(pred))} = ${u32(pred)}`
  );

  console.log("\nGROUP HEADER CANDIDATES:");

  for (let i = 1; i <= 12; i++) {
    const a = g.start - i * 4;
    const v = u32(a);

    console.log(
      `  -${i * 4} @ ${hex(a)} : ${hex(v)} = ${v}`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CROSS-GROUP POINTER COMPARISON");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const g of groups) {
  console.log("");
  console.log(`${g.name} start ${hex(g.start)}`);

  for (
    let a = 0x187000;
    a + 4 <= 0x18ab8d;
    a += 4
  ) {
    const v = u32(a);

    if (v === g.start) {
      console.log(
        `  POINTER ${hex(a)} -> ${hex(v)}`
      );
    }
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceTargetPtonPairV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const TARGET_LT_MIN = 99491281000000n;
const TARGET_LT_MAX = 99491281000020n;

const OP_PTON_TRANSFER = 0x01f3835d;
const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const OP_JETTON_NOTIFY = 0x7362d09c;
const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : whole.toString();
}

function separator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

async function inspect(
    client: TonClient,
    label: string,
    address: Address
) {
    separator(label);

    console.log('ADDRESS:');
    console.log(address.toString());

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(address, {
        limit: 15,
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log(`TRANSACTIONS FETCHED: ${txs.length}`);

    let found = 0;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (
            lt < TARGET_LT_MIN ||
            lt > TARGET_LT_MAX
        ) {
            continue;
        }

        found++;

        const inMessage = tx.inMessage;

        console.log('');
        console.log('---------------- TARGET TX ----------------');

        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(tx.hash().toString('base64'));

        console.log('TIME:');
        console.log(
            new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
        );

        if (!inMessage) {
            console.log('NO INCOMING MESSAGE');
            continue;
        }

        const op = getOpcode(inMessage.body);

        console.log('OPCODE:');
        console.log(opHex(op));

        if (inMessage.info.type === 'internal') {
            console.log('SOURCE:');
            console.log(inMessage.info.src.toString());

            console.log('DESTINATION:');
            console.log(inMessage.info.dest.toString());

            console.log('VALUE:');
            console.log(
                `${inMessage.info.value.coins} nanoTON`
            );

            console.log('VALUE TON:');
            console.log(
                ton(inMessage.info.value.coins)
            );
        }

        console.log('BODY BOC:');
        console.log(
            inMessage.body
                .toBoc()
                .toString('base64')
        );

        try {
            const s = inMessage.body.beginParse();

            if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
                s.loadUint(32);

                if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                    console.log('QUERY ID:');
                    console.log(
                        s.loadUintBig(64).toString()
                    );
                }

                if (
                    op === OP_PTON_TRANSFER &&
                    s.remainingBits >= 16
                ) {
                    const amount = s.loadCoins();

                    console.log('PTON AMOUNT RAW:');
                    console.log(amount.toString());

                    console.log('PTON AMOUNT TON:');
                    console.log(ton(amount));
                }

                if (
                    op === OP_JETTON_TRANSFER &&
                    s.remainingBits >= 16
                ) {
                    const amount = s.loadCoins();

                    console.log('JETTON AMOUNT RAW:');
                    console.log(amount.toString());

                    console.log('JETTON AMOUNT 9 DECIMAL:');
                    console.log(ton(amount));
                }

                console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
                console.log(s.remainingBits);

                console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                console.log(s.remainingRefs);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.log('DECODE ERROR:');
            console.log(String(e));
        }
    }

    if (found === 0) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('TARGET LT NOT FOUND IN RECENT TRANSACTIONS.');
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    separator('TARGET CURRENT v2.2 pTON PAIR TRACE');

    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT RANGE:');
    console.log(
        `${TARGET_LT_MIN.toString()} -> ${TARGET_LT_MAX.toString()}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('PTON WALLET:');
    console.log(PTON_WALLET.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    await inspect(
        client,
        '1. USER WALLET',
        USER
    );

    await inspect(
        client,
        '2. PTON WALLET',
        PTON_WALLET
    );

    separator('END');

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceTargetRecordReaders.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = [
    {
        index: 2053,
        addr: 0x00189dec,
        function: 8952,
    },
    {
        index: 2101,
        addr: 0x00189f9c,
        function: 8927,
    },
    {
        index: 2612,
        addr: 0x0018a8b0,
        function: 8637,
    },
];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];

        value += (b & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: pos };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readMemU32(mem: Uint8Array, addr: number): number {
    return (
        mem[addr] |
        (mem[addr + 1] << 8) |
        (mem[addr + 2] << 16) |
        (mem[addr + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * DATA SECTION -> LINEAR MEMORY
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let pos = 8;

let dataStart = -1;
let dataEnd = -1;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (sectionId === 11) {
        dataStart = start;
        dataEnd = end;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (dataStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("DATA SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let p = dataStart;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const count = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

const segments: {
    offset: number;
    bytes: Uint8Array;
}[] = [];

let maxAddress = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const flagsInfo = readULEB(p);
    const flags = flagsInfo.value;
    p = flagsInfo.next;

    let offset = -1;

    if (flags === 0) {
        const opcode = buf[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(`Bad data offset opcode at segment ${i}`);
        }

        const off = readULEB(p);
        offset = off.value;
        p = off.next;

        if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error(`Bad data segment end at ${i}`);
        }
    } else if (flags === 2) {
        const mem = readULEB(p);
        p = mem.next;

        const opcode = buf[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(`Bad data offset opcode at segment ${i}`);
        }

        const off = readULEB(p);
        offset = off.value;
        p = off.next;

        if (buf[p++] !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error(`Bad data segment end at ${i}`);
        }
    } else if (flags === 1) {
        const lenInfo = readULEB(p);
        p = lenInfo.next + lenInfo.value;
        continue;
    } else {
        throw new Error(`Unsupported data flags ${flags}`);
    }

    const lenInfo = readULEB(p);
    const len = lenInfo.value;
    p = lenInfo.next;

    const bytes = buf.subarray(p, p + len);
    p += len;

    segments.push({ offset, bytes });

    maxAddress = Math.max(maxAddress, offset + len);
}

if (p !== dataEnd) {
    throw new Error(
        `DATA PARSER MISMATCH ${hex(p)} != ${hex(dataEnd)}`
    );
}

const memory = new Uint8Array(
    Math.max(maxAddress + 0x1000, 0x02000000)
);

for (const seg of segments) {
    if (seg.offset >= 0) {
        memory.set(seg.bytes, seg.offset);
    }
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * VERIFIED TARGET RECORDS
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET RECORD READER SCAN");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();
console.log(`WASM size = ${buf.length}`);
console.log(`DATA segments = ${count}`);
console.log(`MEMORY size = ${memory.length}`);

console.log();
console.log("TARGET RECORDS");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    const actual = readMemU32(memory, t.addr);

    console.log(
        `  ${t.index} @ ${hex(t.addr)}` +
        ` -> ${actual}` +
        ` -> Function ${t.function}`
    );
}

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * CODE SECTION
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 */

let codeStart = -1;
let codeEnd = -1;

pos = 8;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const start = pos;
    const end = start + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    pos = end;
}

if (codeStart < 0) {
    throw new Error("CODE SECTION NOT FOUND");
}

let q = codeStart;

const functionCountInfo = readULEB(q);
const functionCount = functionCountInfo.value;
q = functionCountInfo.next;

console.log();
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS = ${functionCount}`);

/*
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 * DECODE MINIMAL INSTRUCTIONS
 * ------------------------------------------------------------
 *
 * We look for:
 *
 *   i32.const
 *   i32.load
 *
 *   i32.add
 *
 * around references to:
 *
 *   0x189dec
 *   0x189f9c
 *   0x18a8b0
 *
 * Because the target addresses are data addresses,
 * direct constants may be absent.
 * Therefore we also inspect loads whose base/value
 * can point into SEGMENT 412.
 */

const interesting = new Set<number>();

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    interesting.add(t.addr);
}

function isSeg412(addr: number): boolean {
    return (
        addr >= 0x0018744c &&
        addr < 0x0018ab8d
    );
}

function isSeg413(addr: number): boolean {
    return (
        addr >= 0x0018ab98 &&
        addr < 0x0018f512
    );
}

let totalFunctions = 0;
let totalInstructions = 0;

const functionHits: {
    globalFunction: number;
    body: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    hits: string[];
}[] = [];

for (let body = 0; body < functionCount; body++) {
    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(q);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    q = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = q;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    const globalFunction = body + 83;

    const hits: string[] = [];

    /*
     * Keep a small symbolic stack of constants and memory addresses.
     */
    const stack: {
        kind: string;
        value?: number;
    }[] = [];

    let ip = bodyStart;

    /*
     * locals count
     */
    const localGroupInfo = readULEB(ip);
    const localGroups = localGroupInfo.value;
    ip = localGroupInfo.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
        ip = readULEB(ip).next;
        ip += 1;
    }

    while (ip < bodyEnd) {
        const at = ip;
        const op = buf[ip++];

        totalInstructions++;

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const c = readULEB(ip);
            ip = c.next;

            stack.push({
                kind: "const",
                value: c.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const l = readULEB(ip);
            ip = l.next;

            stack.push({
                kind: `local${l.value}`,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
            const l = readULEB(ip);
            ip = l.next;

            if (op === 0x22) {
                stack.push({
                    kind: `local${l.value}`,
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x28) {
            const align = buf[ip++];
            const off = readULEB(ip);
            ip = off.next;

            const base = stack.pop();

            if (
                base?.kind === "const" &&
                base.value !== undefined
            ) {
                const addr = base.value + off.value;

                if (
                    isSeg412(addr) ||
                    isSeg413(addr) ||
                    interesting.has(addr)
                ) {
                    hits.push(
                        `${hex(at)} i32.load ` +
                        `base=${hex(base.value)} ` +
                        `offset=${off.value} ` +
                        `addr=${hex(addr)}`
                    );
                }
            }

            stack.push({
                kind: "load",
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x6a) {
            const b = stack.pop();
            const a = stack.pop();

            if (
                a?.kind === "const" &&
                b?.kind === "const" &&
                a.value !== undefined &&
                b.value !== undefined
            ) {
                stack.push({
                    kind: "const",
                    value: (a.value + b.value) >>> 0,
                });
            } else {
                stack.push({
                    kind: "add",
                });
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            /*
             * call_indirect:
             * type index + table index
             */
            const typeInfo = readULEB(ip);
            ip = typeInfo.next;

            ip += 1;

            const index = stack.pop();

            if (index?.kind === "const") {
                if (
                    index.value === 2053 ||
                    index.value === 2101 ||
                    index.value === 2612
                ) {
                    hits.push(
                        `${hex(at)} call_indirect ` +
                        `INDEX=${index.value}`
                    );
                }
            }

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Simple stack effects for common instructions.
         */

        if (
            op === 0x46 ||
            op === 0x47 ||
            op === 0x48 ||
            op === 0x49 ||
            op === 0x4a ||
            op === 0x4b ||
            op === 0x4c ||
            op === 0x4d ||
            op === 0x4e ||
            op === 0x4f ||
            op === 0x50 ||
            op === 0x51 ||
            op === 0x52 ||
            op === 0x53 ||
            op === 0x54 ||
            op === 0x55 ||
            op === 0x56 ||
            op === 0x57 ||
            op === 0x58 ||
            op === 0x59 ||
            op === 0x5a ||
            op === 0x5b ||
            op === 0x5c ||
            op === 0x5d ||
            op === 0x5e ||
            op === 0x5f
        ) {
            stack.pop();
            stack.pop();
            stack.push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === 0x45 ||
            op === 0x44 ||
            op === 0x43
        ) {
            if (op === 0x43) {
                ip += 4;
            } else if (op === 0x44) {
                ip += 8;
            }

            stack.push({ kind: "unknown" });
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Conservative reset at control-flow instructions.
         */
        if (
            op === 0x0b ||
            op === 0x0c ||
            op === 0x0d ||
            op === 0x0e ||
            op === 0x0f
        ) {
            stack.length = 0;
            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Unknown instruction:
         * keep parser moving but don't trust stack.
         */
    }

    if (hits.length > 0) {
        functionHits.push({
            globalFunction,
            body,
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyEnd,
            hits,
        });
    }

    totalFunctions++;
    q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("CODE SCAN RESULTS");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Functions parsed = ${totalFunctions}`);
console.log(`Instructions scanned = ${totalInstructions}`);
console.log(`Functions with relevant hits = ${functionHits.length}`);

for (const f of functionHits) {
    console.log();
    console.log(
        `Function ${f.globalFunction} ` +
        `(body ${f.body})`
    );

    console.log(
        `  range ${hex(f.start)} .. ${hex(f.end)}`
    );

    for (const h of f.hits) {
        console.log(`  ${h}`);
    }
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DIRECT TARGET ADDRESS BYTE SEARCH");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const t of TARGETS) {
    const pattern = [
        t.addr & 0xff,
        (t.addr >>> 8) & 0xff,
        (t.addr >>> 16) & 0xff,
        (t.addr >>> 24) & 0xff,
    ];

    let hits = 0;

    for (let i = codeStart; i + 4 <= codeEnd; i++) {
        if (
            buf[i] === pattern[0] &&
            buf[i + 1] === pattern[1] &&
            buf[i + 2] === pattern[2] &&
            buf[i + 3] === pattern[3]
        ) {
            hits++;

            if (hits <= 20) {
                console.log(
                    `TARGET ${t.index} address ` +
                    `${hex(t.addr)} found at ` +
                    `${hex(i)}`
                );
            }
        }
    }

    console.log(
        `TARGET ${t.index}: TOTAL BYTE PATTERN HITS = ${hits}`
    );
}

console.log();
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceTargetTableEntries.ts
====================================================================================================
import * as fs from "fs";

const file =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const ELEMENT_START = 0x00002b0d;
const ELEMENT_END   = 0x00006e74;

const TARGETS = new Set([
  8637,
  8927,
  8952
]);

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {

    const x = b[pos + i];

    value += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** shift;

    if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
      return {
        value,
        end: pos + i + 1
      };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }

  return null;
}

function hex(n: number) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTION -> TABLE ENTRY TRACE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("");

let p = ELEMENT_START;

const segmentCount = readULEB(p)!;
p = segmentCount.end;

console.log(`Element segments = ${segmentCount.value}`);

const flags = b[p++];

console.log(`Flags = ${flags}`);

if (flags !== 0) {
  throw new Error("Unexpected element segment flags");
}

/*
   flags=0:

   offset expression:
   i32.const 1
   end
*/

if (b[p++] !== 0x41)
  throw new Error("Expected i32.const");

const tableOffset = readULEB(p)!;
p = tableOffset.end;

if (b[p++] !== 0x0b)
  throw new Error("Expected end");

console.log(`Table offset = ${tableOffset.value}`);

const count = readULEB(p)!;
p = count.end;

console.log(`Function entries = ${count.value}`);
console.log("");

let found = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {

  const fn = readULEB(p);

  if (!fn)
    throw new Error(`Bad function index at ${hex(p)}`);

  const tableIndex =
    tableOffset.value + i;

  if (TARGETS.has(fn.value)) {

    found++;

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(`FUNCTION       = ${fn.value}`);
    console.log(`ELEMENT INDEX  = ${i}`);
    console.log(`TABLE INDEX    = ${tableIndex}`);
    console.log(`BYTE OFFSET    = ${hex(p)}`);
  }

  p = fn.end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`TARGET ENTRIES FOUND = ${found}`);
console.log(`FINAL POINTER        = ${hex(p)}`);
console.log(`EXPECTED END         = ${hex(ELEMENT_END)}`);
console.log("============================================================");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceTargetUserOutgoingV22.ts

import { Address, TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const TARGET_LT = 99491281000001n;

const OP_PTON_TRANSFER = 0x01f3835d;
const OP_JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const OP_JETTON_NOTIFY = 0x7362d09c;
const OP_PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;

function opHex(op: number | null): string {
    if (op === null) return 'UNKNOWN';
    return '0x' + op.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

function getOpcode(body: any): number | null {
    try {
        const s = body.beginParse();

        if (s.remainingBits < 32) {
            return null;
        }

        return s.loadUint(32);
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

function ton(value: bigint): string {
    const whole = value / 1_000_000_000n;

    const frac = (value % 1_000_000_000n)
        .toString()
        .padStart(9, '0')
        .replace(/0+$/, '');

    return frac ? `${whole}.${frac}` : whole.toString();
}

function separator(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    separator('CURRENT v2.2 USER OUTGOING TRACE');

    console.log('READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TARGET LT:');
    console.log(TARGET_LT.toString());

    const client = new TonClient({
        endpoint:
            'https://testnet.toncenter.com/api/v2/jsonRPC',
    });

    const txs = await client.getTransactions(USER, {
        limit: 15,
    });

    let targetFound = false;

    for (const tx of txs) {
        const lt = BigInt(tx.lt);

        if (lt !== TARGET_LT) {
            continue;
        }

        targetFound = true;

        separator('TARGET USER TRANSACTION');

        console.log('LT:');
        console.log(tx.lt.toString());

        console.log('HASH:');
        console.log(
            tx.hash().toString('base64')
        );

        console.log('TIME:');
        console.log(
            new Date(tx.now * 1000).toISOString()
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('OUT MESSAGE COUNT:');
        console.log(
            tx.outMessages.size
        );

        let index = 0;

        for (const message of tx.outMessages.values()) {
            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `---------------- OUTGOING ${index} ----------------`
            );

            const body = message.body;
            const op = getOpcode(body);

            console.log('OPCODE:');
            console.log(opHex(op));

            if (message.info.type === 'internal') {
                console.log('DESTINATION:');
                console.log(
                    message.info.dest.toString()
                );

                console.log('VALUE:');
                console.log(
                    `${message.info.value.coins} nanoTON`
                );

                console.log('VALUE TON:');
                console.log(
                    ton(message.info.value.coins)
                );
            }

            console.log('BODY BOC:');
            console.log(
                body.toBoc().toString('base64')
            );

            try {
                const s = body.beginParse();

                if (s.remainingBits >= 32) {
                    s.loadUint(32);

                    if (s.remainingBits >= 64) {
                        console.log('QUERY ID:');
                        console.log(
                            s.loadUintBig(64).toString()
                        );
                    }

                    if (
                        op === OP_PTON_TRANSFER &&
                        s.remainingBits >= 16
                    ) {
                        const amount = s.loadCoins();

                        console.log(
                            'PTON AMOUNT RAW:'
                        );
                        console.log(
                            amount.toString()
                        );

                        console.log(
                            'PTON AMOUNT TON:'
                        );
                        console.log(
                            ton(amount)
                        );
                    }

                    if (
                        op === OP_JETTON_TRANSFER &&
                        s.remainingBits >= 16
                    ) {
                        const amount = s.loadCoins();

                        console.log(
                            'JETTON AMOUNT RAW:'
                        );
                        console.log(
                            amount.toString()
                        );

                        console.log(
                            'JETTON AMOUNT 9 DECIMAL:'
                        );
                        console.log(
                            ton(amount)
                        );
                    }

                    console.log('REMAINING BITS:');
                    console.log(s.remainingBits);

                    console.log('REMAINING REFS:');
                    console.log(s.remainingRefs);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.log('DECODE ERROR:');
                console.log(String(e));
            }

            index++;
        }

        separator('TARGET USER TRANSACTION BOC');

        if (tx.inMessage) {
            console.log(
                tx.inMessage.body
                    .toBoc()
                    .toString('base64')
            );
        }
    }

    if (!targetFound) {
        separator('RESULT');
        console.log('TARGET TRANSACTION NOT FOUND.');
    }

    separator('FINAL');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import fs from "fs";

const FILE =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(FILE);

function readULEB(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[p++];
        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: p,
    };
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + v.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function u32LE(pos: number): number {
    return (
        wasm[pos] |
        (wasm[pos + 1] << 8) |
        (wasm[pos + 2] << 16) |
        (wasm[pos + 3] << 24)
    ) >>> 0;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" THREE DISPATCH STACK TRACE");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;
let codeEnd = 0;

while (p < wasm.length) {
    const id = wasm[p++];

    const len = readULEB(p);

    const start = len.next;
    const end = start + len.value;

    if (id === 10) {
        codeStart = start;
        codeEnd = end;
        break;
    }

    p = end;
}

if (!codeStart)
    throw new Error("Code section not found");

console.log(`CODE ${hex(codeStart)} .. ${hex(codeEnd)}`);
console.log();

const targets = [
    {
        fn: 8637,
        dispatch: 0x002ced6e,
        start: 0x002ced52,
        end: 0x002ced76,
    },
    {
        fn: 8927,
        dispatch: 0x00302117,
        start: 0x003020f0,
        end: 0x0030211e,
    },
    {
        fn: 8952,
        dispatch: 0x00302299,
        start: 0x00302280,
        end: 0x003022a1,
    },
];

function decodeSimple(pos: number, end: number) {
    const out: {
        pos: number;
        op: string;
        arg?: number;
    }[] = [];

    let p = pos;

    while (p < end) {
        const at = p;
        const op = wasm[p++];

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "local.get",
                arg: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "local.set",
                arg: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "local.tee",
                arg: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x28) {
            const align = wasm[p++];
            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: `i32.load align=${align} offset=${off.value}`,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x2d) {
            const align = wasm[p++];
            const off = readULEB(p);
            p = off.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: `i32.load8_u align=${align} offset=${off.value}`,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "i32.const",
                arg: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x6a) {
            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "i32.add",
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x6b) {
            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "i32.sub",
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const type = readULEB(p);
            p = type.next;

            const table = readULEB(p);
            p = table.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`,
            });

            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const r = readULEB(p);
            p = r.next;

            out.push({
                pos: at,
                op: "call",
                arg: r.value,
            });

            continue;
        }

        /*
         * Skip common one-byte instructions.
         */

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x45: "i32.eqz",
            0x46: "i32.eq",
            0x47: "i32.ne",
            0x4a: "i32.gt_u",
            0x4b: "i32.lt_u",
            0x71: "i32.and",
            0x72: "i32.or",
            0x73: "i32.xor",
            0x0b: "end",
            0x0f: "return",
        };

        out.push({
            pos: at,
            op: names[op] ?? `op=0x${op.toString(16)}`,
        });
    }

    return out;
}

for (const t of targets) {

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`FUNCTION ${t.fn}`);
    console.log(`DISPATCH @ ${hex(t.dispatch)}`);
    console.log("============================================================");

    const decoded = decodeSimple(t.start, t.end);

    for (const x of decoded) {

        let extra = "";

        if (x.arg !== undefined)
            extra = ` ${x.arg}`;

        console.log(
            `${hex(x.pos)}  ${x.op}${extra}`
        );
    }

    console.log();

    /*
     * Exact dispatch stack reconstruction.
     *
     * We specifically reconstruct the expression immediately
     * before call_indirect:
     *
     *     ... base
     *     i32.load offset=4
     *     call_indirect type=4
     */

    const stack: string[] = [];

    for (const x of decoded) {

        const op = x.op;

        if (op === "local.get") {

            if (x.arg! < 2) {
                stack.push(`param[${x.arg}]`);
            } else {
                stack.push(`local[${x.arg}]`);
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op.startsWith("i32.const")) {

            stack.push(`const(${x.arg})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (op.startsWith("i32.load align=2 offset=")) {

            if (stack.length < 1)
                stack.push("UNKNOWN_BASE");
            else {
                const base = stack.pop()!;
                const off =
                    Number(op.split("offset=")[1]);

                stack.push(
                    `MEM[${base}+${off}]`
                );
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op.startsWith("i32.load8_u")) {

            if (stack.length < 1)
                stack.push("UNKNOWN_BASE");
            else {
                const base = stack.pop()!;
                const off =
                    Number(op.split("offset=")[1]);

                stack.push(
                    `MEM8[${base}+${off}]`
                );
            }

            continue;
        }

        if (op === "i32.add") {

            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}+${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === "i32.sub") {

            const b = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";
            const a = stack.pop() ?? "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(`(${a}-${b})`);
            continue;
        }

        if (op === "local.tee") {

            const v =
                stack[stack.length - 1] ??
                "UNKNOWN";

            stack.push(
                `local[${x.arg}]=${v}`
            );

            continue;
        }

        if (op === "local.set") {

            if (stack.length)
                stack.pop();

            continue;
        }

        if (
            op === "call_indirect type=4 table=0"
        ) {

            console.log(
                "STACK BEFORE CALL_INDIRECT:"
            );

            const n = stack.length;

            for (
                let i = 0;
                i < n;
                i++
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `  [${i}] ${stack[i]}`
                );
            }

            console.log();
        }
    }

    /*
     * Raw bytes around the dispatch.
     */

    const from =
        Math.max(codeStart, t.dispatch - 32);

    const to =
        Math.min(codeEnd, t.dispatch + 12);

    console.log(
        `RAW ${hex(from)} .. ${hex(to)}`
    );

    console.log(
        Array.from(wasm.slice(from, to))
            .map(v =>
                v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
            )
            .join(" ")
    );

    console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("END");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\traceType1Selectors.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;
  do {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    s += 7;
  } while (x & 128);
  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  let size;
  [size, p] = u(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;
let count;
[count, q] = u(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TYPE-1 SELECTOR TRACE");
console.log("TARGET : FUNC 1968 / TABLE[5220,5232,5235]");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  let size;
  [size, q] = u(q);

  const start = q;
  const end = q + size;
  const func = i + 91;

  for (let x = start; x < end - 3; x++) {

    if (b[x] !== 0x11) continue;

    let typeIdx, z;
    [typeIdx, z] = u(x + 1);

    let tableIdx;
    [tableIdx, z] = u(z);

    if (typeIdx !== 1 || tableIdx !== 0) continue;

    const from = Math.max(start, x - 40);
    const raw = [...b.slice(from, x + 3)]
      .map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ");

    // Look backwards for direct i32.const immediately preceding
    // the final selector-producing stack operation.
    let direct = [];

    for (let k = Math.max(start, x - 40); k < x; k++) {
      if (b[k] !== 0x41) continue;

      let v = 0, s = 0, y = k + 1;

      while (y < x) {
        const c = b[y++];
        v += (c & 127) * 2 ** s;
        s += 7;
        if (!(c & 128)) break;
      }

      v >>>= 0;

      if (v === 5220 || v === 5232 || v === 5235) {
        direct.push({ offset: k, value: v });
      }
    }

    if (direct.length) {
      console.log("");
      console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
      console.log("FUNC", func);
      console.log("CALL_INDIRECT", "0x" + x.toString(16));
      console.log("TYPE", typeIdx, "TABLE", tableIdx);

      for (const d of direct) {
        console.log(
          "DIRECT CONST :",
          "0x" + d.offset.toString(16),
          "=",
          d.value
        );
      }

      console.log("RAW:", raw);
      hits++;
    }
  }

  q = end;
}

console.log("");
console.log("MATCHING INDIRECT CALLS :", hits);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
