FILE: scripts\traceType7IndexDataflow.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = {
    3901: {
        call: 0x001baa93,
        table: 1209
    },
    6521: {
        call: 0x0022b104,
        table: 579
    },
    8952: {
        call: 0x0030229c,
        table: 2053
    }
};

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos) {
    let result = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[pos++];
        result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readVec(pos) {
    return uleb(pos);
}

function sectionRange(id) {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const sid = buf[p++];
        const s = uleb(p);
        const start = s.next;
        const end = start + s.value;

        if (sid === id)
            return { start, end, size: s.value };

        p = end;
    }

    return null;
}

function readName(pos) {
    const x = uleb(pos);
    const end = x.next + x.value;

    return {
        text: buf.subarray(x.next, end).toString("utf8"),
        next: end
    };
}

function parseTypes() {
    const sec = sectionRange(1);
    if (!sec) throw new Error("Type section not found");

    let p = sec.start;

    const count = uleb(p);
    p = count.next;

    const types = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
        const form = buf[p++];

        if (form !== 0x60) {
            throw new Error(
                `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at ${p - 1}`
            );
        }

        const pc = uleb(p);
        p = pc.next;

        const params = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++)
            params.push(buf[p++]);

        const rc = uleb(p);
        p = rc.next;

        const results = [];

        for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++)
            results.push(buf[p++]);

        types.push({ params, results });
    }

    return types;
}

function parseFunctions(types) {
    const importSec = sectionRange(2);
    const funcSec = sectionRange(3);
    const codeSec = sectionRange(10);

    if (!importSec || !funcSec || !codeSec)
        throw new Error("Required sections missing");

    let p = importSec.start;

    const ic = uleb(p);
    p = ic.next;

    let importedFunctions = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < ic.value; i++) {
        const mod = readName(p);
        p = mod.next;

        const nm = readName(p);
        p = nm.next;

        const kind = buf[p++];

        if (kind === 0) {
            const ti = uleb(p);
            p = ti.next;
            importedFunctions++;
        } else if (kind === 1) {
            p += 4;
            const lim = uleb(p);
            p = lim.next;

            if (lim.value === 1) {
                const max = uleb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 2) {
            const lim = uleb(p);
            p = lim.next;

            if (lim.value === 1) {
                const max = uleb(p);
                p = max.next;
            }
        } else if (kind === 3) {
            p += 2;
        } else {
            throw new Error(
                `Unknown import kind ${kind} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }

    p = funcSec.start;

    const fc = uleb(p);
    p = fc.next;

    const definedTypes = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < fc.value; i++) {
        const t = uleb(p);
        p = t.next;
        definedTypes.push(t.value);
    }

    // Code bodies
    p = codeSec.start;

    const cc = uleb(p);
    p = cc.next;

    if (cc.value !== fc.value)
        throw new Error("Function/code count mismatch");

    const bodies = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < cc.value; i++) {
        const bs = uleb(p);
        const bodyStart = bs.next;
        const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bs.value;

        bodies.push({
            func: importedFunctions + i,
            type: definedTypes[i],
            start: bodyStart,
            end: bodyEnd
        });

        p = bodyEnd;
    }

    return {
        importedFunctions,
        bodies
    };
}

function hex(n) {
    return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function scanFunction(body, types) {
    const out = [];

    let p = body.start;

    // local declarations
    const localGroups = uleb(p);
    p = localGroups.next;

    let localCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < localGroups.value; i++) {
        const n = uleb(p);
        p = n.next;

        const type = buf[p++];

        localCount += n.value;
    }

    while (p < body.end) {
        const opPos = p;
        const op = buf[p++];

        // local.get
        if (op === 0x20) {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "local.get",
                local: x.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // local.set
        if (op === 0x21) {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "local.set",
                local: x.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // local.tee
        if (op === 0x22) {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "local.tee",
                local: x.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // i32.const
        if (op === 0x41) {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "i32.const",
                value: x.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // i32.load
        if (op === 0x28) {
            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = uleb(p);
            p = off.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "i32.load",
                offset: off.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // i32.store
        if (op === 0x36) {
            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = uleb(p);
            p = off.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "i32.store",
                offset: off.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // i32.load8_u
        if (op === 0x2d) {
            const align = uleb(p);
            p = align.next;

            const off = uleb(p);
            p = off.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "i32.load8_u",
                offset: off.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // call
        if (op === 0x10) {
            const x = uleb(p);
            p = x.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "call",
                func: x.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // call_indirect
        if (op === 0x11) {
            const type = uleb(p);
            p = type.next;

            const table = uleb(p);
            p = table.next;

            out.push({
                pos: opPos,
                kind: "call_indirect",
                type: type.value,
                table: table.value
            });
            continue;
        }

        // end
        if (op === 0x0b) {
            continue;
        }

        // common no-immediate instructions
        const noImmediate = new Set([
            0x00, 0x01,
            0x1a,
            0x45,
            0x46,
            0x47,
            0x48,
            0x4a,
            0x4b,
            0x4c,
            0x4d,
            0x4e,
            0x4f,
            0x50,
            0x51,
            0x52,
            0x53,
            0x54,
            0x55,
            0x56,
            0x57,
            0x58,
            0x59,
            0x5a,
            0x5b,
            0x5c,
            0x5d,
            0x5e,
            0x5f,
            0x60,
            0x61,
            0x62,
            0x63,
            0x64,
            0x65,
            0x66,
            0x67,
            0x68,
            0x69,
            0x6a,
            0x6b,
            0x6c,
            0x6d,
            0x6e,
            0x6f,
            0x70,
            0x71,
            0x72,
            0x73,
            0x74,
            0x75,
            0x76,
            0x77,
            0x78,
            0x79,
            0x7a,
            0x7b,
            0x7c,
            0x7d,
            0x7e,
            0x7f
        ]);

        if (noImmediate.has(op))
            continue;

        // For unsupported instructions, stop decoding this body.
        // We do not guess operand lengths.
        break;
    }

    return out;
}

console.log("============================================================");

                    : "",

const fs = require("fs");

const wasmPath =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const targets = [3901, 6521, 8952];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(wasmPath);

function readULEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    result |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: result >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function readSLEB(pos) {
  let result = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte;

  while (true) {
    byte = buf[pos++];
    result |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    result |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: result | 0, next: pos };
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Sections
// ------------------------------------------------------------

let pos = 8;

let functionSection = null;
let codeSection = null;

while (pos < buf.length) {
  const id = buf[pos++];

  const sizeInfo = readULEB(pos);
  const size = sizeInfo.value;
  pos = sizeInfo.next;

  const start = pos;
  const end = start + size;

  if (id === 3) functionSection = { start, end };
  if (id === 10) codeSection = { start, end };

  pos = end;
}

if (!functionSection || !codeSection) {
  throw new Error("Required sections not found.");
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Defined function types
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const definedTypes = [];

{
  let p = functionSection.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const t = readULEB(p);
    definedTypes.push(t.value);
    p = t.next;
  }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Code bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const bodies = [];

{
  let p = codeSection.start;

  const countInfo = readULEB(p);
  const count = countInfo.value;
  p = countInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    const start = sizeInfo.next;
    const end = start + size;

    bodies.push({ start, end });

    p = end;
  }
}

const IMPORTED = 83;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Decode only target bodies
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function inspectFunction(funcIndex) {
  const bodyIndex = funcIndex - IMPORTED;
  const body = bodies[bodyIndex];

  let p = body.start;

  // locals
  const localDeclInfo = readULEB(p);
  const localDeclCount = localDeclInfo.value;
  p = localDeclInfo.next;

  for (let i = 0; i < localDeclCount; i++) {
    const countInfo = readULEB(p);
    p = countInfo.next;
    p += 1;
  }

  const instructions = [];

  while (p < body.end - 1) {
    const addr = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    let text = `opcode=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`;

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text = `local.get ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x21) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text = `local.set ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x22) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text = `local.tee ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = readSLEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text = `i32.const ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x28) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i32.load align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x2c) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i32.load8_s align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x2d) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i32.load8_u align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x36) {
      const align = readULEB(p);
      p = align.next;

      const offset = readULEB(p);
      p = offset.next;

      text = `i32.store align=${align.value} offset=${offset.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = readULEB(p);
      p = r.next;
      text = `call ${r.value}`;
    }

    else if (op === 0x11) {
      const type = readULEB(p);
      p = type.next;

      const table = readULEB(p);
      p = table.next;

      text = `call_indirect type=${type.value} table=${table.value}`;
    }

    instructions.push({ addr, text });
  }

  return instructions;
}

// Output
// ------------------------------------------------------------

console.log("============================================================");

    ) {
      console.log(
        "  0x" +
          ins.addr.toString(16).padStart(8, "0"),
        ins.text
      );
    }
  }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\traceWriter2774.ts

import * as fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const FUNC_INDEX = 2774;
const TARGET = 114;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

interface Ins {
    off: number;
    op: number;
    text: string;
    imm?: number;
    offset?: number;
    local?: number;
    func?: number;
    type?: number;
    table?: number;
}

interface Body {
    index: number;
    start: number;
    end: number;
    code: Uint8Array;
}

function hex(v: number): string {
    return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;

        shift += 7;
    }

    return {
        value: value >>> 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let b = 0;

    while (true) {
        b = wasm[pos++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
        shift += 7;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0)
            break;
    }

    if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40))
        value |= (~0 << shift);

    return {
        value: value | 0,
        next: pos
    };
}

function parseCodeBodies(): Body[] {
    let p = 8;

    while (p < wasm.length) {
        const sectionId = wasm[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
        const sectionSize = sizeInfo.value;
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const sectionEnd = p + sectionSize;

        if (sectionId !== 10) {
            p = sectionEnd;
            continue;
        }

        const countInfo = readULEB(p);
        const count = countInfo.value;
        p = countInfo.next;

        const result: Body[] = [];

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(p);
            const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
            p = bodySizeInfo.next;

            const bodyStart = p;
            const bodyEnd = p + bodySize;

            const localDeclInfo = readULEB(p);
            const localDeclCount = localDeclInfo.value;
            p = localDeclInfo.next;

            for (let j = 0; j < localDeclCount; j++) {
                const n = readULEB(p);
                p = n.next;

                // valtype
                p++;
            }

            const codeStart = p;

            result.push({
                index: 83 + i,
                start: codeStart,
                end: bodyEnd,
                code: wasm.subarray(codeStart, bodyEnd)
            });

            p = bodyEnd;
        }

        return result;
    }

    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

function decode(body: Body): Ins[] {
    const out: Ins[] = [];

    let rel = 0;

    const push = (
        op: number,
        text: string,
        extra: Partial<Ins> = {}
    ) => {
        out.push({
            off: body.start + rel,
            op,
            text,
            ...extra
        });
    };

    while (rel < body.code.length) {
        const op = body.code[rel];

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Control
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x00) {
            push(op, "unreachable");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x01) {
            push(op, "nop");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x02) {
            // blocktype
            const bt = body.code[rel + 1];

            push(
                op,
                `block type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`
            );

            rel += 2;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x03) {
            const bt = body.code[rel + 1];

            push(
                op,
                `loop type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`
            );

            rel += 2;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x04) {
            const bt = body.code[rel + 1];

            push(
                op,
                `if type=0x${bt.toString(16)}`
            );

            rel += 2;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x05) {
            push(op, "else");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0b) {
            push(op, "end");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0c) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `br ${x.value}`,
                { imm: x.value }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0d) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `br_if ${x.value}`,
                { imm: x.value }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0e) {
            let q = body.start + rel + 1;

            const n = readULEB(q);
            q = n.next;

            for (let i = 0; i < n.value; i++) {
                q = readULEB(q).next;
            }

            const def = readULEB(q);

            push(
                op,
                `br_table ${n.value} default=${def.value}`
            );

            rel = def.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x0f) {
            push(op, "return");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x10) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `call ${x.value}`,
                {
                    func: x.value,
                    imm: x.value
                }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x11) {
            const t = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
            const table = readULEB(t.next);

            push(
                op,
                `call_indirect type=${t.value} table=${table.value}`,
                {
                    type: t.value,
                    table: table.value
                }
            );

            rel = table.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Parametric
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x1a) {
            push(op, "drop");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x1b) {
            push(op, "select");
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Variables
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x20) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `local.get ${x.value}`,
                {
                    local: x.value,
                    imm: x.value
                }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x21) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `local.set ${x.value}`,
                {
                    local: x.value,
                    imm: x.value
                }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x22) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `local.tee ${x.value}`,
                {
                    local: x.value,
                    imm: x.value
                }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x23) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `global.get ${x.value}`,
                { imm: x.value }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x24) {
            const x = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `global.set ${x.value}`,
                { imm: x.value }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Constants
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (op === 0x41) {
            const x = readSLEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `i32.const ${x.value}`,
                { imm: x.value }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        if (op === 0x42) {
            const x = readSLEB(body.start + rel + 1);

            push(
                op,
                `i64.const ${x.value}`,
                { imm: x.value }
            );

            rel = x.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Loads
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x35) {
            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x28: "i32.load",
                0x29: "i64.load",
                0x2a: "f32.load",
                0x2b: "f64.load",
                0x2c: "i32.load8_s",
                0x2d: "i32.load8_u",
                0x2e: "i32.load16_s",
                0x2f: "i32.load16_u",
                0x30: "i64.load8_s",
                0x31: "i64.load8_u",
                0x32: "i64.load16_s",
                0x33: "i64.load16_u",
                0x34: "i64.load32_s",
                0x35: "i64.load32_u"
            };

            const a = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
            const o = readULEB(a.next);

            push(
                op,
                `${names[op]} offset=${o.value}`,
                { offset: o.value }
            );

            rel = o.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Stores
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        if (op >= 0x36 && op <= 0x3e) {
            const names: Record<number, string> = {
                0x36: "i32.store",
                0x37: "i64.store",
                0x38: "f32.store",
                0x39: "f64.store",
                0x3a: "i32.store8",
                0x3b: "i32.store16",
                0x3c: "i64.store8",
                0x3d: "i64.store16",
                0x3e: "i64.store32"
            };

            const a = readULEB(body.start + rel + 1);
            const o = readULEB(a.next);

            push(
                op,
                `${names[op]} offset=${o.value}`,
                { offset: o.value }
            );

            rel = o.next - body.start;
            continue;
        }

        // --------------------------------------------------------
        // Numeric
        // --------------------------------------------------------

        const names: Record<number, string> = {
            0x45: "i32.eqz",
            0x46: "i32.eq",
            0x47: "i32.ne",
            0x48: "i32.lt_s",
            0x49: "i32.lt_u",
            0x4a: "i32.gt_s",
            0x4b: "i32.gt_u",
            0x4c: "i32.le_s",
            0x4d: "i32.le_u",
            0x4e: "i32.ge_s",
            0x4f: "i32.ge_u",

            0x67: "i32.clz",
            0x68: "i32.ctz",
            0x69: "i32.popcnt",

            0x6a: "i32.add",
            0x6b: "i32.sub",
            0x6c: "i32.mul",
            0x6d: "i32.div_s",
            0x6e: "i32.div_u",
            0x6f: "i32.rem_s",
            0x70: "i32.rem_u",
            0x71: "i32.and",
            0x72: "i32.or",
            0x73: "i32.xor",
            0x74: "i32.shl",
            0x75: "i32.shr_s",
            0x76: "i32.shr_u",
            0x77: "i32.rotl",
            0x78: "i32.rotr"
        };

        if (names[op]) {
            push(op, names[op]);
            rel++;
            continue;
        }

        // Unknown opcode: preserve one byte.
        push(
            op,
            `op=0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
        );

        rel++;
    }

    return out;
}

function showRange(
    ins: Ins[],
    center: number,
    radius = 18
) {
    const idx = ins.findIndex(
        x => x.off === center
    );

    if (idx < 0)
        return;

    const a = Math.max(0, idx - radius);
    const b = Math.min(
        ins.length,
        idx + radius + 1
    );

    for (let i = a; i < b; i++) {
        console.log(
            `  ${hex(ins[i].off)} ${ins[i].text}`
        );
    }
}

function findLocalSources(
    ins: Ins[],
    targetLocal: number,
    beforeIndex: number
): number[] {
    const result: number[] = [];

    for (
        let i = beforeIndex - 1;
        i >= 0;
        i--
    ) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.text ===
            `local.set ${targetLocal}`
        ) {
            result.push(i);

            // Continue backwards; later definitions
            // can reveal branch-dependent sources.
        }

        if (
            x.text ===
            `local.tee ${targetLocal}`
        ) {
            result.push(i);
        }
    }

    return result;
}

function traceLocalSource(
    ins: Ins[],
    local: number,
    setIndex: number,
    depth = 0
): string {
    if (depth > 6)
        return "DEPTH_LIMIT";

    for (
        let i = setIndex - 1;
        i >= Math.max(0, setIndex - 80);
        i--
    ) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            x.text ===
            `i32.const ${TARGET}`
        ) {
            return `CONST_114 @ ${hex(x.off)}`;
        }

        if (
            x.text.startsWith(
                "local.get "
            )
        ) {
            const sourceLocal = x.local!;

            const sourceSets = findLocalSources(
                ins,
                sourceLocal,
                i
            );

            if (sourceSets.length > 0) {
                return (
                    `LOCAL_${sourceLocal} -> ` +
                    traceLocalSource(
                        ins,
                        sourceLocal,
                        sourceSets[0],
                        depth + 1
                    )
                );
            }

            return `LOCAL_${sourceLocal}`;
        }

        if (
            x.text.startsWith("call ")
        ) {
            return (
                `CALL_${x.func} before local ${local}`
            );
        }

        if (
            x.text.includes("load")
        ) {
            return (
                `MEMORY_LOAD before local ${local}`
            );
        }

        if (
            x.text === "if" ||
            x.text.startsWith("block") ||
            x.text.startsWith("loop") ||
            x.text === "else" ||
            x.text === "end"
        ) {
            continue;
        }
    }

    return "UNKNOWN";
}

export async function run() {
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " TRACE FUNCTION 2774 WRITERS OF 114"
    );
    console.log(
        " READ ONLY - OFFLINE"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "WASM size :",
        wasm.length
    );

    console.log(
        "Function  :",
        FUNC_INDEX
    );

    console.log(
        "Target    :",
        TARGET
    );

    console.log("");

    const bodies = parseCodeBodies();

    const body = bodies.find(
        x => x.index === FUNC_INDEX
    );

    if (!body)
        throw new Error(
            `Function ${FUNC_INDEX} not found`
        );

    const ins = decode(body);

    console.log(
        "Function range:",
        `${hex(body.start)} .. ${hex(body.end - 1)}`
    );

    console.log(
        "Instructions:",
        ins.length
    );

    console.log("");

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Locate CONST 114
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const constSites: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        if (
            ins[i].text ===
            `i32.const ${TARGET}`
        ) {
            constSites.push(i);
        }
    }

    console.log(
        "CONST(114) sites:",
        constSites.length
    );

    console.log("");

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Analyze each CONST(114)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    for (
        let n = 0;
        n < constSites.length;
        n++
    ) {
        const ci = constSites[n];

        console.log(
            "------------------------------------------------------------"
        );

        console.log(
            `CONST(114) #${n + 1} @ ${hex(ins[ci].off)}`
        );

        console.log("");

        showRange(
            ins,
            ins[ci].off,
            16
        );

        console.log("");

        // Search next store.
        let storeIndex = -1;

        for (
            let i = ci + 1;
            i < Math.min(
                ins.length,
                ci + 20
            );
            i++
        ) {
            if (
                ins[i].text.includes("store")
            ) {
                storeIndex = i;
                break;
            }

            if (
                ins[i].text.startsWith(
                    "call_indirect"
                )
            )
                break;
        }

        if (storeIndex < 0) {
            console.log(
                "Next STORE: none"
            );
            console.log("");
            continue;
        }

        console.log(
            "Next STORE:",
            hex(ins[storeIndex].off)
        );

        console.log(
            "STORE instruction:",
            ins[storeIndex].text
        );

        /*
         * WebAssembly i32.store stack:
         *
         *   address
         *   value
         *   i32.store
         *
         * Therefore for:
         *
         *   local.get 2
         *   i32.const 114
         *   i32.store
         *
         * local 2 is the address.
         */

        if (
            ci + 1 === storeIndex - 1 &&
            ins[ci + 1].text.includes("store")
        ) {
            console.log(
                "Direct CONST(114) -> STORE : YES"
            );

            const addressIns = ins[ci - 1];

            if (
                addressIns &&
                addressIns.text.startsWith(
                    "local.get "
                )
            ) {
                const local =
                    addressIns.local!;

                console.log(
                    "STORE address local:",
                    local
                );

                const sources =
                    findLocalSources(
                        ins,
                        local,
                        ci
                    );

                console.log(
                    "Previous local definitions:",
                    sources.length
                );

                for (
                    const si of sources.slice(0, 20)
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `  SET @ ${hex(ins[si].off)}`
                    );

                    console.log(
                        `    source = ${traceLocalSource(
                            ins,
                            local,
                            si
                        )}`
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Locate all stores and identify address/value pattern
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " ALL i32 STORE SITES IN FUNCTION 2774"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    let storeCount = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < ins.length; i++) {
        const x = ins[i];

        if (
            !x.text.startsWith("i32.store")
        )
            continue;

        storeCount++;

        console.log(
            `STORE #${storeCount} @ ${hex(x.off)} ${x.text}`
        );

        const a = Math.max(
            0,
            i - 4
        );

        for (
            let j = a;
            j <= i;
            j++
        ) {
            console.log(
                `  ${hex(ins[j].off)} ${ins[j].text}`
            );
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    console.log(
        "Total i32 stores:",
        storeCount
    );

    console.log("");

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Search loads of locals used as the address of 114 stores.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " FORWARD SEARCH AFTER 114 WRITERS"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    for (
        let n = 0;
        n < constSites.length;
        n++
    ) {
        const ci = constSites[n];

        if (
            ci === 0 ||
            !ins[ci - 1].text.startsWith(
                "local.get "
            )
        )
            continue;

        const addrLocal =
            ins[ci - 1].local!;

        let storeIndex = -1;

        for (
            let i = ci + 1;
            i < Math.min(
                ins.length,
                ci + 10
            );
            i++
        ) {
            if (
                ins[i].text.startsWith(
                    "i32.store"
                )
            ) {
                storeIndex = i;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (storeIndex < 0)
            continue;

        console.log(
            `Writer #${n + 1}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  address local = ${addrLocal}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  CONST @ ${hex(ins[ci].off)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  STORE @ ${hex(ins[storeIndex].off)}`
        );

        console.log("");

        let loads = 0;
        let indirects = 0;

        for (
            let i = storeIndex + 1;
            i < ins.length;
            i++
        ) {
            const x = ins[i];

            if (
                x.text ===
                `local.get ${addrLocal}`
            ) {
                console.log(
                    `  LOCAL GET @ ${hex(x.off)}`
                );

                // Show following instructions.
                for (
                    let j = i + 1;
                    j < Math.min(
                        ins.length,
                        i + 8
                    );
                    j++
                ) {
                    console.log(
                        `    ${hex(ins[j].off)} ${ins[j].text}`
                    );

                    if (
                        ins[j].text.includes(
                            "load"
                        )
                    )
                        loads++;

                    if (
                        ins[j].text.startsWith(
                            "call_indirect"
                        )
                    ) {
                        indirects++;

                        console.log(
                            "    >>> CALL_INDIRECT AFTER WRITER <<<"
                        );
                    }
                }
            }

            if (
                x.text === "return"
            )
                break;
        }

        console.log(
            `  loads after writer = ${loads}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  indirects after writer = ${indirects}`
        );

        console.log("");
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // Final
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log(
        " FINAL"
    );
    console.log(
        "============================================================"
    );
    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "Function 2774 : analyzed"
    );

    console.log(
        "CONST(114)    :",
        constSites.length
    );

    console.log(
        "i32 stores    :",
        storeCount
    );

    console.log("");

    console.log(
        "IMPORTANT:"
    );

    console.log(
        "CONST(114)->STORE proves only a memory write."
    );

    console.log(
        "It does NOT yet prove table[114]."
    );

    console.log(
        "The decisive evidence is a later LOAD of the same"
    );

    console.log(
        "memory location feeding call_indirect type=0 table=0."
    );

    console.log("");
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { JettonWallet } from '../wrappers/JettonWallet';

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const sourceJettonWallet = Address.parse(
        'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
    );

    const destinationOwner = Address.parse(
        'EQCcuKtKTEvFaHvh0eKWal4G2AvhHW_Y89iCb8HIBX2dyTom'
    );

    const wallet = provider.open(
        JettonWallet.createFromAddress(sourceJettonWallet)
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('===== ROOSTER TRANSFER =====');
    console.log('Source Jetton Wallet:', sourceJettonWallet.toString());
    console.log('Destination Owner:', destinationOwner.toString());
    console.log('Amount: 100 ROOSTER');
    console.log('TON attached: 0.05 TON');
    console.log('============================');
    console.log('');

    await wallet.sendTransfer(
        provider.sender(),
        destinationOwner
    );

    console.log('Transfer transaction sent.');
}

FILE: scripts\validateCurrentPoolV22.ts

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100000000000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249908n;
const BOC_MIN_LP = 150109265n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.fi v2.2 CURRENT POOL READ-ONLY VALIDATOR');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED ROUTER:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED ROOSTER WALLET:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('EXPECTED pTON WALLET:');
    console.log(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('USER:');
    console.log(USER.toString());

    const poolProvider = provider.provider(POOL);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' 1. CURRENT get_pool_data');
    console.log('============================================================');

    try {
        const result = await poolProvider.get(
            'get_pool_data',
            []
        );

        console.log('get_pool_data: SUCCESS');
        console.log('');

        const stack = result.stack;

        const isLocked = stack.readBoolean();
        const routerAddress = stack.readAddress();
        const totalSupply = stack.readBigNumber();
        const reserve0 = stack.readBigNumber();
        const reserve1 = stack.readBigNumber();
        const token0 = stack.readAddress();
        const token1 = stack.readAddress();
        const lpFee = stack.readBigNumber();

        console.log('CURRENT ON-CHAIN DATA');
        console.log('---------------------');
        console.log(`locked:       ${isLocked}`);
        console.log(`router:       ${routerAddress?.toString()}`);
        console.log(`total LP:     ${totalSupply}`);
        console.log(`reserve0:     ${reserve0}`);
        console.log(`reserve1:     ${reserve1}`);
        console.log(`token0:       ${token0?.toString()}`);
        console.log(`token1:       ${token1?.toString()}`);
        console.log(`lp fee:       ${lpFee}`);

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' 2. ADDRESS VALIDATION');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(
            `${routerAddress?.equals(EXPECTED_ROUTER) ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Router`
        );

        console.log(
            `${token0?.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET) ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Token0 = ROOSTER`
        );

        console.log(
            `${token1?.equals(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET) ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Token1 = pTON`
        );

        console.log(
            `${isLocked === false ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Pool unlocked`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' 3. LIQUIDITY INPUT CROSS-CHECK');
        console.log('============================================================');

        console.log(`ROOSTER input: ${ROOSTER_AMOUNT}`);
        console.log(`ROOSTER display: ${Number(ROOSTER_AMOUNT) / 1e9}`);

        console.log(`pTON input: ${PTON_AMOUNT}`);
        console.log(`pTON display: ${Number(PTON_AMOUNT) / 1e9}`);

        console.log(`BOC minLpOut: ${BOC_MIN_LP}`);

        console.log('');
        console.log('CURRENT RESERVES');
        console.log(`ROOSTER reserve: ${reserve0}`);
        console.log(`pTON reserve:    ${reserve1}`);

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' 4. BASIC RESERVE SANITY');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const roosterReservePositive = reserve0 > 0n;
        const ptonReservePositive = reserve1 > 0n;
        const lpSupplyPositive = totalSupply > 0n;

        console.log(
            `${roosterReservePositive ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | ROOSTER reserve > 0`
        );

        console.log(
            `${ptonReservePositive ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | pTON reserve > 0`
        );

        console.log(
            `${lpSupplyPositive ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | LP supply > 0`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' 5. LP PROPORTION CHECK');
        console.log('============================================================');

        /*
         * For a proportional two-sided deposit, the LP amount is
         * constrained by both reserve ratios:
         *
         *   LP0 = amount0 * totalLP / reserve0
         *   LP1 = amount1 * totalLP / reserve1
         *
         * The smaller side is the limiting side.
         *
         * This is only a diagnostic calculation.
         * It does NOT build or send a transaction.
         */

        if (
            reserve0 > 0n &&
            reserve1 > 0n &&
            totalSupply > 0n
        ) {
            const lpFromRooster =
                (ROOSTER_AMOUNT * totalSupply) / reserve0;

            const lpFromPton =
                (PTON_AMOUNT * totalSupply) / reserve1;

            const proportionalLp =
                lpFromRooster < lpFromPton
                    ? lpFromRooster
                    : lpFromPton;

            console.log(`LP from ROOSTER side: ${lpFromRooster}`);
            console.log(`LP from pTON side:    ${lpFromPton}`);
            console.log(`Limiting LP amount:   ${proportionalLp}`);
            console.log(`BOC minLpOut:         ${BOC_MIN_LP}`);

            console.log('');

            console.log(
                `${BOC_MIN_LP <= proportionalLp ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | BOC minLpOut <= proportional LP estimate`
            );

            if (proportionalLp > 0n) {
                const slippageNumerator =
                    proportionalLp - BOC_MIN_LP;

                console.log(
                    `Difference from estimate: ${slippageNumerator}`
                );

                console.log(
                    `BOC minimum / estimate: ${Number(BOC_MIN_LP) / Number(proportionalLp)}`
                );
            }
        } else {
            console.log(
                'FAIL | Cannot calculate LP proportion because one or more reserves are zero.'
            );
        }

        console.log('');
        console.log('============================================================');
        console.log(' 6. FINAL READ-ONLY STATUS');
        console.log('============================================================');

        const addressesPass =
            routerAddress?.equals(EXPECTED_ROUTER) &&
            token0?.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET) &&
            token1?.equals(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET);

        const reservesPass =
            reserve0 > 0n &&
            reserve1 > 0n &&
            totalSupply > 0n;

        console.log(
            `${addressesPass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Pool addresses`
        );

        console.log(
            `${reservesPass ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Pool reserves`
        );

        console.log(
            `${isLocked === false ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | Pool unlocked`
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            addressesPass && reservesPass && !isLocked
                ? 'STATUS: PASS'
                : 'STATUS: FAIL'
        );

    } catch (error) {
        console.log('');
        console.log('get_pool_data ERROR:');
        console.log(error);
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY INSPECTION FINISHED');
    console.log(' NO TRANSACTION SENT');
    console.log(' NO WALLET SIGNATURE REQUESTED');
    console.log('============================================================');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';
import { readFileSync } from 'fs';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'

const EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(

const ROOSTER_BOC = './rooster_v22_generated.boc';
const PTON_BOC = './pton_v22_generated.boc';

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_VALUE = 300000000n;
const EXPECTED_PTON_VALUE = 310249908n;

function header(title: string) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(` ${title}`);
    console.log('============================================================');
}

function check(
    label: string,
    actual: boolean,
    actualValue?: string,
    expectedValue?: string
) {
    console.log(`${actual ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'} | ${label}`);

    if (!actual) {
        if (actualValue !== undefined) {
            console.log('  ACTUAL  :', actualValue);
        }

        if (expectedValue !== undefined) {
            console.log('  EXPECTED:', expectedValue);
        }
    }
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {

    header('STON.FI v2.2 TRANSACTION ENVELOPE VALIDATOR');

    console.log('READ ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO WALLET SIGNATURE WILL BE REQUESTED.');
    console.log('NO FUNDS WILL MOVE.');

    // ============================================================
    // 1. SDK OBJECTS
    // ============================================================

    header('1. SDK ENVELOPE RECONSTRUCTION');

    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    const proxyTon =
        new DEX.v2_2.pTON(PTON_MASTER);

    console.log('Router:', ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('pTON master:', PTON_MASTER.toString());

    // ============================================================
    // 2. ROOSTER ENVELOPE
    // ============================================================

    header('2. ROOSTER EXTERNAL ENVELOPE');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,

                sendTokenAddress:
                    ROOSTER_MASTER,

                otherTokenAddress:
                    PTON_MASTER,

                sendAmount:
                    100000000000n,

                minLpOut:
                    150109265n,

                receiverAddress:
                    USER,

                refundAddress:
                    USER,

                excessesAddress:
                    USER,

                transferExcessAddress:
                    USER,

                // Deadline is irrelevant for the
                // external destination/value check.
                // The saved BOC itself is validated separately.
                deadline:
                    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 600,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK ROOSTER ENVELOPE');
    console.log('--------------------');

    console.log(
        'TO:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    check(
        'ROOSTER external destination = ROOSTER wallet',
        roosterTx.to.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET),
        roosterTx.to.toString(),
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET.toString()
    );

    check(
        'ROOSTER value = 0.3 TON',
        roosterTx.value === EXPECTED_ROOSTER_VALUE,
        roosterTx.value.toString(),
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_VALUE.toString()
    );

    // ============================================================
    // 3. pTON ENVELOPE
    // ============================================================

    header('3. pTON EXTERNAL ENVELOPE');

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress:
                    USER,

                proxyTon:
                    proxyTon,

                otherTokenAddress:
                    ROOSTER_MASTER,

                sendAmount:
                    249908n,

                minLpOut:
                    150109265n,

                receiverAddress:
                    USER,

                refundAddress:
                    USER,

                excessesAddress:
                    USER,

                deadline:
                    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 600,
            }
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK pTON ENVELOPE');
    console.log('----------------');

    console.log(
        'TO:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'VALUE:',
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        'nanoTON'
    );

    check(
        'pTON external destination = pTON wallet',
        ptonTx.to.equals(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET),
        ptonTx.to.toString(),
        EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    check(
        'pTON value = 310249908 nanoTON',
        ptonTx.value === EXPECTED_PTON_VALUE,
        ptonTx.value.toString(),
        EXPECTED_PTON_VALUE.toString()
    );

    // ============================================================
    // 4. SAVED BOC EXISTENCE / SIZE
    // ============================================================

    header('4. SAVED BOC FILES');

    let roosterBody: Buffer;
    let ptonBody: Buffer;

    try {
        roosterBody =
            readFileSync(ROOSTER_BOC);

        console.log(
            'ROOSTER BOC:',
            ROOSTER_BOC,
            `(${roosterBody.length} bytes)`
        );

        check(
            'ROOSTER BOC exists',
            roosterBody.length > 0
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('ROOSTER BOC ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
        return;
    }

    try {
        ptonBody =
            readFileSync(PTON_BOC);

        console.log(
            'pTON BOC:',
            PTON_BOC,
            `(${ptonBody.length} bytes)`
        );

        check(
            'pTON BOC exists',
            ptonBody.length > 0
        );
    } catch (e) {
        console.log('pTON BOC ERROR:');
        console.log(e);
        return;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // 5. BOC BODY CONSISTENCY
    // ============================================================

    header('5. BODY / ENVELOPE CONSISTENCY');

    /*
     * The BOC files contain only the transaction body.
     *
     * The external transaction destination and value are NOT
     * stored in these BOCs.
     *
     * Therefore:
     *
     *   SDK -> verifies external TO/VALUE
     *   BOC -> independently verified by finalValidateV22
     *
     * This section makes that distinction explicit.
     */

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER BOC is body-only:',
        'YES'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON BOC is body-only:',
        'YES'
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'ROOSTER external TO:',
        roosterTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER body file:',
        ROOSTER_BOC
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'pTON external TO:',
        ptonTx.to.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON body file:',
        PTON_BOC
    );

    // ============================================================
    // 6. FINAL
    // ============================================================

    header('6. FINAL ENVELOPE STATUS');

    const roosterPass =
        roosterTx.to.equals(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_WALLET) &&
        roosterTx.value === EXPECTED_ROOSTER_VALUE;

    const ptonPass =
        ptonTx.to.equals(EXPECTED_PTON_WALLET) &&
        ptonTx.value === EXPECTED_PTON_VALUE;

    check(
        'ROOSTER envelope',
        roosterPass
    );

    check(
        'pTON envelope',
        ptonPass
    );

    console.log('');

    if (roosterPass && ptonPass) {
        console.log('STATUS: PASS');
    } else {
        console.log('STATUS: FAIL');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION SENT.');
    console.log('NO WALLET SIGNATURE REQUESTED.');
    console.log('NO FUNDS MOVED.');
}

FILE: scripts\verify656Signature.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const TARGET_FUNC = 656;

const CALLS = [
  { func: 473,  addr: 0x00020087 },
  { func: 599,  addr: 0x000284fb },
  { func: 1249, addr: 0x00057842 },
  { func: 1601, addr: 0x00071954 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0b8 },
  { func: 2161, addr: 0x0009b0fe },
  { func: 3490, addr: 0x0010da53 },
  { func: 6665, addr: 0x0022fa9e },
  { func: 7020, addr: 0x0023d95e },
];

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function uleb(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (true) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;
  }
}

function section(id: number) {
  let p = 8;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const sid = wasm[p++];

    const s = uleb(p);
    p = s.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = start + s.value;

    if (sid === id) {
      return { start, end };
    }

    p = end;
  }

  throw new Error(`SECTION ${id} NOT FOUND`);
}

function readTypes() {
  const sec = section(1);

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const types: {
    params: number[];
    results: number[];
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const form = wasm[p++];

    if (form !== 0x60) {
      throw new Error(
        `Unexpected type form 0x${form.toString(16)} at ${p - 1}`
      );
    }

    const pc = uleb(p);
    p = pc.next;

    const params: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < pc.value; j++) {
      params.push(wasm[p++]);
    }

    const rc = uleb(p);
    p = rc.next;

    const results: number[] = [];

    for (let j = 0; j < rc.value; j++) {
      results.push(wasm[p++]);
    }

    types.push({ params, results });
  }

  return types;
}

function getFunctionTypes() {
  const sec = section(3);

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const out: number[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const t = uleb(p);
    p = t.next;
    out.push(t.value);
  }

  return out;
}

function getBodies() {
  const sec = section(10);

  let p = sec.start;

  const count = uleb(p);
  p = count.next;

  const bodies: {
    start: number;
    end: number;
    code: number;
  }[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
    const sz = uleb(p);
    p = sz.next;

    const start = p;
    const end = p + sz.value;

    const locals = uleb(p);
    p = locals.next;

    for (let j = 0; j < locals.value; j++) {
      const n = uleb(p);
      p = n.next;

      p++;
    }

    bodies.push({
      start,
      end,
      code: p,
    });

    p = end;
  }

  return bodies;
}

function hex(x: number) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

const types = readTypes();
const funcTypes = getFunctionTypes();
const bodies = getBodies();

console.log("VERIFY FUNC 656 SIGNATURE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

const definedOrdinal = TARGET_FUNC - 83;

console.log(`GLOBAL FUNC = ${TARGET_FUNC}`);
console.log(`DEFINED ORD  = ${definedOrdinal}`);

const typeIndex = funcTypes[definedOrdinal];

console.log(`TYPE INDEX   = ${typeIndex}`);

const sig = types[typeIndex];

if (!sig) {
  throw new Error("TYPE NOT FOUND");
}

console.log(
  `PARAM COUNT  = ${sig.params.length}`
);

console.log(
  `RESULT COUNT = ${sig.results.length}`
);

console.log(
  `PARAM TYPES  = [${sig.params.join(", ")}]`
);

console.log(
  `RESULT TYPES = [${sig.results.join(", ")}]`
);

console.log("");
console.log("CALL SITES");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const c of CALLS) {
  const body = bodies.find(
    b => c.addr >= b.code && c.addr < b.end
  );

  console.log("");
  console.log(`FUNC ${c.func} @ ${hex(c.addr)}`);

  if (!body) {
    console.log("BODY NOT FOUND");
    continue;
  }

  /*
   * Scan backward and print only the instructions that
   * immediately create the apparent stack values.
   *
   * This is diagnostic; no execution.
   */
  const start = Math.max(body.code, c.addr - 40);

  let p = start;

  while (p < c.addr) {
    const op = wasm[p];

    if (op === 0x20) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} local.get ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x41) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i32.const ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x22) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} local.tee ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x21) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} local.set ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x28) {
      const a = uleb(p + 1);
      const b = uleb(a.next);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} i32.load align=${a.value} offset=${b.value}`
      );

      p = b.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (op === 0x10) {
      const r = uleb(p + 1);

      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} call ${r.value}`
      );

      p = r.next;
      continue;
    }

    if (
      op === 0x6a ||
      op === 0x6b ||
      op === 0x6c ||
      op === 0x6d ||
      op === 0x6e ||
      op === 0x71 ||
      op === 0x74 ||
      op === 0x76 ||
      op === 0xa7
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );

      p++;
      continue;
    }

    /*
     * Stop at structural instructions.
     */
    if (
      op === 0x02 ||
      op === 0x03 ||
      op === 0x04 ||
      op === 0x05 ||
      op === 0x0b ||
      op === 0x0c ||
      op === 0x0d
    ) {
      console.log(
        `${hex(p)} structural opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
      );

      p++;
      continue;
    }

    console.log(
      `${hex(p)} opcode 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
    );

    p++;
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("READ ONLY - NO EXECUTION - NO TRANSACTION");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\verifyCall87WindowsV1.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM = "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CALLS = [
  0x122c4, 0x122dc, 0x122f6, 0x12310, 0x1232c,
  0x12346, 0x12358, 0x1236b, 0x1237b, 0x1238b,
  0x1239b, 0x123ae, 0x123be, 0x123ce, 0x123de,
  0x123f0, 0x12403, 0x12416, 0x1242b, 0x12440,
  0x12455,
  0x12675, 0x12685, 0x12695, 0x126a5, 0x126b4,
  0x126c4, 0x126d4, 0x126e4, 0x126f4,
];

const EXPECTED_DEST = [
  432, 0, 384, 320, 96,
  208, 16, 224, 80, 192,
  464, 336, 304, 144, 416,
  32, 272, 160, 368, 128,
  256,
  48, 448, 400, 288, 432,
  176, 352, 112, 240,
];

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

function hex(n: number, width = 6) {
  return n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readU32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    const b = buf[pos++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: pos };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("ULEB overflow");
    }
  }

  throw new Error("EOF in ULEB");
}

function readI32Leb(pos: number): { value: number; next: number } {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let byte = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    byte = buf[pos++];
    value |= (byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error("SLEB overflow");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (-1 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: pos };
}

function readI64Leb(pos: number): { value: bigint; next: number } {
  let value = 0n;
  let shift = 0n;
  let byte = 0;

  while (pos < buf.length) {
    byte = buf[pos++];
    value |= BigInt(byte & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7n;

    if ((byte & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 70n) {
      throw new Error("I64 SLEB overflow");
    }
  }

  if (shift < 64n && (byte & 0x40)) {
    value |= (-1n) << shift;
  }

  return { value, next: pos };
}

function readU32(pos: number) {
  return buf.readUInt32LE(pos);
}

function readU64(pos: number) {
  return buf.readBigUInt64LE(pos);
}

function showBytes(start: number, end: number) {
  const a: string[] = [];

  for (let p = start; p < end; p += 16) {
    const e = Math.min(p + 16, end);

    let s = "";
    for (let q = p; q < e; q++) {
      s += buf[q].toString(16).padStart(2, "0") + " ";
    }

    console.log(
      `0x${hex(p)}  ${s.padEnd(48)}`
    );
  }
}

/*
 * Lightweight forward decoder.
 *
 * Important:
 * This is NOT the Function-122 semantic emulator.
 * It only recognizes instructions whose immediate encoding
 * is unambiguous and records the stack expression.
 */

type Val = {
  text: string;
};

function pop(stack: Val[], where: number): Val {
  if (stack.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(`STACK_UNDERFLOW @ 0x${hex(where)}`);
  }

  return stack.pop()!;
}

function binary(
  stack: Val[],
  op: string,
  where: number
) {
  const b = pop(stack, where);
  const a = pop(stack, where);

  stack.push({
    text: `(${a.text}${op}${b.text})`,
  });
}

function decodeWindow(
  start: number,
  end: number
) {
  const stack: Val[] = [];

  let p = start;

  while (p < end) {
    const at = p;
    const op = buf[p++];

    try {
      switch (op) {
        case 0x20: { // local.get
          const x = readU32Leb(p);
          p = x.next;

          stack.push({
            text: `L${x.value}`,
          });

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} local.get ${x.value}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x21: { // local.set
          const x = readU32Leb(p);
          p = x.next;

          const v = pop(stack, at);

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} local.set ${x.value} = ${v.text}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x22: { // local.tee
          const x = readU32Leb(p);
          p = x.next;

          const v = pop(stack, at);

          stack.push(v);

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} local.tee ${x.value} = ${v.text}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x41: { // i32.const
          const x = readI32Leb(p);
          p = x.next;

          stack.push({
            text: `I32CONST(${x.value})`,
          });

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} i32.const ${x.value}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x42: { // i64.const
          const x = readI64Leb(p);
          p = x.next;

          stack.push({
            text: `I64CONST(${x.value})`,
          });

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} i64.const ${x.value}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x28: { // i32.load
          const align = readU32Leb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const off = readU32Leb(p);
          p = off.next;

          const addr = pop(stack, at);

          stack.push({
            text: `MEM32[${addr.text}+${off.value}]`,
          });

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} i32.load offset=${off.value}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x29: { // i64.load
          const align = readU32Leb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const off = readU32Leb(p);
          p = off.next;

          const addr = pop(stack, at);

          stack.push({
            text: `MEM64[${addr.text}+${off.value}]`,
          });

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} i64.load offset=${off.value}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x36: { // i32.store
          const align = readU32Leb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const off = readU32Leb(p);
          p = off.next;

          const value = pop(stack, at);
          const addr = pop(stack, at);

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} i32.store offset=${off.value}`,
            `ADDR=${addr.text}`,
            `VALUE=${value.text}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x37: { // i64.store
          const align = readU32Leb(p);
          p = align.next;

          const off = readU32Leb(p);
          p = off.next;

          const value = pop(stack, at);
          const addr = pop(stack, at);

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} i64.store offset=${off.value}`,
            `ADDR=${addr.text}`,
            `VALUE=${value.text}`
          );
          break;
        }

        case 0x6a:
          binary(stack, "+", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i32.add`);
          break;

        case 0x6b:
          binary(stack, "-", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i32.sub`);
          break;

        case 0x7c:
          binary(stack, "+", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.add`);
          break;

        case 0x7d:
          binary(stack, "-", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.sub`);
          break;

        case 0x7e:
          binary(stack, "*", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.mul`);
          break;

        case 0x7f:
          binary(stack, "/", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.div_s`);
          break;

        case 0x80:
          binary(stack, "/u", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.div_u`);
          break;

        case 0x81:
          binary(stack, "%", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.rem_s`);
          break;

        case 0x82:
          binary(stack, "%u", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.rem_u`);
          break;

        case 0x83:
          binary(stack, "&", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.and`);
          break;

        case 0x84:
          binary(stack, "|", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.or`);
          break;

        case 0x85:
          binary(stack, "^", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.xor`);
          break;

        case 0x86:
          binary(stack, "<<", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.shl`);
          break;

        case 0x87:
          binary(stack, ">>s", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.shr_s`);
          break;

        case 0x88:
          binary(stack, ">>u", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.shr_u`);
          break;

        case 0x89:
          binary(stack, "rotl", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.rotl`);
          break;

        case 0x8a:
          binary(stack, "rotr", at);
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} i64.rotr`);
          break;

        case 0x10: { // call
          const f = readU32Leb(p);
          p = f.next;

          console.log(
            `0x${hex(at)} CALL ${f.value}`
          );

          /*
           * Function 87 has five i64 arguments:
           *
           * destination
           * A
           * arg2
           * B
           * arg4
           *
           * The exact type order was established separately.
           */
          if (f.value === 87) {
            const arg4 = pop(stack, at);
            const B = pop(stack, at);
            const arg2 = pop(stack, at);
            const A = pop(stack, at);
            const dest = pop(stack, at);

            console.log(`  DEST = ${dest.text}`);
            console.log(`  A    = ${A.text}`);
            console.log(`  B    = ${B.text}`);
            console.log(`  arg2 = ${arg2.text}`);
            console.log(`  arg4 = ${arg4.text}`);

            console.log(
              `  WRITE LOW  = MEM64[${dest.text}+0]`
            );

            console.log(
              `  WRITE HIGH = MEM64[${dest.text}+8]`
            );
          }

          break;
        }

        case 0x0b:
          console.log(`0x${hex(at)} END`);
          return;

        default:
          throw new Error(
            `UNKNOWN_OPCODE 0x${op.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")}`
          );
      }

    } catch (e) {
      console.log(
        `TRACE BARRIER @ 0x${hex(at)} : ${(e as Error).message}`
      );

      /*
       * Do not attempt to continue symbolic stack through
       * an unknown instruction.
       */
      stack.length = 0;

      /*
       * We stop this individual window.
       */
      return;
    }
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALL 87 WINDOW VERIFIER v1");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\verifyCall87WindowsV2.ts
