import fs from "fs";

const WASM_PATH =
  "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const CALLS = [
  [0x0122c4, 432],
  [0x0122dc, 0],
  [0x0122f6, 384],
  [0x012310, 320],
  [0x01232c, 96],
  [0x012346, 208],
  [0x012358, 16],
  [0x01236b, 224],
  [0x01237b, 80],
  [0x01238b, 192],
  [0x01239b, 464],
  [0x0123ae, 336],
  [0x0123be, 304],
  [0x0123ce, 144],
  [0x0123de, 416],
  [0x0123f0, 32],
  [0x012403, 272],
  [0x012416, 160],
  [0x01242b, 368],
  [0x012440, 128],
  [0x012455, 256],
  [0x012675, 48],
  [0x012685, 448],
  [0x012695, 400],
  [0x0126a5, 288],
  [0x0126b4, 432],
  [0x0126c4, 176],
  [0x0126d4, 352],
  [0x0126e4, 112],
  [0x0126f4, 240],
] as const;

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

function hex(n: number, width = 6) {
  return "0x" + n.toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readULEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    const b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
      return { value: value >>> 0, next: p };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`ULEB too large @ ${hex(pos)}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error(`EOF in ULEB @ ${hex(pos)}`);
}

function readSLEB(pos: number) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let p = pos;
  let b = 0;

  while (p < wasm.length) {
    b = wasm[p++];
    value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
    shift += 7;

    if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

    if (shift > 35) {
      throw new Error(`SLEB too large @ ${hex(pos)}`);
    }
  }

  if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
    value |= (~0 << shift);
  }

  return { value: value | 0, next: p };
}

function readMemarg(pos: number) {
  const align = readULEB(pos);
  const offset = readULEB(align.next);

  return {
    align: align.value,
    offset: offset.value,
    next: offset.next,
  };
}

function opcodeName(op: number): string {
  const names: Record<number, string> = {
    0x0b: "end",
    0x10: "call",
    0x20: "local.get",
    0x21: "local.set",
    0x22: "local.tee",
    0x41: "i32.const",
    0x42: "i64.const",

    0x29: "i64.load",
    0x37: "i64.store",

    0x6a: "i32.add",
    0x6b: "i32.sub",

    0x7c: "i64.add",
    0x7d: "i64.sub",
    0x7e: "i64.mul",
    0x7f: "i64.div_s",
    0x80: "i64.div_u",
    0x81: "i64.rem_s",
    0x82: "i64.rem_u",
    0x83: "i64.and",
    0x84: "i64.or",
    0x85: "i64.xor",
    0x86: "i64.shl",
    0x87: "i64.shr_s",
    0x88: "i64.shr_u",
    0x89: "i64.rotl",
    0x8a: "i64.rotr",
  };

  return names[op] ?? `opcode_${hex(op, 2)}`;
}

function decodeOne(pos: number) {
  const start = pos;
  const op = wasm[pos++];

  switch (op) {
    case 0x20:
    case 0x21:
    case 0x22: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);
      return {
        start,
        next: x.next,
        text: `${opcodeName(op)} ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x41: {
      const x = readSLEB(pos);
      return {
        start,
        next: x.next,
        text: `i32.const ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x42: {
      /*
       * For this verifier we only need to identify i64.const.
       * Read signed LEB byte-wise.
       */
      let p = pos;
      let shift = 0n;
      let value = 0n;
      let b = 0;

      while (p < wasm.length) {
        b = wasm[p++];
        value |= BigInt(b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) break;

        shift += 7n;
      }

      return {
        start,
        next: p,
        text: `i64.const ${value.toString()}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x29:
    case 0x37: {
      const m = readMemarg(pos);

      return {
        start,
        next: m.next,
        text:
          `${opcodeName(op)} align=${m.align} offset=${m.offset}`,
      };
    }

    case 0x6a:
    case 0x6b:
    case 0x7c:
    case 0x7d:
    case 0x7e:
    case 0x7f:
    case 0x80:
    case 0x81:
    case 0x82:
    case 0x83:
    case 0x84:
    case 0x85:
    case 0x86:
    case 0x87:
    case 0x88:
    case 0x89:
    case 0x8a:
    case 0x0b:
      return {
        start,
        next: pos,
        text: opcodeName(op),
      };

    case 0x10: {
      const x = readULEB(pos);

      return {
        start,
        next: x.next,
        text: `call ${x.value}`,
      };
    }

    default:
      return {
        start,
        next: pos,
        text: `UNKNOWN ${hex(op, 2)}`,
      };
  }
}

/*
 * We know the CALL position exactly.
 *
 * Instead of pretending that the preceding bytes form an independent
 * function, locate instruction boundaries by trying candidate starts
 * and require exact decoding to end at the CALL byte.
 */
function findInstructionBoundary(callPos: number, maxBack = 48) {
  for (
    let candidate = Math.max(0, callPos - maxBack);
    candidate < callPos;
    candidate++
  ) {
    let p = candidate;
    let count = 0;

    try {
      while (p < callPos && count < 32) {
        const d = decodeOne(p);

        if (d.next <= p || d.next > callPos) {
          break;
        }

        p = d.next;
        count++;
      }

      if (p === callPos) {
        return candidate;
      }
    } catch {
      // try next candidate
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function printWindow(callPos: number) {
  const start = Math.max(0, callPos - 24);

  console.log(`RAW ${hex(start)} .. ${hex(callPos + 4)}`);

  for (let p = start; p < callPos + 4;) {
    const d = decodeOne(p);

    const bytes = Array.from(
      wasm.subarray(p, d.next)
    )
      .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0"))
      .join(" ");

    console.log(
      `${hex(p)}  ${bytes.padEnd(24)}  ${d.text}`
    );

    if (d.next <= p) break;
    p = d.next;
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" CALL 87 WINDOW VERIFIER v2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log();

let passed = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < CALLS.length; i++) {
  const [callPos, expectedDest] = CALLS[i];

  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
  console.log(
    `CALL #${i + 1} @ ${hex(callPos)}`
  );
  console.log(
    `EXPECTED DEST OFFSET = L2 + ${expectedDest}`
  );
  console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

  if (wasm[callPos] !== 0x10) {
    console.log(
      `FAIL: byte @ CALL position = ${hex(wasm[callPos], 2)}, expected 0x10`
    );
    console.log();
    continue;
  }

  const target = readULEB(callPos + 1);

  console.log(
    `CALL TARGET = ${target.value}`
  );

  if (target.value !== 87) {
    console.log(
      `FAIL: expected call target 87`
    );
    console.log();
    continue;
  }

  const boundary = findInstructionBoundary(callPos);

  if (boundary === null) {
    console.log(
      `BOUNDARY: NOT FOUND within 48 bytes`
    );
  } else {
    console.log(
      `BOUNDARY: ${hex(boundary)}`
    );
  }

  /*
   * Print only the raw bytes and correctly parsed instructions.
   * This is deliberately independent of symbolic stack tracking.
   */
  printWindow(callPos);

  /*
   * Direct byte-level verification of the CALL itself.
   */
  console.log();
  console.log("CALL BYTE CHECK:");
  console.log(
    `  opcode      = ${hex(wasm[callPos], 2)}  ${wasm[callPos] === 0x10 ? "OK" : "FAIL"}`
  );
  console.log(
    `  target      = ${target.value}  ${target.value === 87 ? "OK" : "FAIL"}`
  );

  /*
   * Expected call encoding:
   *   10 57
   */
  if (
    wasm[callPos] === 0x10 &&
    wasm[callPos + 1] === 0x57
  ) {
    console.log("  encoding    = 10 57  OK");
    passed++;
  } else {
    console.log("  encoding    = FAIL");
  }

  console.log();
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SUMMARY");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`CALL87 verified : ${passed}/${CALLS.length}`);

if (passed === CALLS.length) {
  console.log();
  console.log("RESULT: ALL 30 CALL SITES ARE BYTE-EXACT CALL 87.");
}

console.log();
console.log("DONE - NO TRANSACTION / NO NETWORK ACCESS");

  if (buf[i] === 0x10 && buf[i + 1] === 0x60) {

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

const TARGETS = [
  0x50098050,
  0x50098054
];

function hex(v, width = 8) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(width, "0");
}

function readSLEB32(pos) {
  let value = 0;
  let shift = 0;
  let bytes = 0;

  while (pos + bytes < b.length) {
    const x = b[pos + bytes];

    value |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    bytes++;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) {
      shift += 7;

      // Sign extension for i32
      if (shift < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
        value |= (~0 << shift);
      }

      return {
        signed: value | 0,
        unsigned: value >>> 0,
        bytes
      };
    }

    shift += 7;

    if (shift > 35) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("VERIFY 0x50098050 / 0x50098054 I32.CONST");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`FILE SIZE : ${b.length}`);
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

let totalHits = 0;

for (let i = 8; i < b.length; i++) {

  // i32.const
  if (b[i] !== 0x41) {
    continue;
  }

  const r = readSLEB32(i + 1);

  if (!r) {
    continue;
  }

  if (!TARGETS.includes(r.unsigned)) {
    continue;
  }

  totalHits++;

  console.log("");
  console.log(`HIT @ ${hex(i)}`);
  console.log(`  OPCODE       : 0x41`);
  console.log(`  SIGNED       : ${r.signed}`);
  console.log(`  UNSIGNED     : ${r.unsigned}`);
  console.log(`  HEX          : ${hex(r.unsigned)}`);
  console.log(`  ULEB/SLEB BYTES : ${r.bytes}`);

  const start = Math.max(0, i - 12);
  const end = Math.min(
    b.length,
    i + 1 + r.bytes + 12
  );

  console.log("  RAW CONTEXT:");

  for (let j = start; j < end; j += 8) {

    const lineEnd = Math.min(end, j + 8);

    const bytes = [];

    for (let k = j; k < lineEnd; k++) {
      bytes.push(
        b[k].toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
      );
    }

    console.log(
      `    ${hex(j)}  ${bytes.join(" ")}`
    );
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
console.log(`TOTAL TARGET HITS : ${totalHits}`);

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET SUMMARY");

for (const target of TARGETS) {
  console.log(
    `  ${hex(target)}`
  );
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("VERIFY DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\verifyDispatchTable.mjs

import fs from "fs";

const file = "emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";
const b = fs.readFileSync(file);

function uleb(pos) {
  let v = 0;
  let shift = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[pos++];
    v |= (x & 0x7f) << shift;
    if (!(x & 0x80)) return { value: v >>> 0, next: pos };
    shift += 7;
  }
}

function skipInitExpr(pos) {
  const opcode = b[pos++];

  // i32.const
  if (opcode === 0x41) {
    const x = uleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;
  }
  // i64.const
  else if (opcode === 0x42) {
    const x = uleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;
  }
  // global.get
  else if (opcode === 0x23) {
    const x = uleb(pos);
    pos = x.next;
  }
  else {
    throw new Error(
      `Unknown init-expr opcode 0x${opcode.toString(16)} at 0x${(pos - 1).toString(16)}`
    );
  }

  if (b[pos++] !== 0x0b)
    throw new Error("Missing init-expr END");

  return pos;
}

let p = 8;
let tableCount = 0;
let found = new Map();

while (p < b.length) {
  const sectionId = b[p++];

  const s = uleb(p);
  const sectionSize = s.value;
  const sectionStart = s.next;
  const sectionEnd = sectionStart + sectionSize;

  if (sectionId === 9) {
    let q = sectionStart;

    const n = uleb(q);
    q = n.next;

    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log("ELEMENT SECTION RAW RESOLVER");
    console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
    console.log("============================================================");
    console.log(`ELEMENT SEGMENTS : ${n.value}`);
    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

    for (let seg = 0; seg < n.value; seg++) {
      const flag = uleb(q);
      q = flag.next;

      let tableIndex = 0;
      let active = true;

      /*
       Element segment flags:

       0 = active, table 0, elemkind omitted
       1 = passive, elemkind omitted
       2 = active, explicit table, elemkind omitted
       3 = declarative, elemkind omitted
       4 = active, expressions
       5 = passive, expressions
       6 = active explicit table, expressions
       7 = declarative, expressions
      */

      if (flag.value === 0) {
        q = skipInitExpr(q);
      }
      else if (flag.value === 1) {
        active = false;
      }
      else if (flag.value === 2) {
        const ti = uleb(q);
        tableIndex = ti.value;
        q = ti.next;
        q = skipInitExpr(q);
      }
      else if (flag.value === 3) {
        active = false;
      }
      else if (flag.value === 4) {
        q = skipInitExpr(q);
      }
      else if (flag.value === 5) {
        active = false;
      }
      else if (flag.value === 6) {
        const ti = uleb(q);
        tableIndex = ti.value;
        q = ti.next;
        q = skipInitExpr(q);
      }
      else if (flag.value === 7) {
        active = false;
      }
      else {
        throw new Error(`Unknown element flag ${flag.value}`);
      }

      let exprElements = false;

      if (flag.value >= 4)
        exprElements = true;

      if (!exprElements) {
        /*
         For flags 0/1/2/3:
         element type is funcref only for legacy encoding
         OR omitted in the active/passive forms depending on flag.
        */

        if (flag.value === 0 || flag.value === 1 || flag.value === 2) {
          // elemkind
          q++;
        }

        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
          const f = uleb(q);
          q = f.next;

          const absoluteIndex =
            (() => {
              /*
               Active segments are placed at their declared offset.
               For this resolver we collect only if offset was a constant.
              */
              return f.value;
            })();

          if (
            absoluteIndex === 4788 ||
            absoluteIndex === 4789 ||
            absoluteIndex === 4790 ||
            absoluteIndex === 4791
          ) {
            found.set(absoluteIndex, {
              func: f.value,
              segment: seg,
              tableIndex,
              elementPosition: i,
              count: count.value
            });
          }
        }
      }
      else {
        /*
         Expression element format:
         element type / ref type then expressions.
        */
        const refType = b[q++];

        const count = uleb(q);
        q = count.next;

        for (let i = 0; i < count.value; i++) {
          const exprOpcode = b[q++];

          if (exprOpcode !== 0xd2)
            throw new Error(
              `Expected ref.func, got 0x${exprOpcode.toString(16)}`
            );

          const f = uleb(q);
          q = f.next;

          if (b[q++] !== 0x0b)
            throw new Error("Missing expression END");

          if (
            i === 4788 ||
            i === 4789 ||
            i === 4790 ||
            i === 4791
          ) {
            found.set(i, {
              func: f.value,
              segment: seg,
              tableIndex,
              elementPosition: i,
              count: count.value,
              refType
            });
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  p = sectionEnd;
}

console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");

for (const index of [4788, 4789, 4790, 4791]) {
  const x = found.get(index);

  if (x) {
    console.log(
      `TABLE[${index}] -> FUNC ${x.func} ` +
      `(segment=${x.segment}, position=${x.elementPosition})`
    );
  } else {
    console.log(`TABLE[${index}] -> NOT FOUND`);
  }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");

FILE: scripts\verifyElementTable.js
====================================================================================================
const fs = require("fs");

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

function readU32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return {
                value: value >>> 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function readS32(buf, p) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            if (shift < 32 && (b & 0x40)) {
                value |= (-1 << (shift + 7));
            }

            return {
                value: value | 0,
                next: p
            };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function getSections(buf) {
    const sections = [];

    let p = 8;

    while (p < buf.length) {
        const id = buf[p++];

        const sizeInfo = readU32(buf, p);
        p = sizeInfo.next;

        const start = p;
        const end = start + sizeInfo.value;

        sections.push({
            id,
            start,
            end,
            size: sizeInfo.value
        });

        p = end;
    }

    return sections;
}

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("VERIFY ELEMENT TABLE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log("WASM SIZE :", wasm.length);
console.log();

const sections = getSections(wasm);

const element = sections.find(x => x.id === 9);

if (!element) {
    throw new Error("Element section not found");
}

console.log(
    "ELEMENT SECTION :",
    `0x${element.start.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`,
    "..",
    `0x${(element.end - 1).toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
);

console.log("ELEMENT SIZE :", element.size);

let p = element.start;

const segmentInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
p = segmentInfo.next;

const segmentCount = segmentInfo.value;

console.log("SEGMENT COUNT :", segmentCount);
console.log();

const table = [];

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

    const flagsInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = flagsInfo.next;

    const flags = flagsInfo.value;

    console.log("SEGMENT", seg, "FLAGS =", flags);

    let tableIndex = 0;

    if (flags === 0) {

        const opcode = wasm[p++];

        console.log(
            "OFFSET OPCODE :",
            "0x" + opcode.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
        );

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                `Expected i32.const, got 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const offsetInfo = readS32(wasm, p);
        p = offsetInfo.next;

        tableIndex = offsetInfo.value;

        const end = wasm[p++];

        if (end !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error("Invalid offset expression end");
        }

    } else if (flags === 2) {

        const tableInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
        p = tableInfo.next;

        console.log("TABLE INDEX :", tableInfo.value);

        const opcode = wasm[p++];

        if (opcode !== 0x41) {
            throw new Error(
                `Expected i32.const, got 0x${opcode.toString(16)}`
            );
        }

        const offsetInfo = readS32(wasm, p);
        p = offsetInfo.next;

        tableIndex = offsetInfo.value;

        const end = wasm[p++];

        if (end !== 0x0b) {
            throw new Error("Invalid offset expression end");
        }

    } else {

        throw new Error(
            `Unsupported element flags ${flags} at segment ${seg}`
        );
    }

    console.log("TABLE OFFSET :", tableIndex);

    const elemType = wasm[p++];

    console.log(
        "ELEMENT TYPE :",
        "0x" + elemType.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")
    );

    const countInfo = readU32(wasm, p);
    p = countInfo.next;

    const count = countInfo.value;

    console.log("FUNCTION COUNT :", count);
    console.log();

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

        const pos = p;

        const f = readU32(wasm, p);
        p = f.next;

        const index = tableIndex + i;

        table[index] = {
            functionIndex: f.value,
            rawPosition: pos
        };
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("TABLE VALIDATION");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log();

console.log("TABLE ENTRIES :", table.length);

const maxIndex = table.length - 1;

console.log("MAX TABLE INDEX :", maxIndex);

console.log();

const targets = [579, 1209, 2053];

for (const index of targets) {

    const e = table[index];

    if (!e) {
        console.log(
            `TABLE[${index}] : MISSING`
        );
        continue;
    }

    console.log(
        `TABLE[${index}] -> FUNC ${e.functionIndex} ` +
        `RAW=0x${e.rawPosition.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
    );
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("SELECTED RAW NEIGHBORHOODS");
console.log("============================================================");

for (const center of targets) {

    console.log();
    console.log(`AROUND TABLE[${center}]`);

    for (
        let i = Math.max(0, center - 5);
        i <= Math.min(maxIndex, center + 5);
        i++
    ) {

        const e = table[i];

        if (!e) continue;

        console.log(
            `TABLE[${String(i).padStart(5)}] -> ` +
            `FUNC ${String(e.functionIndex).padStart(5)} ` +
            `RAW=0x${e.rawPosition.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
    }
}

console.log();

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log("EXPECTED TABLE ENTRIES : 8992");
console.log("ACTUAL TABLE ENTRIES   :", table.length);

if (table.length !== 8992) {
    console.log();
    console.log("WARNING:");
    console.log("ELEMENT TABLE PARSING DOES NOT MATCH EXPECTED 8992.");
    console.log("DO NOT USE THIS RESULT FOR DISPATCH ANALYSIS.");
} else {
    console.log();
    console.log("ELEMENT TABLE COUNT VERIFIED.");
}

console.log();
console.log("READ ONLY : YES");
console.log("NETWORK : NO");
console.log("TRANSACTIONS : NO");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\verifyFinalTonConnectRequestV22.ts

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const USER_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const POOL_PTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const ROOSTER_AMOUNT = 100_000_000_000n;
const PTON_AMOUNT = 249_908n;

const ROOSTER_VALUE = 300_000_000n;
const PTON_VALUE = 310_249_908n;

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150_109_265n;

const PROVIDE_LP = 0x37c096df;
const JETTON_TRANSFER = 0x0f8a7ea5;
const TON_TRANSFER = 0x01f3835d;

const NETWORK = -3;

function sameAddress(a: Address, b: Address): boolean {
    return a.equals(b);
}

function pass(name: string) {
    console.log(`PASS  ${name}`);
}

function fail(name: string, reason: string): never {
    throw new Error(`${name}: ${reason}`);
}

function readAddress(
    slice: ReturnType<Cell['beginParse']>,
    name: string
): Address {
    const address = slice.loadAddress();

    if (!address) {
        fail(name, 'Address is null.');
    }

    return address;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 FINAL TONCONNECT REQUEST VERIFIER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('================================================================');

    const wallet = provider.sender().address;

    if (!wallet) {
        fail('Wallet', 'Connected wallet unavailable.');
    }

    console.log('');
    console.log('CONNECTED WALLET:');
    console.log(wallet.toString());

    if (!sameAddress(wallet, USER)) {
        fail(
            'Wallet',
            `Expected ${USER.toString()}, got ${wallet.toString()}`
        );
    }

    pass('Wallet identity');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * FRESH DEADLINE
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
    const deadline = now + 600;

    console.log('');
    console.log('CURRENT UNIX:', now);
    console.log('DEADLINE:', deadline);

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * SDK OBJECTS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const router = provider.open(
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER)
    );

    const proxyTon = DEX.v2_1.pTON.create(PTON_MASTER);

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * BUILD EXACT SAME TWO TX PARAMS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            otherTokenAddress: PTON_MASTER,
            sendAmount: ROOSTER_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            deadline,
        });

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams({
            userWalletAddress: USER,
            otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER_MASTER,
            sendAmount: PTON_AMOUNT,
            minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
            receiverAddress: USER,
            refundAddress: USER,
            excessesAddress: USER,
            deadline,
            proxyTon,
        });

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
        fail('ROOSTER body', 'Missing body.');
    }

    if (!ptonTx.body) {
        fail('pTON body', 'Missing body.');
    }

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * OUTER TRANSACTION CHECKS
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('OUTER TRANSACTIONS');

    if (!sameAddress(roosterTx.to, USER_ROOSTER_WALLET)) {
        fail('ROOSTER destination', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER destination');

    if (!sameAddress(ptonTx.to, POOL_PTON_WALLET)) {
        fail('pTON destination', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON destination');

    if (BigInt(roosterTx.value) !== ROOSTER_VALUE) {
        fail('ROOSTER value', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER value');

    if (BigInt(ptonTx.value) !== PTON_VALUE) {
        fail('pTON value', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON value');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * SERIALIZE
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const roosterBoc = roosterTx.body.toBoc();
    const ptonBoc = ptonTx.body.toBoc();

    console.log('');
    console.log('BOC SIZES');
    console.log('ROOSTER:', roosterBoc.length);
    console.log('pTON:', ptonBoc.length);

    if (roosterBoc.length !== 264) {
        fail('ROOSTER BOC size', 'Expected 264 bytes.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER BOC size');

    if (ptonBoc.length !== 224) {
        fail('pTON BOC size', 'Expected 224 bytes.');
    }

    pass('pTON BOC size');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * ROOSTER JETTON TRANSFER
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER PAYLOAD');

    const roosterSlice = roosterTx.body.beginParse();

    const roosterOpcode = roosterSlice.loadUint(32);

    if (roosterOpcode !== JETTON_TRANSFER) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER opcode',
            `Expected 0x${JETTON_TRANSFER.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER JettonTransfer opcode');

    const roosterQueryId = roosterSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('ROOSTER queryId:', roosterQueryId.toString());

    const roosterAmount = roosterSlice.loadCoins();

    if (roosterAmount !== ROOSTER_AMOUNT) {
        fail('ROOSTER amount', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER amount');

    const roosterDestination =
        readAddress(roosterSlice, 'ROOSTER destination address');

    if (!sameAddress(roosterDestination, ROUTER)) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER internal destination',
            `Expected router ${ROUTER}, got ${roosterDestination}`
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER internal destination = ROUTER');

    const roosterResponse =
        readAddress(roosterSlice, 'ROOSTER response address');

    if (!sameAddress(roosterResponse, USER)) {
        fail('ROOSTER response address', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER response address');

    /*
     * custom_payload
     */
    const hasCustomPayload = roosterSlice.loadBit();

    if (hasCustomPayload) {
        roosterSlice.loadRef();
    }

    const roosterForwardTon = roosterSlice.loadCoins();

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER forward TON:',
        roosterForwardTon.toString()
    );

    const roosterForwardPayload =
        roosterSlice.loadRef();

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const roosterLpSlice =
        roosterForwardPayload.beginParse();

    const roosterLpOpcode =
        roosterLpSlice.loadUint(32);

    if (roosterLpOpcode !== PROVIDE_LP) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            `Expected 0x${PROVIDE_LP.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER PROVIDE_LP opcode');

    const roosterRouterWallet =
        readAddress(
            roosterLpSlice,
            'ROOSTER router wallet'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(roosterRouterWallet, POOL_PTON_WALLET)) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER router wallet',
            `Expected ${POOL_PTON_WALLET}, got ${roosterRouterWallet}`
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER router wallet');

    const roosterRefund =
        readAddress(
            roosterLpSlice,
            'ROOSTER refund address'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(roosterRefund, USER)) {
        fail('ROOSTER refund address', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER refund address');

    const roosterExcesses =
        readAddress(
            roosterLpSlice,
            'ROOSTER excesses address'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(roosterExcesses, USER)) {
        fail('ROOSTER excesses address', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER excesses address');

    const roosterDeadline =
        roosterLpSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    if (roosterDeadline !== BigInt(deadline)) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER deadline',
            `Expected ${deadline}, got ${roosterDeadline}`
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER deadline');

    const roosterLpRef =
        roosterLpSlice.loadRef().beginParse();

    const roosterMinLp =
        roosterLpRef.loadCoins();

    if (roosterMinLp !== MIN_LP_OUT) {
        fail('ROOSTER minLpOut', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER minLpOut');

    const roosterReceiver =
        readAddress(
            roosterLpRef,
            'ROOSTER receiver'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(roosterReceiver, USER)) {
        fail('ROOSTER receiver', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('ROOSTER receiver');

    const roosterBothPositive =
        roosterLpRef.loadUint(1);

    if (roosterBothPositive !== 1) {
        fail(
            'ROOSTER bothPositive',
            `Expected 1, got ${roosterBothPositive}`
        );
    }

    pass('ROOSTER bothPositive');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * pTON TON_TRANSFER
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON PAYLOAD');

    const ptonSlice = ptonTx.body.beginParse();

    const ptonOpcode =
        ptonSlice.loadUint(32);

    if (ptonOpcode !== TON_TRANSFER) {
        fail(
            'pTON opcode',
            `Expected 0x${TON_TRANSFER.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    pass('pTON TON_TRANSFER opcode');

    const ptonQueryId =
        ptonSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    console.log('pTON queryId:', ptonQueryId.toString());

    const ptonAmount =
        ptonSlice.loadCoins();

    if (ptonAmount !== PTON_AMOUNT) {
        fail('pTON amount', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON amount');

    const ptonRefund =
        readAddress(
            ptonSlice,
            'pTON refund address'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(ptonRefund, USER)) {
        fail('pTON refund address', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON refund address');

    const hasForwardPayload =
        ptonSlice.loadBit();

    if (!hasForwardPayload) {
        fail(
            'pTON forward payload',
            'Expected referenced forward payload.'
        );
    }

    const ptonForwardPayload =
        ptonSlice.loadRef();

    pass('pTON forward payload');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * pTON PROVIDE_LP PAYLOAD
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const ptonLpSlice =
        ptonForwardPayload.beginParse();

    const ptonLpOpcode =
        ptonLpSlice.loadUint(32);

    if (ptonLpOpcode !== PROVIDE_LP) {
        fail(
            'pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode',
            `Expected 0x${PROVIDE_LP.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    pass('pTON PROVIDE_LP opcode');

    const ptonRouterWallet =
        readAddress(
            ptonLpSlice,
            'pTON router wallet'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(
    ptonRouterWallet,
    POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET
)) {
       fail(
    'pTON router wallet',
    `Expected ${POOL_ROOSTER_WALLET}, got ${ptonRouterWallet}`
);
    }

    pass('pTON router wallet');

    const ptonLpRefund =
        readAddress(
            ptonLpSlice,
            'pTON LP refund'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(ptonLpRefund, USER)) {
        fail('pTON LP refund', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON LP refund');

    const ptonLpExcesses =
        readAddress(
            ptonLpSlice,
            'pTON LP excesses'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(ptonLpExcesses, USER)) {
        fail('pTON LP excesses', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON LP excesses');

    const ptonDeadline =
        ptonLpSlice.loadUintBig(64);

    if (ptonDeadline !== BigInt(deadline)) {
        fail(
            'pTON deadline',
            `Expected ${deadline}, got ${ptonDeadline}`
        );
    }

    pass('pTON deadline');

    const ptonLpRef =
        ptonLpSlice.loadRef().beginParse();

    const ptonMinLp =
        ptonLpRef.loadCoins();

    if (ptonMinLp !== MIN_LP_OUT) {
        fail('pTON minLpOut', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON minLpOut');

    const ptonReceiver =
        readAddress(
            ptonLpRef,
            'pTON receiver'
        );

    if (!sameAddress(ptonReceiver, USER)) {
        fail('pTON receiver', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('pTON receiver');

    const ptonBothPositive =
        ptonLpRef.loadUint(1);

    if (ptonBothPositive !== 1) {
        fail(
            'pTON bothPositive',
            `Expected 1, got ${ptonBothPositive}`
        );
    }

    pass('pTON bothPositive');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * CROSS-LEG CONSISTENCY
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('CROSS-LEG CONSISTENCY');

    if (roosterDeadline !== ptonDeadline) {
        fail('Deadline consistency', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('Same deadline');

    if (roosterMinLp !== ptonMinLp) {
        fail('minLpOut consistency', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('Same minLpOut');

    if (!sameAddress(roosterReceiver, ptonReceiver)) {
        fail('Receiver consistency', 'Mismatch.');
    }

    pass('Same receiver');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * FINAL TONCONNECT OBJECT
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    const request = {
        validUntil: deadline,
        network: NETWORK,
        messages: [
            {
                address: roosterTx.to.toString(),
                amount: roosterTx.value.toString(),
                payload: roosterBoc.toString('base64'),
            },
            {
                address: ptonTx.to.toString(),
                amount: ptonTx.value.toString(),
                payload: ptonBoc.toString('base64'),
            },
        ],
    };

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL TONCONNECT STRUCTURE');
    console.log('================================================================');

    console.log('network:', request.network);
    console.log('validUntil:', request.validUntil);
    console.log('message count:', request.messages.length);

    if (request.network !== -3) {
        fail('TonConnect network', 'Not testnet.');
    }

    if (request.messages.length !== 2) {
        fail('TonConnect messages', 'Expected exactly 2.');
    }

    if (request.validUntil !== deadline) {
        fail('TonConnect deadline', 'Does not match BOC deadline.');
    }

    pass('TonConnect network');
    pass('TonConnect message count');
    pass('TonConnect deadline = BOC deadline');

    /*
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     * NO SEND
     * ------------------------------------------------------------
     */

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' SAFETY');
    console.log('================================================================');

    console.log('connector.sendTransaction(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('sender.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('wallet.send(): NOT CALLED');
    console.log('Tonkeeper approval: NOT REQUESTED');
    console.log('Transaction broadcast: NO');
    console.log('Wallet state change: NO');
    console.log('Pool state change: NO');

    console.log('');
    console.log('================================================================');
    console.log(' STATUS');
    console.log('================================================================');

    console.log('FINAL TONCONNECT REQUEST: VERIFIED');
    console.log('ALL BOC FIELDS: PASS');
    console.log('CROSS-LEG CONSISTENCY: PASS');
    console.log('STATUS: READ-ONLY VERIFIED');
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\verifyFunctionMapV2.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const CODE_PAYLOAD_START = 0x6e7d;

function readULEB(offset: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `ULEB too large at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION MAP VERIFIER V2");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log(`Code payload start : 0x${CODE_PAYLOAD_START.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`Import count       : ${IMPORT_COUNT}`);
console.log("");

let p = CODE_PAYLOAD_START;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`Function bodies : ${functionCount}`);
console.log("");

let found205 = false;
let found122 = false;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionCount; bodyIndex++) {
    const bodyStart = p;

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);
    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;
    const codeStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > b.length) {
        throw new Error(
            `Body exceeds file at body #${bodyIndex}: ` +
            `0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const realIndex = IMPORT_COUNT + bodyIndex;

    if (
        realIndex === 122 ||
        realIndex === 205 ||
        bodyIndex === 0 ||
        bodyIndex === 39 ||
        bodyIndex === 122
    ) {
        console.log(
            `body #${bodyIndex.toString().padStart(4, " ")} ` +
            `real=${realIndex.toString().padStart(4, " ")} ` +
            `body=0x${bodyStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}..` +
            `0x${bodyEnd.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")} ` +
            `size=${bodySize} ` +
            `code=0x${codeStart.toString(16).padStart(8, "0")}`
        );
    }

    if (realIndex === 122) {
        found122 = true;

        if (
            bodyStart !== 0x0eec5 ||
            codeStart !== 0x0eec8 ||
            bodyEnd !== 0x0ef44
        ) {
            console.log("  !!! FUNCTION 122 MISMATCH !!!");
        } else {
            console.log("  FUNCTION 122 : VERIFIED");
        }
    }

    if (realIndex === 205) {
        found205 = true;

        if (
            bodyStart !== 0x1226b ||
            codeStart !== 0x1226d ||
            bodyEnd !== 0x1295c
        ) {
            console.log("  !!! FUNCTION 205 MISMATCH !!!");
        } else {
            console.log("  FUNCTION 205 : VERIFIED");
        }
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`Function 122 found : ${found122}`);
console.log(`Function 205 found : ${found205}`);
console.log(`Final parser offset: 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
console.log(`Code section end   : 0x324ef5`);

if (!found122 || !found205) {
    throw new Error("Required functions were not found");
}

if (p !== 0x324ef5) {
    throw new Error(
        `Code section boundary mismatch: ` +
        `parser=0x${p.toString(16)} expected=0x324ef5`
    );
}

console.log("CODE SECTION BOUNDARY : VERIFIED");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\verifyFunctionMapV3.ts
====================================================================================================
import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const b = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

const IMPORT_COUNT = 83;
const CODE_PAYLOAD_START = 0x6e7d;
const CODE_END = 0x324ef5;

function readULEB(offset: number): {
    value: number;
    next: number;
} {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = offset;

    while (true) {
        const x = b[p++];

        value += (x & 0x7f) * Math.pow(2, shift);

        if ((x & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;

        if (shift > 35) {
            throw new Error(
                `ULEB too large at 0x${offset.toString(16)}`
            );
        }
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" FUNCTION MAP VERIFIER V3");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log(`WASM size : ${b.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log("");

let p = CODE_PAYLOAD_START;

const countInfo = readULEB(p);
const functionCount = countInfo.value;
p = countInfo.next;

console.log(`Function bodies : ${functionCount}`);
console.log(`First body size field : 0x${p.toString(16)}`);
console.log("");

let found122 = false;
let found205 = false;

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < functionCount; bodyIndex++) {

    // p points to the SIZE FIELD
    const sizeField = p;

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(p);

    const bodySize = sizeInfo.value;

    // IMPORTANT:
    // bodySize excludes the size field itself.
    const codeStart = sizeInfo.next;
    const bodyEnd = codeStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > CODE_END) {
        throw new Error(
            `Body exceeds Code Section at body #${bodyIndex}: ` +
            `end=0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const realIndex = IMPORT_COUNT + bodyIndex;

    if (
        bodyIndex === 0 ||
        realIndex === 122 ||
        realIndex === 205
    ) {
        console.log(
            `body #${bodyIndex.toString().padStart(4, " ")} ` +
            `real=${realIndex.toString().padStart(4, " ")}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  size field : 0x${sizeField.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  body size  : ${bodySize} ` +
            `(0x${bodySize.toString(16)})`
        );

        console.log(
            `  code start : 0x${codeStart.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log(
            `  body end   : 0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
        );

        console.log("");
    }

    if (realIndex === 122) {
        found122 = true;

        if (
            sizeField !== 0x0eec5 ||
            codeStart !== 0x0eec8 ||
            bodyEnd !== 0x0ef44
        ) {
            console.log("  !!! FUNCTION 122 MISMATCH !!!");
        } else {
            console.log("  FUNCTION 122 : VERIFIED");
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    if (realIndex === 205) {
        found205 = true;

        if (
            sizeField !== 0x1226b ||
            codeStart !== 0x1226d ||
            bodyEnd !== 0x1295c
        ) {
            console.log("  !!! FUNCTION 205 MISMATCH !!!");
        } else {
            console.log("  FUNCTION 205 : VERIFIED");
        }

        console.log("");
    }

    // NEXT BODY:
    // exactly at the byte after this body's contents
    p = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("FINAL");
console.log("============================================================");

console.log(`Function 122 found : ${found122}`);
console.log(`Function 205 found : ${found205}`);
console.log(
    `Final parser offset: 0x${p.toString(16)}`
);
console.log(
    `Code section end   : 0x${CODE_END.toString(16)}`
);

if (!found122 || !found205) {
    throw new Error("Required functions were not found");
}

if (p !== CODE_END) {
    throw new Error(
        `Code section boundary mismatch: ` +
        `parser=0x${p.toString(16)} ` +
        `expected=0x${CODE_END.toString(16)}`
    );
}

console.log("");
console.log("CODE SECTION BOUNDARY : VERIFIED");
console.log("FUNCTION MAP : VERIFIED");

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\verifyFunctionTypesRaw.mjs
====================================================================================================

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function str(p) {
  const [n, q] = u(p);
  return [b.toString("utf8", q, q + n), q + n];
}

function valtype(x) {
  const m = {
    0x7f: "i32",
    0x7e: "i64",
    0x7d: "f32",
    0x7c: "f64",
    0x70: "funcref",
    0x6f: "externref"
  };
  return m[x] ?? `0x${x.toString(16)}`;
}

function parseType(p) {
  const form = b[p++];
  if (form !== 0x60) {
    throw new Error(`Expected functype at offset 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);
  }

  const [pc, q0] = u(p);
  p = q0;

  const params = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < pc; i++) {
    params.push(valtype(b[p++]));
  }

  const [rc, q1] = u(p);
  p = q1;

  const results = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < rc; i++) {
    results.push(valtype(b[p++]));
  }

  return [{ params, results }, p];
}

const typeEntries = [];
const importFuncTypeIdx = [];
const definedFuncTypeIdx = [];
const importFuncNames = [];

let p = 8;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (end > b.length) {
    throw new Error(`Section ${id} exceeds WASM bounds`);
  }

  // TYPE SECTION
  if (id === 1) {
    const [count, q0] = u(p);
    p = q0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const [t, q1] = parseType(p);
      p = q1;
      typeEntries.push(t);
    }
  }

  // IMPORT SECTION
  if (id === 2) {
    const [count, q0] = u(p);
    p = q0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      let moduleName, fieldName;

      [moduleName, p] = str(p);
      [fieldName, p] = str(p);

      const kind = b[p++];

      if (kind === 0) {
        const [typeIdx, q1] = u(p);
        p = q1;

        importFuncTypeIdx.push(typeIdx);
        importFuncNames.push({
          index: importFuncTypeIdx.length - 1,
          moduleName,
          fieldName,
          typeIdx
        });
      } else if (kind === 1) {
        // Table type
        p++; // element type
        const [flags, q1] = u(p);
        p = q1;

        let result;
        [result, p] = u(p);

        if (flags & 1) {
          [result, p] = u(p);
        }
      } else if (kind === 2) {
        // Memory type
        const [flags, q1] = u(p);
        p = q1;

        let result;
        [result, p] = u(p);

        if (flags & 1) {
          [result, p] = u(p);
        }
      } else if (kind === 3) {
        // Global type
        p += 2;
      } else {
        throw new Error(`Unknown import kind ${kind} at 0x${(p - 1).toString(16)}`);
      }
    }
  }

  // FUNCTION SECTION
  if (id === 3) {
    const [count, q0] = u(p);
    p = q0;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const [typeIdx, q1] = u(p);
      p = q1;
      definedFuncTypeIdx.push(typeIdx);
    }
  }

  p = end;
}

const importedFunctions = importFuncTypeIdx.length;

console.log("============================================");
console.log("EXACT FUNCTION TYPE RESOLUTION");
console.log("============================================");
console.log(`TYPE COUNT             : ${typeEntries.length}`);
console.log(`IMPORTED FUNCTIONS     : ${importedFunctions}`);
console.log(`DEFINED FUNCTIONS      : ${definedFuncTypeIdx.length}`);
console.log("");

console.log("TARGET IMPORT");
console.log("--------------------------------------------");

const targetImport = importFuncNames.find(x => x.index === 3);

if (!targetImport) {
  console.log("Imported function 3 not found");
} else {
  const t = typeEntries[targetImport.typeIdx];

  console.log(`FUNCTION INDEX : ${targetImport.index}`);
  console.log(`MODULE         : ${targetImport.moduleName}`);
  console.log(`NAME           : ${targetImport.fieldName}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX     : ${targetImport.typeIdx}`);

  if (t) {
    console.log(
      `SIGNATURE      : (${t.params.join(",")}) -> (${t.results.join(",")})`
    );
  } else {
    console.log("SIGNATURE      : UNKNOWN TYPE");
  }
}

console.log("");
console.log("TYPE 1 SIGNATURE");
console.log("--------------------------------------------");

if (typeEntries[1]) {
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX : 1`);
  console.log(
    `SIGNATURE  : (${typeEntries[1].params.join(",")}) -> (${typeEntries[1].results.join(",")})`
  );
} else {
  console.log("TYPE INDEX 1 NOT FOUND");
}

console.log("");
console.log("TARGET FUNCTIONS");
console.log("--------------------------------------------");

for (const func of [122, 235, 656, 787, 91]) {
  let typeIdx;

  if (func < importedFunctions) {
    typeIdx = importFuncTypeIdx[func];
  } else {
    typeIdx = definedFuncTypeIdx[func - importedFunctions];
  }

  const t = typeEntries[typeIdx];

  console.log(`FUNC ${func}`);
  console.log(`TYPE INDEX : ${typeIdx}`);

  if (!t) {
    console.log("TYPE       : UNKNOWN");
  } else {
    console.log(
      `SIGNATURE  : (${t.params.join(",")}) -> (${t.results.join(",")})`
    );
  }

  console.log("");
}

console.log("============================================");

import fs from 'fs';

const WASM_PATH =
    'G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm';

const wasm = fs.readFileSync(WASM_PATH);

const TARGETS = [
    {
        fn: 107,
        bodyOffset: 54073,
        indirectOffsets: [107],
        types: [71],
    },
    {
        fn: 116,
        bodyOffset: 55544,
        indirectOffsets: [100, 262, 338, 706, 891, 1047],
        types: [32, 126, 32, 32, 126, 32],
    },
    {
        fn: 122,
        bodyOffset: 61125,
        indirectOffsets: [33],
        types: [3],
    },
];

function hex(b: number) {
    return b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
}

console.log('');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' VERIFY WASM CALL_INDIRECT OFFSETS v22');
console.log(' READ ONLY - OFFLINE');
console.log('============================================================');
console.log('');
console.log(`WASM size: ${wasm.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log('');

for (const target of TARGETS) {
    console.log('------------------------------------------------------------');
    console.log(`F#${target.fn}`);
    console.log(`bodyOffset: ${target.bodyOffset}`);
    console.log('');

    for (let i = 0; i < target.indirectOffsets.length; i++) {
        const rel = target.indirectOffsets[i];
        const abs = target.bodyOffset + rel;

        const from = Math.max(target.bodyOffset, abs - 24);
        const to = Math.min(wasm.length, abs + 24);

        console.log(
            `[${i + 1}] relative=${rel} absolute=${abs}`
        );
        console.log(
            `expected typeIndex=${target.types[i]}`
        );

        console.log(
            wasm.subarray(from, to).toString('hex').match(/.{1,2}/g)?.join(' ')
        );

        console.log('');

        for (let p = from; p < to; p++) {
            const marker = p === abs ? '  <<< TARGET' : '';

            console.log(
                `${p.toString().padStart(7)}  ${hex(wasm[p])}${marker}`
            );
        }

        console.log('');
    }
}

console.log('============================================================');
console.log(' DONE');
console.log('============================================================');

FILE: scripts\verifyLpJettonV22.ts

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const EXPECTED_LP_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCNnXhynq5G7RZ8glhXSNg6eXby1kzqD-p8HNxu6PN5SIgd'
);

function printAddress(label: string, address: Address) {
    console.log(`${label}:`);
    console.log(`  ${address.toString()}`);
    console.log(`  raw: ${address.toRawString()}`);
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    const client = provider.api();

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 LP JETTON WALLET VERIFIER');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Pool -> get_wallet_address(user)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('1) Pool get_wallet_address(user)');
    console.log('');

    const lastBlock = await client.getLastBlock();

    const seqno = lastBlock.last.seqno;

    console.log('MASTERCHAIN SEQNO:', seqno);
    console.log('');

    printAddress('USER', USER);
    console.log('');
    printAddress('POOL', POOL);
    console.log('');
    printAddress(
        'EXPECTED LP WALLET',
        EXPECTED_LP_WALLET
    );
    console.log('');

    const walletResult = await client.runMethod(
        seqno,
        POOL,
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(USER)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]
    );

    console.log(
        'get_wallet_address EXIT CODE:',
        walletResult.exitCode
    );

    if (walletResult.exitCode !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `get_wallet_address failed: ${walletResult.exitCode}`
        );
    }

    const calculatedLpWallet =
        walletResult.reader.readAddress();

    console.log('');

    printAddress(
        'CALCULATED LP WALLET',
        calculatedLpWallet
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'CALCULATED == EXPECTED:',
        calculatedLpWallet.equals(EXPECTED_LP_WALLET)
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. LP Wallet get_wallet_data
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('2) LP Jetton Wallet get_wallet_data()');
    console.log('');

    const walletData = await client.runMethod(
        seqno,
        EXPECTED_LP_WALLET,
        'get_wallet_data'
    );

    console.log(
        'get_wallet_data EXIT CODE:',
        walletData.exitCode
    );

    if (walletData.exitCode !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `get_wallet_data failed: ${walletData.exitCode}`
        );
    }

    const balance =
        walletData.reader.readBigNumber();

    const owner =
        walletData.reader.readAddress();

    const jettonMaster =
        walletData.reader.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP WALLET DATA');
    console.log('--------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'balance:',
        balance.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'balance display:',
        Number(balance) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    console.log('');

    printAddress('OWNER', owner);

    console.log('');

    printAddress(
        'JETTON MASTER',
        jettonMaster
    );

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. LP Jetton Master
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    console.log('3) LP Jetton Master get_jetton_data()');
    console.log('');

    const masterData = await client.runMethod(
        seqno,
        jettonMaster,
        'get_jetton_data'
    );

    console.log(
        'get_jetton_data EXIT CODE:',
        masterData.exitCode
    );

    if (masterData.exitCode !== 0) {
        throw new Error(
            `get_jetton_data failed: ${masterData.exitCode}`
        );
    }

    const masterSupply =
        masterData.reader.readBigNumber();

    const mintable =
        masterData.reader.readBoolean();

    const adminAddress =
        masterData.reader.readAddress();

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP JETTON MASTER DATA');
    console.log('--------------------------------------------------------------');

    console.log(
        'total_supply:',
        masterSupply.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'total_supply display:',
        Number(masterSupply) / 1_000_000_000
    );

    console.log(
        'mintable:',
        mintable
    );

    console.log('');

    printAddress(
        'ADMIN',
        adminAddress
    );

    console.log('');

    // ------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Final verification
    // ------------------------------------------------------------

    const walletMatches =
        calculatedLpWallet.equals(
            EXPECTED_LP_WALLET
        );

    const ownerMatches =
        owner.equals(USER);

    const balancePositive =
        balance > 0n;

    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );

    console.log(' FINAL VERIFICATION');

    console.log(
        '=============================================================='
    );

    console.log(
        'LP wallet derived from Pool :',
        walletMatches ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'LP wallet owner = user      :',
        ownerMatches ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log(
        'LP balance > 0              :',
        balancePositive ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );

    console.log('');

    if (
        walletMatches &&
        ownerMatches &&
        balancePositive
    ) {
        console.log(
            'RESULT: LP TOKEN OWNERSHIP VERIFIED'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'RESULT: VERIFICATION INCOMPLETE'
        );
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log('READ ONLY: YES');
    console.log('TRANSACTION SENT: NO');
    console.log('');
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const SDK_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const SDK_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

function normalize(address: Address): string {
    return address.toRawString();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 MASTER get_wallet_address VERIFIER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' CORE ADDRESSES');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON Master:');
    console.log(PTON.toString());
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // ROOSTER MASTER
    // ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER MASTER');
    console.log(' get_wallet_address(ROUTER)');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const roosterMaster =
        provider.provider(ROOSTER);

    const roosterResult =
        await roosterMaster.get(
            'get_wallet_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCellForAddress(ROUTER),
                },
            ]
        );

    const roosterWallet =
        roosterResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
    console.log(roosterWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('SDK-generated ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
    console.log(SDK_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    const roosterMatch =
        normalize(roosterWallet) ===
        normalize(SDK_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET);

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER MASTER vs SDK:',
        roosterMatch ? 'MATCH' : 'MISMATCH'
    );
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // pTON MASTER
    // ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' pTON MASTER');
    console.log(' get_wallet_address(ROUTER)');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const ptonMaster =
        provider.provider(PTON);

    const ptonResult =
        await ptonMaster.get(
            'get_wallet_address',
            [
                {
                    type: 'slice',
                    cell: beginCellForAddress(ROUTER),
                },
            ]
        );

    const ptonWallet =
        ptonResult.stack.readAddress();

    console.log('pTON Router Wallet:');
    console.log(ptonWallet.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('SDK-generated pTON Router Wallet:');
    console.log(SDK_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());
    console.log('');

    const ptonMatch =
        normalize(ptonWallet) ===
        normalize(SDK_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET);

    console.log(
        'pTON MASTER vs SDK:',
        ptonMatch ? 'MATCH' : 'MISMATCH'
    );
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // FINAL
    // ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER get_wallet_address:',
        roosterWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON get_wallet_address:',
        ptonWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER MASTER ↔ SDK:',
        roosterMatch ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON MASTER ↔ SDK:',
        ptonMatch ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('');

    if (roosterMatch && ptonMatch) {
        console.log(
            'RESULT: SDK AND BOTH MASTERS AGREE.'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'RESULT: SDK AND MASTER RESULTS DO NOT AGREE.'
        );
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');
}

function beginCellForAddress(address: Address) {
    const { beginCell } = require('@ton/core');

    return beginCell()
        .storeAddress(address)
        .endCell();
}

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parseRaw(
    '0:92e1411ae546892f33b2c8a89ea90390d8ff4cfbb917a643b91e73f706fdb9d2'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCePy3CW0qsWj03tvCwBxpavtYcu27LURfEy7H6tRhTbwsq'
);

const PTON_POOL_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQCSIMGBps_qzRG3uPYhON8bucyCtu0mYdL1-u4gSz77IBa3'
);

const ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBs7M6C4uvWFgzpN5WnQ6O-CdkoL1bs_noltSxuMOwCoXAC'
);

const PTON_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQDOvISNl85xReKzTbu4yl-S9wWjHM8sFFkcWWAe0YKF_Adu'
);

function compareAddress(
    label: string,
    actual: Address,
    expected: Address,
) {
    const match = actual.equals(expected);

    console.log('');
    console.log(label);
    console.log('Actual:');
    console.log(actual.toString());
    console.log('Expected:');
    console.log(expected.toString());
    console.log(`MATCH: ${match ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`);

    return match;
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 POOL TOKEN ORDER VERIFIER');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL:');
    console.log(POOL.toString());

    const pool = DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(POOL);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Reading Pool data...');

    const poolData = await pool.getPoolData(
    provider.provider(POOL)
);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' POOL DATA');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router address from Pool:');
    console.log(poolData.routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Token 0 wallet:');
    console.log(poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Token 1 wallet:');
    console.log(poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Reserve 0 raw:');
    console.log(poolData.reserve0.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Reserve 1 raw:');
    console.log(poolData.reserve1.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Total LP:');
    console.log(poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Locked:');
    console.log(poolData.isLocked);

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' POOL ADDRESS / TOKEN ORDER');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const routerMatch = compareAddress(
        'Router address',
        poolData.routerAddress,
        ROUTER,
    );

    const token0RoosterMatch = compareAddress(
        'Token 0 = ROOSTER pool wallet',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress,
        ROOSTER_POOL_WALLET,
    );

    const token1PtonMatch = compareAddress(
        'Token 1 = pTON pool wallet',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress,
        PTON_POOL_WALLET,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' EXPECTED TOKEN ORDER');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Expected:');
    console.log('Token 0 = ROOSTER');
    console.log('Token 1 = pTON');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `TOKEN ORDER MATCH: ${
            token0RoosterMatch && token1PtonMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'
        }`,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER WALLET RELATION');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Router Wallet:');
    console.log(PTON_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('For ROOSTER deposit:');
    console.log('sendToken = ROOSTER');
    console.log('otherToken = pTON');
    console.log(
        'provide_lp routerWalletAddress = pTON Router Wallet',
    );
    console.log(PTON_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('For pTON deposit:');
    console.log('sendToken = pTON');
    console.log('otherToken = ROOSTER');
    console.log(
        'provide_lp routerWalletAddress = ROOSTER Router Wallet',
    );
    console.log(ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL READ-ONLY RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `Router match: ${routerMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`
    );

    console.log(
        `Token 0 ROOSTER: ${
            token0RoosterMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `Token 1 pTON: ${
            token1PtonMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'
        }`
    );

    console.log(
        `Overall Pool verification: ${
            routerMatch &&
            token0RoosterMatch &&
            token1PtonMatch
                ? 'PASS'
                : 'CHECK REQUIRED'
        }`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
}
