import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';

export async function run(provider: any) {
    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const knownLibraryHash =
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

    console.log('===== VERIFY ROUTER LIBRARY HASH =====');
    console.log('Router:', router.toString());
    console.log('Known Library Hash:', knownLibraryHash);

    const state = await provider.provider(router).getState();

    console.log('\n===== ROUTER STATE =====');
    console.log('State:', state.state.type);

    if (state.state.type !== 'active') {
        console.log('Router is not active.');
        return;
    }

    const code = state.state.code;

    if (!code) {
        console.log('Router code is missing.');
        return;
    }

    console.log('Code bytes:', code.length);

    const hex = code.toString('hex');

    console.log('\n===== RAW CODE BOC =====');
    console.log('BOC prefix:', hex.substring(0, 8));
    console.log('BOC length:', hex.length / 2);

    const roots = Cell.fromBoc(code);

    console.log('\n===== BOC =====');
    console.log('Root count:', roots.length);

    if (roots.length !== 1) {
        console.log('Unexpected BOC root count.');
        return;
    }

    const root = roots[0];

    console.log('\n===== ROOT CELL =====');
    console.log('Bits:', root.bits.length);
    console.log('Refs:', root.refs.length);
    console.log('Exotic:', root.isExotic);
    console.log('Type:', root.type);
    console.log('Root hash:', root.hash().toString('hex'));

    console.log('\n===== ROOT HASH COMPARISON =====');
    console.log(
        'Root hash == known library hash:',
        root.hash().toString('hex') === knownLibraryHash
    );

    console.log('\n===== ROOT CELL DETAILS =====');

    try {
        const slice = root.beginParse();

        console.log('Remaining bits:', slice.remainingBits);
        console.log('Remaining refs:', slice.remainingRefs);

        if (slice.remainingBits >= 8) {
            console.log('First 8 bits:', slice.loadUint(8));
        }

        console.log(
            'beginParse(): SUCCESS'
        );
    } catch (error) {
        console.log(
            'beginParse(): FAILED'
        );
        console.log(error);
    }

    console.log('\n===== ROOT REFS =====');

    for (let i = 0; i < root.refs.length; i++) {
        const ref = root.refs[i];

        console.log(`Ref ${i}:`);
        console.log('  bits:', ref.bits.length);
        console.log('  refs:', ref.refs.length);
        console.log('  exotic:', ref.isExotic);
        console.log('  type:', ref.type);
        console.log('  hash:', ref.hash().toString('hex'));
    }

    console.log('\n===== EXPECTED LIBRARY HASH =====');
    console.log(knownLibraryHash);

    console.log('\n===== RESULT =====');

    if (root.isExotic) {
        console.log('Root IS an exotic cell.');
    } else {
        console.log('Root is NOT an exotic cell.');
    }

    if (root.hash().toString('hex') === knownLibraryHash) {
        console.log('Root hash MATCHES the known Library Hash.');
    } else {
        console.log('Root hash DOES NOT MATCH the known Library Hash.');
    }
}

import {
    LiteClient,
    LiteSingleEngine,
} from 'ton-lite-client';

import { Address, Cell } from '@ton/core';

async function main() {

    const router = Address.parse(
        'EQALh-JBBIKK7gr0o4AVf9JZnEsFndqO0qTCyT-D-yBsWval'
    );

    const libraryHashHex =
        '5a34c333bcdcac70d41f9afca79aa6e782f1c80a5e4b2ef896ec5e20e9182c86';

    const libraryHash =
        Buffer.from(libraryHashHex, 'hex');

    const expectedRouterCodeHash =
        'ca4c3052a1396aef50ca358ac355e2a99be4e1683eb87a2c11fbd452bb4acec5';

    // فقط Server 1
    const server = {
        ip: -1468571697,
        port: 27787,
        key: 'Y/QVf6G5VDiKTZOKitbFVm067WsuocTN8Vg036A4zGk=',
    };

    const host =
        `tcp://${intToIP(server.ip)}:${server.port}`;

    console.log('========================================');
    console.log(' ROUTER LIBRARY DIRECT TEST');
    console.log('========================================');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Server:');
    console.log(host);
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(router.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected Router Code Hash:');
    console.log(expectedRouterCodeHash);
    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected Library Hash:');
    console.log(libraryHashHex);
    console.log('');

    const engine = new LiteSingleEngine({
        host,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(server.key, 'base64'),
        reconnectTimeout: 3000,
    });

    const client = new LiteClient({
        engine,
        batchSize: 1,
    });

    try {

        // =========================================================
        // MASTERCHAIN
        // =========================================================

        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('MASTERCHAIN');

        const master =
            await client.getMasterchainInfo();

        console.log(
            'Masterchain seqno:',
            master.last.seqno
        );

        console.log(
            'Root hash:',
            Buffer.from(
                master.last.rootHash
            ).toString('hex')
        );

        // =========================================================
        // ACCOUNT STATE
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER ACCOUNT STATE');

        const account =
            await client.getAccountState(
                router,
                master.last
            );

        if (
            account.state === null ||
            account.state.storage.state.type !== 'active'
        ) {
            console.log('ERROR: Router is not active.');

            console.log(
                'State:',
                account.state === null
                    ? 'null'
                    : account.state.storage.state.type
            );

            return;
        }

        console.log('Router active: YES');

        const activeState =
            account.state.storage.state.state;

        const code: Cell | null | undefined =
            activeState.code;

        if (!code) {
            console.log('ERROR: Router code is missing.');
            return;
        }

        console.log('Router code exists: YES');

        // =========================================================
        // CODE CELL
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER CODE CELL');

        console.log(
            'Cell type:',
            code.type
        );

        console.log(
            'Bits:',
            code.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Refs:',
            code.refs.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Exotic:',
            code.type !== -1 ? 'YES' : 'NO'
        );

        // =========================================================
        // CODE HASH
        // =========================================================

        const actualCodeHash =
            code.hash().toString('hex');

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER CODE HASH');

        console.log('Actual:');
        console.log(actualCodeHash);

        console.log('');
        console.log('Expected:');
        console.log(expectedRouterCodeHash);

        console.log('');
        console.log(
            'MATCH:',
            actualCodeHash === expectedRouterCodeHash
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // =========================================================
        // CODE BOC
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER CODE BOC');

        const boc =
            code.toBoc({
                idx: false,
            });

        console.log(
            Buffer.from(boc).toString('base64')
        );

        // =========================================================
        // CELL BITS
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROUTER CODE CELL BITS');

        const cellBits =
            code.bits.toString();

        console.log(
            'Bits length:',
            code.bits.length
        );

        console.log(
            'First 8 bits:',
            cellBits.substring(0, 8)
        );

        console.log(
            'First 16 bits:',
            cellBits.substring(0, 16)
        );

        console.log(
            'First 64 bits:',
            cellBits.substring(0, 64)
        );

        // =========================================================
        // LIBRARY REFERENCE ANALYSIS
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('LIBRARY REFERENCE ANALYSIS');

        if (code.type === -1) {

            console.log(
                'Root code cell is ordinary.'
            );

        } else {

            console.log(
                'Root code cell is EXOTIC.'
            );

            console.log(
                'Exotic cell type:',
                code.type
            );
        }

        /*
         * TON Library Reference Cell:
         *
         * 8 bits  = exotic type
         * 256 bits = library hash
         *
         * For a library reference:
         * type = 2
         */

        if (
            code.type === 2 &&
            code.bits.length >= 264
        ) {

            const extractedHash =
                cellBits.substring(8, 264);

            const extractedHashHex =
                BigInt(
                    '0b' + extractedHash
                )
                    .toString(16)
                    .padStart(64, '0');

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Detected Library Reference Cell: YES'
            );

            console.log(
                'Extracted Library Hash:'
            );

            console.log(
                extractedHashHex
            );

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Expected Library Hash:'
            );

            console.log(
                libraryHashHex
            );

            console.log('');

            console.log(
                'Library Hash MATCH:',
                extractedHashHex.toLowerCase() ===
                    libraryHashHex.toLowerCase()
                    ? 'YES'
                    : 'NO'
            );

        } else {

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                'Detected Library Reference Cell: NO'
            );

            console.log(
                'This Router root cell is not a standard'
            );

            console.log(
                'exotic Library Reference Cell.'
            );
        }

        // =========================================================
        // ROOT REFERENCES
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('ROOT CELL REFERENCES');

        console.log(
            'Reference count:',
            code.refs.length
        );

        for (
            let i = 0;
            i < code.refs.length;
            i++
        ) {

            const ref =
                code.refs[i];

            console.log('');
            console.log(
                `Reference ${i}`
            );

            console.log(
                'Type:',
                ref.type
            );

            console.log(
                'Bits:',
                ref.bits.length
            );

            console.log(
                'Refs:',
                ref.refs.length
            );

            console.log(
                'Hash:',
                ref.hash().toString('hex')
            );
        }

        // =========================================================
        // REAL LIBRARY LOOKUP
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('REAL LIBRARY LOOKUP');

        console.log(
            'Requesting:',
            libraryHashHex
        );

        const realResult =
            await client.getLibraries([
                libraryHash,
            ]);

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Result object:'
        );

        console.dir(
            realResult,
            {
                depth: 5,
            }
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Result count:',
            realResult.result.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Library returned:',
            realResult.result.length > 0
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // =========================================================
        // FAKE LIBRARY LOOKUP
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('----------------------------------------');
        console.log('FAKE LIBRARY LOOKUP');

        const fakeLibraryHash =
            Buffer.alloc(32, 0);

        const fakeHashHex =
            fakeLibraryHash.toString('hex');

        console.log(
            'Fake Hash:',
            fakeHashHex
        );

        const fakeResult =
            await client.getLibraries([
                fakeLibraryHash,
            ]);

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Fake Result:'
        );

        console.dir(
            fakeResult,
            {
                depth: 5,
            }
        );

        console.log('');

        console.log(
            'Fake result count:',
            fakeResult.result.length
        );

        console.log(
            'Fake library returned:',
            fakeResult.result.length > 0
                ? 'YES'
                : 'NO'
        );

        // =========================================================
        // FINAL
        // =========================================================

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
        console.log('========================================');

        console.log(
            'Router active:',
            'YES'
        );

        console.log(
            'Router code exists:',
            'YES'
        );

        console.log(
            'Router code hash:',
            actualCodeHash === expectedRouterCodeHash
                ? 'MATCH'
                : 'MISMATCH'
        );

        console.log(
            'Library lookup:',
            realResult.result.length > 0
                ? 'RETURNED'
                : 'NOT RETURNED'
        );

        console.log(
            'Fake lookup:',
            fakeResult.result.length > 0
                ? 'RETURNED'
                : 'NOT RETURNED'
        );

        console.log('');
        console.log('========================================');
        console.log(' NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
        console.log('========================================');

    } finally {

        engine.close();
    }
}

function intToIP(int: number) {

    const part1 =
        int & 255;

    const part2 =
        (int >> 8) & 255;

    const part3 =
        (int >> 16) & 255;

    const part4 =
        (int >> 24) & 255;

    return `${part4}.${part3}.${part2}.${part1}`;
}

main().catch((error) => {

    console.error('');
    console.error('========================================');
    console.error(' ERROR');
    console.error('========================================');

    console.error(error);

    process.exit(1);
});

export async function run() {
    await main();
}

FILE: scripts\verifyRouterWalletFromMasterV22.ts

import { Address } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROUTER = Address.parseRaw(
    '0:92e1411ae546892f33b2c8a89ea90390d8ff4cfbb917a643b91e73f706fdb9d2'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const SDK_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBs7M6C4uvWFgzpN5WnQ6O-CdkoL1bs_noltSxuMOwCoXAC'
);

const SDK_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQDOvISNl85xReKzTbu4yl-S9wWjHM8sFFkcWWAe0YKF_Adu'
);

async function getWalletAddress(
    provider: NetworkProvider,
    master: Address,
    owner: Address,
): Promise<Address> {
    const result = await provider
        .provider(master)
        .get('get_wallet_address', [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginAddressCell(owner),
            },
        ]);

    return result.stack.readAddress();
}

function beginAddressCell(address: Address) {
    const { beginCell } = require('@ton/core');

    return beginCell()
        .storeAddress(address)
        .endCell();
}

function compare(
    name: string,
    calculated: Address,
    sdkAddress: Address,
) {
    console.log('');
    console.log(`===== ${name} =====`);

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calculated from Master:');
    console.log(calculated.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('SDK Router Wallet:');
    console.log(sdkAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('MATCH:');
    console.log(calculated.equals(sdkAddress) ? 'YES' : 'NO');
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI V2.2 ROUTER WALLET VERIFIER');
    console.log('============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');

    console.log('');
    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON Master:');
    console.log(PTON_MASTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calculating ROOSTER Router Wallet...');

    const roosterWallet = await getWalletAddress(
        provider,
        ROOSTER_MASTER,
        ROUTER,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('Calculating pTON Router Wallet...');

    const ptonWallet = await getWalletAddress(
        provider,
        PTON_MASTER,
        ROUTER,
    );

    compare(
        'ROOSTER',
        roosterWallet,
        SDK_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET,
    );

    compare(
        'pTON',
        ptonWallet,
        SDK_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' FINAL RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');

    const roosterMatch = roosterWallet.equals(
        SDK_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET,
    );

    const ptonMatch = ptonWallet.equals(
        SDK_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET,
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        `ROOSTER Router Wallet verified: ${roosterMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`
    );

    console.log(
        `pTON Router Wallet verified: ${ptonMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'}`
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const KNOWN_POOL = Address.parse(
    'EQD5ALaCuEqJ7Mi9qlXOA7BHMrTq8e2fZ5B_teWN17MZq-cf'
);

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

async function getJettonWalletAddress(
    masterProvider: any,
    ownerAddress: Address,
): Promise<Address> {
    const result = await masterProvider.get(
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(ownerAddress)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ],
    );

    return result.stack.readAddress();
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' STON.FI v2.2 ROUTER / POOL VERIFICATION');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('KNOWN POOL:');
    console.log(KNOWN_POOL.toString());

    /*
     * Contract providers
     *
     * These are read-only providers for the corresponding contracts.
     */
    const routerProvider = provider.provider(ROUTER);
    const poolProvider = provider.provider(KNOWN_POOL);

    const roosterMasterProvider =
    provider.provider(ROOSTER_MASTER);

const ptonMasterProvider =
    provider.provider(PTON_MASTER);

    /*
     * Router's Jetton wallet addresses
     *
     * The SDK's internal JettonMinter implementation does exactly
     * this get-method call. We reproduce it directly so that we
     * do not depend on an unavailable public JettonMinter export.
     */
    const roosterRouterWallet =
        await getJettonWalletAddress(
            roosterMasterProvider,
            ROUTER,
        );

    const ptonRouterWallet =
        await getJettonWalletAddress(
            ptonMasterProvider,
            ROUTER,
        );

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROOSTER WALLET OWNED BY ROUTER:');
    console.log(roosterRouterWallet.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('pTON WALLET OWNED BY ROUTER:');
    console.log(ptonRouterWallet.toString());

    /*
     * Create v2.2 Router wrapper.
     */
    const router =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER),
        );

    /*
     * Ask the actual v2.2 Router for the pool address.
     */
   const calculatedPool =
    await router.getPoolAddress({
        token0: roosterRouterWallet,
        token1: ptonRouterWallet,
    });

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL CALCULATED BY ROUTER:');
    console.log(calculatedPool.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('KNOWN POOL:');
    console.log(KNOWN_POOL.toString());

    const poolMatch =
        calculatedPool.equals(KNOWN_POOL);

    console.log('');
    console.log('POOL ADDRESS MATCH:');
    console.log(poolMatch);

    /*
     * Read actual pool data.
     */
    const pool =
        provider.open(
            DEX.v2_2.Pool.CPI.create(KNOWN_POOL),
        );

    const poolData =
    await pool.getPoolData();

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' ACTUAL POOL DATA');
    console.log('==============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log('LOCKED:');
    console.log(poolData.isLocked);

    console.log('');
    console.log('ROUTER ADDRESS:');
    console.log(poolData.routerAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TOTAL LP SUPPLY:');
    console.log(poolData.totalSupplyLP.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('RESERVE 0:');
    console.log(poolData.reserve0.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('RESERVE 1:');
    console.log(poolData.reserve1.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TOKEN 0 WALLET:');
    console.log(poolData.token0WalletAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('TOKEN 1 WALLET:');
    console.log(poolData.token1WalletAddress.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('LP FEE:');
    console.log(poolData.lpFee.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('PROTOCOL FEE:');
    console.log(poolData.protocolFee.toString());

    console.log('');
    console.log('PROTOCOL FEE ADDRESS:');
    console.log(
        poolData.protocolFeeAddress
            ? poolData.protocolFeeAddress.toString()
            : 'null',
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('COLLECTED TOKEN 0 PROTOCOL FEE:');
    console.log(
        poolData.collectedToken0ProtocolFee.toString(),
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('COLLECTED TOKEN 1 PROTOCOL FEE:');
    console.log(
        poolData.collectedToken1ProtocolFee.toString(),
    );

    /*
     * Cross-check the addresses returned by the Router
     * against the addresses stored in the Pool.
     */
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' CROSS-CHECKS');
    console.log('==============================================================');

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TOKEN 0 MATCH:',
        poolData.token0WalletAddress.equals(
            roosterRouterWallet,
        ),
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'TOKEN 1 MATCH:',
        poolData.token1WalletAddress.equals(
            ptonRouterWallet,
        ),
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log(
        'POOL ROUTER MATCH:',
        poolData.routerAddress.equals(ROUTER),
    );

    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================================');
    console.log(' VERIFICATION COMPLETE');
    console.log(' READ ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT');
    console.log('==============================================================');
}

import { Address, Cell, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';
import { DEX } from '@ston-fi/sdk';

const ROUTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCS4UEa5UaJLzOyyKieqQOQ2P9M-7kXpkO5HnP3Bv250cN3'
);

const ROOSTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const PTON = Address.parse(
    'EQBnGWMCf3-FZZq1W4IWcWiGAc3PHuZ0_H-7sad2oY00o83S'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQDOvISNl85xReKzTbu4yl-S9wWjHM8sFFkcWWAe0YKF_Adu'
);

const EXPECTED_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBs7M6C4uvWFgzpN5WnQ6O-CdkoL1bs_noltSxuMOwCoXAC'
);

const MIN_LP_OUT = 150109265n;

function normalize(a: Address): string {
    return a.toRawString();
}

function decodeProvideLpBody(body: Cell) {
    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);

    const routerWallet = s.loadAddress();
    const refundAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const excessAddress = s.loadAddress();
    const deadline = s.loadUintBig(64);

    const ref = s.loadRef().beginParse();

    const minLpOut = ref.loadCoins();
    const receiver = ref.loadAddress();
    const bothPositive = ref.loadBit();
    const gasAmount = ref.loadCoins();

    const dexCustomPayload =
        ref.remainingRefs > 0
            ? ref.loadRef()
            : null;

    return {
        opcode,
        routerWallet,
        refundAddress,
        excessAddress,
        deadline,
        minLpOut,
        receiver,
        bothPositive,
        gasAmount,
        dexCustomPayload,
    };
}

function decodeJettonTransferBody(body: Cell) {
    const s = body.beginParse();

    const opcode = s.loadUint(32);
    const queryId = s.loadUintBig(64);
    const amount = s.loadCoins();

    const destination = s.loadAddress();
    const responseDestination = s.loadAddress();

    const customPayload = s.loadMaybeRef();

    const forwardTonAmount = s.loadCoins();

    const forwardPayloadIsRef = s.loadBit();

    let forwardPayload: Cell;

    if (forwardPayloadIsRef) {
        forwardPayload = s.loadRef();
    } else {
        forwardPayload = beginCell()
            .storeSlice(s)
            .endCell();
    }

    return {
        opcode,
        queryId,
        amount,
        destination,
        responseDestination,
        customPayload,
        forwardTonAmount,
        forwardPayload,
    };
}

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('      STON.FI V2.2 SDK ROUTER WALLET VERIFIER');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');
    console.log('READ-ONLY');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WILL BE TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');

    // Keep the router UNOPENED.
    // This matches the working buildLiquidityTxV22.ts.
    const router =
        DEX.v2_2.Router.CPI.create(ROUTER);

    const routerProvider =
        provider.provider(ROUTER);

    console.log('===== CORE ADDRESSES =====');
    console.log('');

    console.log('Router:');
    console.log(ROUTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('ROOSTER Master:');
    console.log(ROOSTER.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON Master:');
    console.log(PTON.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('User:');
    console.log(USER.toString());
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // SDK GENERATED ROOSTER TX
    // ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' SDK GENERATED ROOSTER TX');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const roosterTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityJettonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                sendTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                otherTokenAddress: PTON,
                sendAmount: 100000000000n,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                deadline:
                    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600,
            }
        );

    console.log('Outer destination:');
    console.log(roosterTx.to.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('TON value:');
    console.log(roosterTx.value.toString());
    console.log('');

    if (!roosterTx.body) {
        throw new Error('ROOSTER TX body is missing.');
    }

    console.log('Body exists: YES');
    console.log('');

    const roosterTransfer =
        decodeJettonTransferBody(roosterTx.body);

    console.log('JETTON TRANSFER opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + roosterTransfer.opcode.toString(16)
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('JETTON amount:');
    console.log(roosterTransfer.amount.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('JETTON transfer destination:');
    console.log(
        roosterTransfer.destination.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('JETTON response destination:');
    console.log(
        roosterTransfer.responseDestination.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('Forward TON amount:');
    console.log(
        roosterTransfer.forwardTonAmount.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    const roosterDecoded =
        decodeProvideLpBody(
            roosterTransfer.forwardPayload
        );

    console.log('PROVIDE_LP opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + roosterDecoded.opcode.toString(16)
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('PROVIDE_LP Router Wallet:');
    console.log(
        roosterDecoded.routerWallet.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected pTON Router Wallet:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    const roosterMatch =
        normalize(roosterDecoded.routerWallet) ===
        normalize(EXPECTED_PTON_ROUTER_WALLET);

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER Router Wallet MATCH:',
        roosterMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // SDK GENERATED pTON TX
    // ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log(' SDK GENERATED pTON TX');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    const ptonTx =
        await router.getProvideLiquidityTonTxParams(
            routerProvider,
            {
                userWalletAddress: USER,
                otherTokenAddress: ROOSTER,
                sendAmount: 249908n,
                receiverAddress: USER,
                refundAddress: USER,
                excessesAddress: USER,
                minLpOut: MIN_LP_OUT,
                proxyTon:
                    DEX.v2_2.pTON.create(PTON),
                deadline:
                    Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600,
            }
        );

    console.log('Outer destination:');
    console.log(ptonTx.to.toString());
    console.log('');

    console.log('TON value:');
    console.log(ptonTx.value.toString());
    console.log('');

    if (!ptonTx.body) {
        throw new Error('pTON TX body is missing.');
    }

    console.log('Body exists: YES');
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // pTON TON_TRANSFER BODY
    //
    // Actual SDK structure:
    //
    // opcode (32)
    // queryId (64)
    // tonAmount
    // refundAddress
    // forwardPayloadPresent (1)
    // forwardPayload ref
    //
    // No response destination.
    // No forward TON amount.
    // ============================================================

    const ptonTransfer =
        ptonTx.body.beginParse();

    const ptonOpcode =
        ptonTransfer.loadUint(32);

    const ptonQueryId =
        ptonTransfer.loadUintBig(64);

    const ptonAmount =
        ptonTransfer.loadCoins();

    const ptonRefund =
        ptonTransfer.loadAddress();

    const ptonForwardPayloadIsRef =
        ptonTransfer.loadBit();

    let ptonForwardPayload: Cell;

    if (ptonForwardPayloadIsRef) {
        ptonForwardPayload =
            ptonTransfer.loadRef();
    } else {
        ptonForwardPayload =
            beginCell()
                .storeSlice(ptonTransfer)
                .endCell();
    }

    console.log('TON_TRANSFER opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + ptonOpcode.toString(16)
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('TON_TRANSFER query ID:');
    console.log(
        ptonQueryId.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON amount:');
    console.log(
        ptonAmount.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('pTON refund:');
    console.log(
        ptonRefund.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('Forward payload by reference:');
    console.log(
        ptonForwardPayloadIsRef
            ? 'YES'
            : 'NO'
    );
    console.log('');

    const ptonDecoded =
        decodeProvideLpBody(
            ptonForwardPayload
        );

    console.log('PROVIDE_LP opcode:');
    console.log(
        '0x' + ptonDecoded.opcode.toString(16)
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('PROVIDE_LP Router Wallet:');
    console.log(
        ptonDecoded.routerWallet.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    console.log('Expected ROOSTER Router Wallet:');
    console.log(
        EXPECTED_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET.toString()
    );
    console.log('');

    const ptonMatch =
        normalize(ptonDecoded.routerWallet) ===
        normalize(EXPECTED_ROOSTER_ROUTER_WALLET);

    console.log(
        'pTON Router Wallet MATCH:',
        ptonMatch ? 'YES' : 'NO'
    );
    console.log('');

    // ============================================================
    // FINAL
    // ============================================================

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('                 FINAL SDK RESULT');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER TX → pTON Router Wallet:',
        roosterMatch ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log(
        'pTON TX → ROOSTER Router Wallet:',
        ptonMatch ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'
    );

    console.log('');

    if (roosterMatch && ptonMatch) {
        console.log(
            'SDK ROUTER WALLET MAPPING: VERIFIED'
        );
    } else {
        console.log(
            'SDK ROUTER WALLET MAPPING: NOT VERIFIED'
        );
    }

    console.log('');

    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('                 READ-ONLY COMPLETE');
    console.log('============================================================');
    console.log('');

    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');
    console.log('NO ROOSTER WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('NO TON WAS TRANSFERRED.');
    console.log('');
}

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

const targets = new Set([5220, 5232, 5235]);

function uleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;

  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  return [v >>> 0, p];
}

function sleb(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0, x;

  while (true) {
    x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 0x7f) * 2 ** s;

    if (!(x & 0x80)) break;
    s += 7;
  }

  if (s < 32 && (x & 0x40)) {
    v |= (~0 << s);
  }

  return [v | 0, p];
}

function hex(x) {
  return "0x" + x.toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

// Find Code section
let p = 8;
let codeStart = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];

  let size;
  [size, p] = uleb(p);

  if (id === 10) {
    codeStart = p;
    break;
  }

  p += size;
}

let q = codeStart;

let count;
[count, q] = uleb(q);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("VERIFIED SELECTOR CONSTANTS");
console.log("REAL WASM OPCODE PARSE");
console.log("READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");

let hits = 0;

for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
  let bodySize;
  [bodySize, q] = uleb(q);

  const bodyStart = q;
  const bodyEnd = q + bodySize;
  const func = i + 91;

  let x = bodyStart;

  // locals
  let localCount;
  [localCount, x] = uleb(x);

  for (let l = 0; l < localCount; l++) {
    let n, type;
    [n, x] = uleb(x);
    type = b[x++];
  }

  while (x < bodyEnd) {
    const opPos = x;
    const op = b[x++];

    // end
    if (op === 0x0b) break;

    // i32.const
    if (op === 0x41) {
      let value;
      [value, x] = sleb(x);

      if (targets.has(value >>> 0)) {
        console.log("");
        console.log("--------------------------------------------");
        console.log("FUNC   :", func);
        console.log("OFFSET :", hex(opPos));
        console.log("VALUE  :", value);
        console.log("OPCODE : i32.const");
        hits++;
      }

      continue;
    }

    // i64.const
    if (op === 0x42) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, x] = sleb(x);
      continue;
    }

    // block / loop / if
    if (op === 0x02 || op === 0x03 || op === 0x04) {
      x++; // block type
      continue;
    }

    // br / br_if
    if (op === 0x0c || op === 0x0d) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, x] = uleb(x);
      continue;
    }

    // call
    if (op === 0x10) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, x] = uleb(x);
      continue;
    }

    // call_indirect
    if (op === 0x11) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, x] = uleb(x);
      x++; // table index
      continue;
    }

    // local.get / local.set / local.tee
    if (op === 0x20 || op === 0x21 || op === 0x22) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, x] = uleb(x);
      continue;
    }

    // global.get / global.set
    if (op === 0x23 || op === 0x24) {
      let dummy;
      [dummy, x] = uleb(x);
      continue;
    }

    // loads/stores 0x28..0x3e
    if (op >= 0x28 && op <= 0x3e) {
      let align, offset;
      [align, x] = uleb(x);
      [offset, x] = uleb(x);
      continue;
    }

    // memory.size / memory.grow
    if (op === 0x3f || op === 0x40) {
      x++;
      continue;
    }

    // i32 comparisons / arithmetic
    if (op >= 0x45 && op <= 0x4f) continue;
    if (op >= 0x50 && op <= 0x66) continue;
    if (op >= 0x67 && op <= 0x78) continue;

    // conversions
    if (op >= 0xa7 && op <= 0xbf) continue;

    // misc prefixes
    if (op === 0xfc || op === 0xfd || op === 0xfe) {
      let sub;
      [sub, x] = uleb(x);

      // We do not attempt to decode the prefixed immediate here.
      // Skip only known simple cases conservatively.
      continue;
    }

    // Unknown opcode: stop this body rather than producing false hits.
    // The target constants must be verified only through decoded paths.
  }

  q = bodyEnd;
}

console.log("");
console.log("TOTAL VERIFIED :", hits);
console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");

import fs from "fs";

const b = fs.readFileSync("emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm");

function u(p) {
  let v = 0, s = 0;
  while (true) {
    const x = b[p++];
    v += (x & 127) * 2 ** s;
    if (!(x & 128)) return [v >>> 0, p];
    s += 7;
  }
}

function hex(v) {
  return "0x" + (v >>> 0).toString(16);
}

let p = 8;
let elemStart = 0;
let elemEnd = 0;

while (p < b.length) {
  const id = b[p++];
  const [size, q] = u(p);
  p = q;

  const start = p;
  const end = p + size;

  if (id === 9) {
    elemStart = start;
    elemEnd = end;
    break;
  }

  p = end;
}

if (!elemStart) {
  throw new Error("Element section not found");
}

p = elemStart;

const [segmentCount, q0] = u(p);
p = q0;

console.log("");

for (let seg = 0; seg < segmentCount; seg++) {

  const flags = b[p++];

  console.log(`SEGMENT ${seg} FLAGS ${flags}`);

  if (flags !== 0) {
    console.log("Non-zero flags: parser not needed for target.");
    break;
  }

  // offset expression: i32.const <value>, end
  const opcode = b[p++];

  if (opcode !== 0x41) {
    throw new Error("Unexpected offset expression");
  }

  const [offset, q1] = u(p);
  p = q1;

  if (b[p++] !== 0x0b) {
    throw new Error("Missing offset expression end");
  }

  const [count, q2] = u(p);
  p = q2;

  console.log(`TABLE OFFSET : ${offset}`);
  console.log(`ELEMENT COUNT: ${count}`);
  console.log("");

  const targetStart = 4787;
  const targetEnd = 4791;

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {

    const [func, q] = u(p);
    p = q;

    const tableIndex = offset + i;

    if (tableIndex >= targetStart &&
        tableIndex <= targetEnd) {

      console.log(
        `RAW ELEMENT[${i}] -> ` +
        `TABLE[${tableIndex}] = FUNC ${func}`
      );

  }

  console.log("");
}

import { Address, beginCell } from '@ton/core';
import { NetworkProvider } from '@ton/blueprint';

const ROOSTER_MASTER = Address.parse(
    'EQCyuv-cYXFibA05bhiL3seM3Ur2B_bpsc364Zb8F3NMtrYx'
);

const USER = Address.parse(
    'EQDxn5MLbFyV649-OBviQrMp5KqWjGHNVwFM6GTeaLl8N0Ix'
);

const EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET = Address.parse(
    'EQBzQOOJu9qQVli2R0o6p3pXvi_wj9O2ncMhv3UyAjvMiMOh'
);

const REQUIRED_AMOUNT = 200073535827652n;

export async function run(provider: NetworkProvider) {
    console.log('=================================================');
    console.log(' ROOSTER USER JETTON WALLET BALANCE CHECK');
    console.log(' READ-ONLY - NO TRANSACTION WILL BE SENT');
    console.log('=================================================');

    console.log('\n===== 1. USER =====');

    console.log(
        'User:',
        USER.toString()
    );

    console.log('\n===== 2. DERIVE ROOSTER WALLET =====');

    const masterProvider =
        provider.provider(ROOSTER_MASTER);

    const result = await masterProvider.get(
        'get_wallet_address',
        [
            {
                type: 'slice',
                cell: beginCell()
                    .storeAddress(USER)
                    .endCell(),
            },
        ]
    );

    const derivedWallet =
        result.stack.readAddress();

    console.log(
        'Derived ROOSTER wallet:',
        derivedWallet.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Expected ROOSTER wallet:',
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toString()
    );

    if (
        derivedWallet.toRawString() !==
        EXPECTED_USER_JETTON_WALLET.toRawString()
    ) {
        throw new Error(
            'ROOSTER wallet address mismatch.'
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER wallet address: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 3. READ JETTON WALLET DATA =====');

    const walletProvider =
        provider.provider(derivedWallet);

    const walletData =
        await walletProvider.get(
            'get_wallet_data',
            []
        );

    const balance =
        walletData.stack.readBigNumber();

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER nano balance:',
        balance.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER display balance:',
        Number(balance) / 1e9
    );

    console.log(
        'Required nano amount:',
        REQUIRED_AMOUNT.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Required ROOSTER:',
        Number(REQUIRED_AMOUNT) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('\n===== 4. BALANCE CHECK =====');

    if (balance < REQUIRED_AMOUNT) {
        throw new Error(
            `Insufficient ROOSTER balance.\n` +
            `Required: ${REQUIRED_AMOUNT}\n` +
            `Available: ${balance}`
        );
    }

    console.log(
        'ROOSTER balance: PASS'
    );

    console.log('\n===== 5. REMAINING BALANCE =====');

    const remaining =
        balance - REQUIRED_AMOUNT;

    console.log(
        'Remaining nano balance:',
        remaining.toString()
    );

    console.log(
        'Remaining ROOSTER:',
        Number(remaining) / 1e9
    );

    console.log('\n=================================================');
    console.log(' USER ROOSTER BALANCE VERIFIED');
    console.log('=================================================');

    console.log(
        'NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.'
    );
}

    console.log('\nTestnet bounceable:');

    console.log('\nTestnet non-bounceable:');

    console.log('\n===== TONKEEPER / BLUEPRINT =====');

    console.log('\n===== TESTNET COMPARISON =====');

    if (CONNECTED_WALLET_RAW === nonBounceableTestnet) {

            'PASS | Tonkeeper address matches BOC USER on TESTNET'
        );

    } else {
        console.log(
            'FAIL | Tonkeeper address does NOT match BOC USER'
        );

        console.log('\nExpected testnet non-bounceable:');
        console.log(nonBounceableTestnet);

        console.log('\nActual Tonkeeper / Blueprint:');
        console.log(CONNECTED_WALLET_RAW);

        throw new Error(
            'STOP: TESTNET WALLET IDENTITY MISMATCH.'
        );
    }

    console.log('\n===== FINAL =====');
    console.log('PASS | WALLET IDENTITY VERIFIED');
    console.log('PASS | BOC USER = CONNECTED TESTNET ACCOUNT');
    console.log('NO RPC USED.');
    console.log('NO TRANSACTION WAS SENT.');

    console.log('\n' + '='.repeat(70));
}

main();

function display(amount: bigint, decimals: number): string {

    const whole = amount / base;

    if (fraction === 0n) return whole.toString();

}

        maximumFractionDigits: 12,
    });
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: scripts\xrefType2ImportsV39.ts

import fs from "fs";

const WASM =
    "G:\\Rooster Coin\\Rooster_Coin\\emulator-emscripten-extracted.wasm";

const TARGETS = new Map<number, string>([
    [7, "a.h"],
    [20, "a.u"],
    [22, "a.w"],
    [23, "a.x"],
    [39, "a.N"],
    [60, "a.ga"],
]);

function readULEB(buf: Buffer, pos: number) {
    let value = 0;
    let shift = 0;
    let p = pos;

    while (true) {
        const b = buf[p++];

        value |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;

        if ((b & 0x80) === 0) {
            return { value, next: p };
        }

        shift += 7;
    }
}

function skipImmediate(buf: Buffer, pos: number, opcode: number): number {
    /*
     * We only need reliable scanning for call (0x10).
     * Other opcodes are skipped conservatively where possible.
     *
     * For this pass we search raw bytes for:
     *
     *   0x10 <ULEB function index>
     *
     * and then validate that the target function index is one
     * of the six imported functions.
     *
     * To avoid false positives, we restrict interpretation to
     * decoded code bodies below.
     */

    return pos;
}

const buf = fs.readFileSync(WASM);

console.log("============================================================");
console.log(" TYPE #2 IMPORT CROSS-REFERENCE v39");
console.log(" READ ONLY - OFFLINE");
console.log("============================================================");
console.log();

console.log(`WASM size: ${buf.length.toLocaleString()}`);
console.log();

let pos = 8;

let codePayload = -1;
let codeSize = 0;

while (pos < buf.length) {
    const sectionId = buf[pos++];

    const sizeInfo = readULEB(buf, pos);
    const size = sizeInfo.value;
    pos = sizeInfo.next;

    const payloadStart = pos;
    const payloadEnd = payloadStart + size;

    if (sectionId === 10) {
        codePayload = payloadStart;
        codeSize = size;
        break;
    }

    pos = payloadEnd;
}

if (codePayload < 0) {
    throw new Error("Code section not found");
}

console.log(
    `Code section: offset=0x${codePayload.toString(16)} size=${codeSize}`
);
console.log();

let p = codePayload;

const bodyCountInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
const bodyCount = bodyCountInfo.value;
p = bodyCountInfo.next;

console.log(`Defined function bodies: ${bodyCount}`);
console.log();

const hits = new Map<number, {
    count: number;
    locations: string[];
}>();

for (const index of TARGETS.keys()) {
    hits.set(index, {
        count: 0,
        locations: [],
    });
}

/*
 * IMPORTANT:
 *
 * Function index = imported functions first + defined functions.
 * There are 83 imports.
 *
 * Body #0 therefore belongs to function #83.
 */

for (let bodyIndex = 0; bodyIndex < bodyCount; bodyIndex++) {

    const bodySizeInfo = readULEB(buf, p);
    const bodySize = bodySizeInfo.value;
    p = bodySizeInfo.next;

    const bodyStart = p;
    const bodyEnd = bodyStart + bodySize;

    if (bodyEnd > buf.length) {
        throw new Error(
            `Invalid body ${bodyIndex}: end=0x${bodyEnd.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    const functionIndex = 83 + bodyIndex;

    /*
     * Skip local declarations.
     *
     * bodyStart:
     *   local decl vector
     *   each decl = count ULEB + valtype byte
     */

    let q = bodyStart;

    const localGroupInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
    const localGroups = localGroupInfo.value;
    q = localGroupInfo.next;

    for (let g = 0; g < localGroups; g++) {
        const countInfo = readULEB(buf, q);
        q = countInfo.next;

        q += 1; // value type
    }

    /*
     * Scan instruction bytes for CALL.
     */

    for (let i = q; i < bodyEnd; i++) {

        if (buf[i] !== 0x10) {
            continue;
        }

        const targetInfo = readULEB(buf, i + 1);
        const target = targetInfo.value;

        if (!TARGETS.has(target)) {
            continue;
        }

        const entry = hits.get(target)!;

        entry.count++;

        entry.locations.push(
            `callerFunc=${functionIndex}` +
            ` body=${bodyIndex}` +
            ` callOffset=0x${i.toString(16)}`
        );
    }

    p = bodyEnd;
}

for (const [index, name] of TARGETS) {

    const entry = hits.get(index)!;

    console.log("------------------------------------------------------------");
    console.log(
        `IMPORT #${index} ${name}`
    );
    console.log(
        `CALL COUNT: ${entry.count}`
    );

    for (const loc of entry.locations) {
        console.log(`  ${loc}`);
    }
}

console.log("============================================================");
console.log("DONE");
console.log("============================================================");
